# codeloom v0.1 — Plan 2a: Linker + Evaluation

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** After `codeloom index`, every workspace has `.codeloom/chains.json` — the linked FE → BE → DB/queue
chains (page → HTTP call → route → handler → service → store/queue, plus consumer chains) with a
confidence on every hop — and an empty-or-kept `.codeloom/journeys.json`. `codeloom check` reports how
complete the map is (text or `--json`, `--fail-under N`), and a CI test scores the linker against
hand-written answer keys (demo-shop + 2–3 vendored open-source apps) for precision and recall.

**Architecture:** Plan 1's adapters gain the facts the linker needs (endpoints.json **v2**: HTTP call
kind/base + HTTP clients, route router receiver + mount prefixes, queue exchange/routing key + bindings).
A new `linker/` module builds its own per-file code index over the TypeScript AST (symbols + typed call
sites, cached in the git-ignored fingerprints cache), resolves calls across files (imports, barrels,
`new X()`, `this.svc` DI), matches HTTP calls to routes with a typed path-segment matcher and composed
mount prefixes, and walks handler → service → DB/queue. `linker/chains.ts` turns the result into
stable-id chains and keeps agent-owned journeys linked by chain ids. `check/` turns chains into a
report; `evals/score.ts` turns chains + an answer key into precision/recall. graph.json and the
vendored core are unchanged. No MCP, no network (Plan 2b).

**Tech Stack:** Node ≥ 20, TypeScript 5 (compiler API, already a runtime dependency), zod 4, commander 12,
Vitest, `node:crypto` (sha256 ids). No new runtime dependencies.

**Spec / memo:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-30-codeloom-design.md` (§3 journeys, §4 data files,
§7 testing, §7a check output); design memo `.superpowers/sdd/plan2-design-memo.md` (§2 contracts (a)–(j),
§3 file structure, §4 task list "2a", §5 author decisions — all binding). Built on Plan 1
(`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-30-codeloom-v0.1-plan-1-foundation-indexer.md`) at main `201b92f`.

## Global Constraints

All Plan 1 constraints still hold:

- Package name `codeloom`; CLI binary `codeloom`; data folder `.codeloom/`; workspace file `codeloom.workspace.json`.
- Node `>=20`; ESM only (`"type": "module"`); relative imports end in `.js`.
- No AI calls, no network calls, no telemetry anywhere in the package. **Plan 2a adds none** (the single
  network exception — Tempo — belongs to Plan 2b).
- Every JSON file written by codeloom has a `"version"` field, is validated with zod on read, and is
  written atomically (temp file + rename) via `store/json.ts`.
- A bad file is reported with a clear message (`CodeloomError`), never silently overwritten.
- Understand Anything is MIT: keep `NOTICE`. Do not modify `packages/core` in this plan.
- Fixtures and tests contain no Shopfront or Acme code; use `demo-shop` and the vendored eval apps.
- `.codeloom/fingerprints.json` is a git-ignored cache; every other `.codeloom/` file is committed.
- Adapters parse JS/TS with the TypeScript compiler API; the linker does the same.
- Ask the author before any `git push`, `npm publish`, or creating a public repo. Commit locally after each task.

New for Plan 2a:

- **endpoints.json is `version: 2`.** `EndpointsFileSchema` accepts only `2`. Any reader that finds a
  `version: 1` endpoints.json throws `dataError(...)` (a `CodeloomError` with `exitCode: 2`):
  `<path> was written by an older codeloom (endpoints version 1). Run "codeloom index" to rebuild it.`
  (`readEndpoints` checks the raw `version` before the zod parse so this message wins over the generic
  shape error.) `codeloom index` itself never reads the old committed file, so it simply rewrites it.
- **`CACHE_FORMAT = 4`** in `indexer/index-repo.ts` (cache records now carry the linker's per-file code index
  and v2 endpoints parts); old caches are discarded and rebuilt.
- **File ownership.** `.codeloom/chains.json` is written **only by the linker** (rebuilt on every
  `codeloom index`). `.codeloom/journeys.json` is written **only by an agent/user** — except that the
  indexer (a) creates it as `{ "version": 1, "journeys": [] }` when missing and (b) flips a journey's
  `status` between `"ok"` and `"orphaned"` when its chain ids disappear/reappear; it never adds, deletes,
  renames or reorders journeys or edits any other field.
- In a workspace (folder of repos) chains.json and journeys.json live in the workspace root's `.codeloom/`;
  in a single repo, in that repo's `.codeloom/`. Per-repo graph.json / endpoints.json stay where Plan 1 puts them.
- **Determinism.** Every committed file is written with `writeJsonIfChanged`; every array that lands in a
  committed file is sorted with `util.compare` (code-point order, never `localeCompare`) on a documented
  key; ids are content hashes, never counters or timestamps. Re-running `codeloom index` with no code
  change leaves every committed file byte-identical.
- **`codeloom check` exit codes:** `0` = report printed (whatever it says); `1` = `--fail-under N` given and
  complete% < N; `2` = data or usage error (missing/old/invalid `.codeloom` files, bad flag value). Exit 2
  is carried by `CodeloomError` with `exitCode: 2` (`dataError`, Task 5; `readJson` also throws with exit 2);
  `bin.ts` exits with `err instanceof CodeloomError ? err.exitCode : 1`.
- Confidence vocabulary everywhere: `"exact" | "param" | "guess"`. Precision/recall counts `exact` + `param`
  hops; `guess` hops are reported separately and never counted as found.
- The linker never throws on odd code: anything it cannot resolve becomes an `unresolved` entry on the chain
  (with a reason), which `codeloom check` reports.

## Tasks (overview)

Order is binding: each task only consumes exports of earlier tasks (see the Interfaces Registry).

### Task 1: Endpoints v2 schemas, workspace optional fields, CACHE_FORMAT 4
Bump `EndpointsFileSchema` to `version: 2` with the new optional fields and arrays (HttpCall `kind`/`base`,
`httpClients`, Route `router`, `mounts`, QueueOp `exchange`/`routingKey`, op `"bind"`); add workspace
`repos[].role`, `repos[].apiBase`, `traces`; add `FILES.chains`/`FILES.journeys`; `CACHE_FORMAT = 4`;
`finalizeEndpoints` emits `version: 2` with empty new arrays so everything stays green.
- Files: `store/schemas.ts`, `store/paths.ts`, `adapters/types.ts`, `adapters/run.ts`, `indexer/index-repo.ts`,
  `adapters/http-calls.ts` (interim `kind` so it type-checks; replaced in Task 2)
- Tests: `test/schemas-v2.test.ts` (+ update `run-adapters.test.ts` expectations)

### Task 2: HTTP adapter — call kind, base, HTTP clients
Each HttpCall records `kind` (`literal` / `template` / `unknown`) and the stripped base expression text
(`base`); `path` no longer falls back to raw text for `unknown`. New `detectHttpClients` records
`axios.create` / `ky.create` / `*.create({ baseURL })` instances with their base expression or literal.
- Files: `adapters/ast.ts`, `adapters/http-calls.ts`, `adapters/run.ts`
- Tests: `test/http-calls.test.ts`, `test/ast.test.ts`

### Task 3: Routes adapter — router receiver and mounts
Every Express/Fastify route records `router` (receiver identifier: `router`, `app`, `fastify`…).
New `detectMounts`: `app.use("/p", router)`, `app.use("/p", importedRouter)`, `fastify.register(plugin, { prefix })`,
Nest `app.setGlobalPrefix("api")`. Mount targets that are imports are resolved to a file in `finalizeEndpoints`.
- Files: `adapters/routes.ts`, `adapters/run.ts` (`MountDraft` already exists from Task 1)
- Tests: `test/routes.test.ts`

### Task 4: Queue adapter — exchange, routing key, bindQueue
`publish(ex, key)` records `exchange` + `routingKey` (queue string unchanged for compatibility);
`bindQueue(q, ex, key)` → `{ op: "bind", queue, exchange, routingKey }`; `assertExchange` is ignored.
- Files: `adapters/queues.ts`
- Tests: `test/queues.test.ts`

### Task 5: Store readers and exit-code-carrying errors
`store/read.ts`: `readGraph` (core `KnowledgeGraphSchema`, never `validateGraph`), `readEndpoints` (v1 →
"re-run codeloom index" error), `loadWorkspaceData` (all repos' graph + endpoints + workspace hints).
`CodeloomError` gains `exitCode`; `bin.ts` honours it.
- Files: `store/read.ts`, `errors.ts`, `bin.ts`, `store/json.ts`, `workspace/repos.ts`
- Tests: `test/store-read.test.ts`, `test/errors.test.ts`

### Task 6: Code index — symbols and typed call sites (cached)
Per-file `FileCodeIndex` over the TS AST: symbols named exactly like `enclosingSymbol` (`fn`, `Class.method`,
`obj.method`) with line ranges, imports/re-exports, class fields/constructor parameter properties with a known
class type, and every call site with a typed callee shape. Stored in the fingerprints cache per file.
- Files: `linker/code-index.ts`, `indexer/index-repo.ts`, `store/schemas.ts` (FingerprintsSchema `codeIndex`)
- Tests: `test/code-index.test.ts`, `test/index-repo.test.ts`

### Task 7: Call resolver
Resolve a call site to `{ repo, file, symbol }`: local / named / default / renamed / namespace imports,
`require`, barrels (`export *`, `export {x} from`, ≤3 hops), `new X().m()`, `const s = new X(); s.m()`,
`this.m()`, `this.svc.m()` (initializer or DI parameter property), object-literal services (`svc.m()` where
`svc` is an imported object literal), unique-method `guess`. `walkCalls` = depth 6 with cycle guard. `CallResolver.fileIndex(file)` exposes the
per-file index for Tasks 10–11.
- Files: `linker/resolve-calls.ts`
- Tests: `test/resolve-calls.test.ts`

### Task 8: Path segment model and matcher
Parse paths into literal / param / splat segments (`:x`, `:x*`, `*`, `(.*)`, `[...x]` already `:x*`), match a
call path to a route path with confidence (memo (e)), method `ANY`, specificity ranking, tie → `guess`.
- Files: `linker/paths.ts`
- Tests: `test/paths.test.ts`

### Task 9: Mount resolution and effective paths
Compose route-level, router-level and app-level prefixes (multi-level `use` chains, fastify `register`,
nest global prefix, Adonis group prefix already in `path`) into each route's `effectivePath`; record
whether every prefix was known.
- Files: `linker/mounts.ts`
- Tests: `test/mounts.test.ts`

### Task 10: Link UI — call → page, call → route across backends
For each HttpCall: find the UI pages that reach its enclosing symbol by a reverse walk over resolved calls
(page component → hook/helper → call site, ≤6 hops), and the target route among all backends with routes
(strip known base / client baseURL, match `effectivePath`; several backends → `repos[].role`/`apiBase` hints;
else `guess`). Calls whose base is an absolute foreign host are skipped as external.
- Files: `linker/link.ts` (UI half)
- Tests: `test/link-ui.test.ts`

### Task 11: Link backend — route → handler → store/queue; consumers; manual links
Route → handler symbol (by `handlerFile` + `handler`, or inline by `handlerLines` containing the site line) →
`walkCalls` → DbAccess/QueueOp whose `at.symbol` is visited. Queue publishes pair with consumers (memo (f));
each consumer starts its own chain. Workspace `links` (`from`/`to` strings) add manual hops.
- Files: `linker/link.ts` (backend half)
- Tests: `test/link-backend.test.ts`

### Task 12: chains.json + journeys.json; run the linker in `codeloom index`
Stable chain ids, default names, stops/hops/unresolved; deterministic sort; write `chains.json` via
`writeJsonIfChanged`; create `journeys.json` if missing; flip journey status ok ↔ orphaned. `runIndex` calls
`runLinker` after all repos are indexed and prints a chain summary line. Demo-shop fixture gains the new hop
shapes (see "Fixture changes") and a snapshot test of its chains.
- Files: `store/schemas.ts`, `linker/chains.ts`, `linker/run.ts`, `commands/index-cmd.ts`, `fixtures/demo-shop/**`
- Tests: `test/chains.test.ts`, `test/linker-demo-shop.test.ts`, `test/index-cmd.test.ts`

### Task 13: `codeloom check`
Build the check report from chains + endpoints; text (§7a summary line, items grouped by kind) or `--json`;
`--fail-under <n>` (0–100) → exit 1 when complete% < n; missing/old/invalid data or bad flag → exit 2.
- Files: `store/schemas.ts`, `check/report.ts`, `check/format.ts`, `commands/check.ts`, `cli.ts`
- Tests: `test/check.test.ts`, `test/cli.test.ts`

### Task 14: Answer-key format, scorer, demo-shop key
`ExpectedJourneysSchema`; `evals/score.ts` turns an answer key and chains into per-hop-type precision/recall
(exact + param count; guess reported separately); `fixtures/demo-shop/expected-journeys.json`; the demo-shop
score must be 1.0 / 1.0.
- Files: `store/schemas.ts`, `evals/score.ts`, `fixtures/demo-shop/expected-journeys.json`
- Tests: `test/score.test.ts`

### Task 15: Vendored eval apps + CI threshold gate
Vendor 2–3 apps as trimmed source under `fixtures/evals/<app>/` (with `LICENSE`, `SOURCE.md` = url, pinned sha,
licence) — RealWorld React+Express pair, Nest RealWorld, Prisma `rest-nextjs-api-routes` (confirm licences when
vendoring; drop any whose licence does not allow it); write each answer key; `evals.test.ts` indexes each and
asserts precision ≥ 0.95 and recall ≥ 0.8 per hop type (spec §7a) (thresholds in one exported constant).
- Files: `evals/score.ts` (`EVAL_THRESHOLDS`), `test/helpers.ts` (`materializeEval`), `fixtures/evals/**`
- Tests: `test/evals.test.ts`, `test/helpers.test.ts`

## Tasks (detailed)

### Task 1: Endpoints v2 schemas, workspace optional fields, CACHE_FORMAT 4

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/store/schemas.ts`
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/store/paths.ts`
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/adapters/types.ts`
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/adapters/run.ts`
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/indexer/index-repo.ts`
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/adapters/http-calls.ts` (interim compatibility only — see "Registry
  inconsistency" note below; Task 2 replaces this code with the real `kind`/`base` detection)
- Create: `packages/cli/test/schemas-v2.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/cli/test/run-adapters.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Plan 1 schemas kept as is — `HttpMethodSchema`, `LocSchema`, `UiPageSchema`, `DbOpSchema`,
  `DbAccessSchema`, `TableDefSchema`, `ModelDefSchema`, `RepoConfigSchema` (all unchanged, from the current
  `packages/cli/src/store/schemas.ts`).
- Produces (registry R1): `ConfidenceSchema`/`Confidence`, `HttpCallKindSchema`/`HttpCallKind`,
  `HttpCallSchema`/`HttpCall` (now with `kind`, `base?`), `HttpClientSchema`/`HttpClient`,
  `RouteSchema`/`Route` (now with `router?`), `MountKindSchema`/`MountKind`, `MountSchema`/`Mount`,
  `QueueOpSchema`/`QueueOp` (now with `exchange?`, `routingKey?`, op `"bind"`),
  `EndpointsFileSchema`/`EndpointsFile` (`version: 2`, `httpClients`, `mounts`), `RepoRoleSchema`/`RepoRole`,
  `RepoRefSchema`/`RepoRef` (now with `role?`, `apiBase?`), `TracesConfigSchema`/`TracesConfig`,
  `WorkspaceFileSchema`/`WorkspaceFile` (now with `traces?`). `FILES.chains`, `FILES.journeys`
  (`store/paths.ts`). `MountDraft`, widened `EndpointsPart` (`httpClients`, `mounts`), widened `emptyPart()`
  (`adapters/types.ts`). `finalizeEndpoints` emitting `version: 2` (`adapters/run.ts`).
  `CACHE_FORMAT = 4` (`indexer/index-repo.ts`).

**Registry inconsistency found and fixed:**

R1's `HttpCallSchema` makes `kind: HttpCallKindSchema` a *required* field (no `.optional()`), and R1's
`EndpointsPart.httpCalls` is typed `HttpCall[]` (the final type, not a draft). But Task 2 — not this task —
is the one that teaches `adapters/http-calls.ts#detectHttpCalls` to compute `kind`/`base`
(skeleton: "Task 2 ... Each HttpCall records `kind` ... and the stripped base expression text"). Task 1's
file list does not include `adapters/http-calls.ts`. If Task 1 only changed `schemas.ts`, the existing
`detectHttpCalls` would keep pushing `{ method, path, raw, resolved, client, at }` object literals (no
`kind`) into an array typed `HttpCall[]`, which no longer type-checks once `kind` is required — breaking
`pnpm --filter codeloom typecheck` immediately after Task 1, before Task 2 ever runs.

**Fix applied:** Task 1 also makes one minimal, additive edit to `detectHttpCalls` in
`adapters/http-calls.ts`: it adds `kind: pattern ? "literal" : "unknown"` to the pushed object, using the
`pattern` value the function already computes (no new logic, no behaviour change for any existing test —
none of them assert on `kind`). This keeps the exact field name and enum from the registry, satisfies the
type, and is forward-compatible: Task 2 fully rewrites this function (adding the real `template` case and
`base` extraction) and this interim line is replaced wholesale, not built upon. No other Task 1 file needed
a matching patch: `Route.router`, `QueueOp.exchange`/`routingKey` are `.optional()` in the registry, and
`EndpointsPart.httpClients`/`mounts` are brand-new arrays that start empty via `emptyPart()` (nothing
existing pushes into them yet).

```ts
    },
  ],
};

describe("EndpointsFileSchema v2", () => {
  it("parses a full v2 endpoints file (customer + invoice vocabulary)", () => {
    expect(EndpointsFileSchema.parse(customerEndpointsV2).version).toBe(2);
  });

  it("rejects a v1 file (version literal no longer matches)", () => {
    const v1 = { ...customerEndpointsV2, version: 1 };
    expect(EndpointsFileSchema.safeParse(v1).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("requires httpCalls[].kind and rejects a call missing it", () => {
    const bad = {
      method: "GET", path: "/api/customers", raw: "/api/customers", resolved: true, client: "api",
      at: { file: "lib/get-customer.ts", line: 1, symbol: "getCustomer" },
    };
    expect(HttpCallSchema.safeParse(bad).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("accepts an httpCall with kind 'unknown' and no base", () => {
    const call = {
      method: "GET", path: "", raw: "url", resolved: false, client: "api",
      at: { file: "lib/get-customer.ts", line: 1, symbol: "getCustomer" }, kind: "unknown",
    };
    expect(HttpCallSchema.parse(call).path).toBe("");
  });

  it("accepts a route with no router (unaffected frameworks) and one with router set", () => {
    const nextRoute = {
      method: "GET", path: "/api/customers", handler: "GET", inline: false, framework: "next-api",
      at: { file: "app/api/customers/route.ts", line: 1, symbol: "GET" },
    };
    expect(RouteSchema.parse(nextRoute).router).toBeUndefined();
    const expressRoute = { ...nextRoute, framework: "express" as const, router: "router" };
    expect(RouteSchema.parse(expressRoute).router).toBe("router");
  });

  it("accepts a nest-global mount with no target name/file", () => {
    const mount = {
      prefix: "/api", kind: "nest-global", on: "",
      target: {}, at: { file: "src/main.ts", line: 4, symbol: "<module>" },
    };
    expect(MountSchema.parse(mount).target).toEqual({});
  });

  it("accepts a bind queue op and a plain publish without exchange/routingKey", () => {
    const bind = {
      queue: "emails", op: "bind", exchange: "billing", routingKey: "invoice.*",
      at: { file: "src/notify-consumer.ts", line: 3, symbol: "startNotifier" },
    };
    expect(QueueOpSchema.parse(bind).op).toBe("bind");
    const publish = {
      queue: "invoice.created", op: "publish", at: { file: "src/invoices.ts", line: 12, symbol: "createInvoice" },
    };
    expect(QueueOpSchema.parse(publish).exchange).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("workspace optional fields", () => {
  it("accepts a RepoRef with role and apiBase hints", () => {
    const ref = { id: "api", path: "api", role: "api", apiBase: ["NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL", "api.example.com"] };
    expect(RepoRefSchema.parse(ref).apiBase).toEqual(["NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL", "api.example.com"]);
  });

  it("accepts a RepoRef with neither role nor apiBase (both optional)", () => {
    expect(RepoRefSchema.parse({ id: "api", path: "api" }).role).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("validates a tempo traces config and an otlp-file traces config", () => {
    const tempo = { kind: "tempo", url: "http://tempo.local:3200", services: { api: "customers-api" } };
    expect(TracesConfigSchema.parse(tempo).kind).toBe("tempo");
    const file = { kind: "otlp-file", path: ".codeloom/traces.json" };
    expect(TracesConfigSchema.parse(file).kind).toBe("otlp-file");
  });

  it("rejects a traces config with an unknown kind", () => {
    expect(TracesConfigSchema.safeParse({ kind: "jaeger", url: "x" }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("keeps workspace version 1 and traces optional", () => {
    const ws = { version: 1, name: "demo-shop", repos: [{ id: "api", path: "api" }], links: [] };
    expect(WorkspaceFileSchema.parse(ws).traces).toBeUndefined();
    const withTraces = { ...ws, traces: { kind: "otlp-file", path: ".codeloom/traces.json" } };
    expect(WorkspaceFileSchema.parse(withTraces).traces?.kind).toBe("otlp-file");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { z } from "zod";

export const HttpMethodSchema = z.enum(["GET", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE", "ANY"]);
export type HttpMethod = z.infer<typeof HttpMethodSchema>;

/** Where something is in the code: repo-relative file, 1-based line, enclosing function or Class.method. */
export const LocSchema = z.object({
  file: z.string(),
  line: z.number().int().positive(),
  symbol: z.string(),
});
export type Loc = z.infer<typeof LocSchema>;

export const UiPageSchema = z.object({
  route: z.string(),
  file: z.string(),
  line: z.number().int().positive(),
  framework: z.enum(["next-app", "next-pages", "react-router"]),
});
export type UiPage = z.infer<typeof UiPageSchema>;

export const ConfidenceSchema = z.enum(["exact", "param", "guess"]);
export type Confidence = z.infer<typeof ConfidenceSchema>;

export const HttpCallKindSchema = z.enum(["literal", "template", "unknown"]);
export type HttpCallKind = z.infer<typeof HttpCallKindSchema>;

export const HttpCallSchema = z.object({
  method: HttpMethodSchema,
  /** Normalised route pattern; "" when kind === "unknown" (nothing literal in the URL). */
  path: z.string(),
  raw: z.string(),
  resolved: z.boolean(),
  client: z.string(),
  at: LocSchema,
  /** literal: whole URL is a string; template: literal text + expressions; unknown: no literal text. */
  kind: HttpCallKindSchema,
  /** Source text of a leading base expression that was stripped, e.g. "process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL". */
  base: z.string().optional(),
});
export type HttpCall = z.infer<typeof HttpCallSchema>;

export const HttpClientSchema = z.object({
  /** Variable the instance is bound to (`export const api = axios.create(...)` -> "api"). */
  name: z.string(),
  file: z.string(),
  line: z.number().int().positive(),
  /** "axios" | "ky" | callee text of a generic `create({ baseURL })`. */
  factory: z.string(),
  /** Source text of a non-literal baseURL / prefixUrl expression. */
  baseExpr: z.string().optional(),
  /** baseURL when it is a string literal, normalised by normalizePath (host stripped). */
  baseLiteral: z.string().optional(),
});
export type HttpClient = z.infer<typeof HttpClientSchema>;

export const RouteSchema = z.object({
  method: HttpMethodSchema,
  /** As declared, including Adonis group prefixes (Plan 1 behaviour); mounts are NOT applied here. */
  path: z.string(),
  handler: z.string(),
  inline: z.boolean(),
  framework: z.enum(["adonis", "express", "fastify", "nest", "next-api"]),
  at: LocSchema,
  handlerFile: z.string().optional(),
  handlerLines: z.tuple([z.number().int().positive(), z.number().int().positive()]).optional(),
  /** Receiver identifier the route was registered on (express/fastify): "router", "app", "fastify", ... */
  router: z.string().optional(),
});
export type Route = z.infer<typeof RouteSchema>;

export const MountKindSchema = z.enum(["express-use", "fastify-register", "nest-global"]);
export type MountKind = z.infer<typeof MountKindSchema>;

export const MountSchema = z.object({
  /** Normalised prefix ("/billing"); nest-global "api" -> "/api". */
  prefix: z.string(),
  kind: MountKindSchema,
  /** Receiver the mount was called on ("app", "router", "fastify"); "" for nest-global. */
  on: z.string(),
  /** What gets the prefix. name = local identifier passed (router var / plugin fn); file = repo-relative
   * file it resolves to when imported (set by finalizeEndpoints from the draft's import specifier).
   * Both absent for nest-global (applies to every nest route in the repo). */
  target: z.object({ name: z.string().optional(), file: z.string().optional() }),
  at: LocSchema,
});
export type Mount = z.infer<typeof MountSchema>;

export const DbOpSchema = z.enum(["read", "insert", "update", "delete"]);
export type DbOp = z.infer<typeof DbOpSchema>;

export const DbAccessSchema = z.object({
  table: z.string().optional(),
  model: z.string().optional(),
  op: DbOpSchema,
  via: z.enum(["lucid", "prisma", "typeorm", "drizzle", "query-builder", "sql"]),
  at: LocSchema,
});
export type DbAccess = z.infer<typeof DbAccessSchema>;

export const TableDefSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string(),
  file: z.string(),
  line: z.number().int().positive(),
  source: z.enum(["migration", "prisma", "drizzle", "model"]),
});
export type TableDef = z.infer<typeof TableDefSchema>;

export const ModelDefSchema = z.object({
  model: z.string(),
  table: z.string(),
  file: z.string(),
  source: z.enum(["lucid", "typeorm", "prisma", "drizzle"]),
});
export type ModelDef = z.infer<typeof ModelDefSchema>;

export const QueueOpSchema = z.object({
  /** publish via sendToQueue: queue name; publish via exchange: "exchange:key" (or exchange alone when key
   * is ""); consume / bind: queue name. (Plan 1 encoding kept.) */
  queue: z.string(),
  op: z.enum(["publish", "consume", "bind"]),
  at: LocSchema,
  exchange: z.string().optional(),
  /** Present (possibly "") for exchange publishes and binds. */
  routingKey: z.string().optional(),
});
export type QueueOp = z.infer<typeof QueueOpSchema>;

export const EndpointsFileSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(2),
  repo: z.string(),
  pages: z.array(UiPageSchema),
  httpCalls: z.array(HttpCallSchema),
  httpClients: z.array(HttpClientSchema),
  routes: z.array(RouteSchema),
  mounts: z.array(MountSchema),
  dbAccess: z.array(DbAccessSchema),
  tables: z.array(TableDefSchema),
  models: z.array(ModelDefSchema),
  queues: z.array(QueueOpSchema),
});
export type EndpointsFile = z.infer<typeof EndpointsFileSchema>;

export const RepoConfigSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(1),
  autoUpdate: z.boolean(),
  ignore: z.array(z.string()),
});
export type RepoConfig = z.infer<typeof RepoConfigSchema>;

export function defaultRepoConfig(): RepoConfig {
  return { version: 1, autoUpdate: true, ignore: [] };
}

export const RepoRoleSchema = z.enum(["ui", "api", "worker", "lib"]);
export type RepoRole = z.infer<typeof RepoRoleSchema>;

export const RepoRefSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  path: z.string(),
  /** Optional hint for picking the target backend of an HTTP call. */
  role: RepoRoleSchema.optional(),
  /** Env var names (e.g. "NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL") or hosts ("api.example.com") whose calls go to this repo. */
  apiBase: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
});
export type RepoRef = z.infer<typeof RepoRefSchema>;

/** Stored now, used by Plan 2b. Validated so a typo is caught early. */
export const TracesConfigSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("kind", [
  z.object({
    kind: z.literal("tempo"),
    url: z.string(),
    headersEnv: z.string().optional(),
    services: z.record(z.string(), z.string()).optional(),
    lookback: z.string().optional(),
  }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("otlp-file"), path: z.string() }),
]);
export type TracesConfig = z.infer<typeof TracesConfigSchema>;

export const WorkspaceFileSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(1),
  name: z.string(),
  repos: z.array(RepoRefSchema),
  /** Manual hops. Strings use the chain-stop address form "<repo>:<file>#<symbol>" or
   * "route:<repo>:<METHOD path>" / "queue:<repo>:<queue>" (see R11 parseLinkRef, Task 11). */
  links: z.array(z.object({ from: z.string(), to: z.string() })),
  traces: TracesConfigSchema.optional(),
});
export type WorkspaceFile = z.infer<typeof WorkspaceFileSchema>;

/** Local, git-ignored cache for incremental indexing. structure/calls/endpoints are codeloom's own
 * outputs. It also holds the run's time and commit, which are kept out of the committed files so
 * those depend only on code. */
export const FingerprintsSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(2),
  contextKey: z.string(),
  indexedAt: z.string(),
  gitCommitHash: z.string(),
  files: z.record(
    z.string(),
    z.object({ hash: z.string(), structure: z.any(), calls: z.any(), endpoints: z.any() }),
  ),
});
export type Fingerprints = z.infer<typeof FingerprintsSchema>;
```

Note: `FingerprintsSchema`'s per-file record gains `codeIndex: z.any()` in Task 6 — out of scope here, left
unchanged.

- [ ] **Step 3c: Implementation — `packages/cli/src/adapters/types.ts` (full new file)**

```ts
import type ts from "typescript";
import type {
  DbAccess, HttpCall, HttpClient, ModelDef, Mount, QueueOp, Route, TableDef, UiPage,
} from "../store/schemas.js";

/** A route as an adapter sees it: `handlerImport` is the raw module specifier of an imported
 * handler, resolved to `handlerFile` by the indexer (which knows every file and alias) and then dropped. */
export type RouteDraft = Route & { handlerImport?: string };

/** A mount as an adapter sees it: `targetImport` is the raw module specifier of an imported router/plugin,
 * resolved to `target.file` by `finalizeEndpoints` (which knows every file and alias) and then dropped. */
export type MountDraft = Mount & { targetImport?: string };

export interface ParsedFile {
  rel: string;
  content: string;
  /** Present for JS/TS files. */
  sf?: ts.SourceFile;
}

/** What adapters know about the package a file belongs to. */
export interface AdapterContext {
  /** Dependencies of the file's nearest package.json at or above it, plus the repo root's. */
  deps: Set<string>;
  /** Repo-relative folder of that package.json ("" for the repo root, or when there is none). */
  packageRoot: string;
}

/** Per-file adapter contexts for a whole repo. */
export interface RepoContext {
  contextFor(rel: string): AdapterContext;
  /** Changes whenever any package's dependencies change (part of the cache key). */
  key: string;
}

/** `rel` with its package root removed, so framework folders (app/, pages/) match package-relative. */
export function packageRelative(rel: string, ctx: AdapterContext): string | undefined {
  if (!ctx.packageRoot) return rel;
  return rel.startsWith(`${ctx.packageRoot}/`) ? rel.slice(ctx.packageRoot.length + 1) : undefined;
}

export interface EndpointsPart {
  pages: UiPage[];
  httpCalls: HttpCall[];
  httpClients: HttpClient[];
  routes: RouteDraft[];
  mounts: MountDraft[];
  dbAccess: DbAccess[];
  tables: TableDef[];
  models: ModelDef[];
  queues: QueueOp[];
}

export function emptyPart(): EndpointsPart {
  return {
    pages: [], httpCalls: [], httpClients: [], routes: [], mounts: [], dbAccess: [], tables: [], models: [],
    queues: [],
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3d: Implementation — `packages/cli/src/adapters/http-calls.ts` (interim compatibility edit)**

Only `detectHttpCalls`'s final push changes (one added field, using the `pattern` it already computes);
everything else in the file is untouched. Full new function body:

```ts
export function detectHttpCalls(file: ParsedFile): HttpCall[] {
  if (!file.sf) return [];
  const out: HttpCall[] = [];
  walk(file.sf, (node) => {
    if (!ts.isCallExpression(node)) return;
    const callee = node.expression;
    const [first, second] = node.arguments;
    let method: HttpMethod | undefined;
    let urlExpr: ts.Expression | undefined;
    let client = "";

    if (ts.isIdentifier(callee) && FETCH_FNS.has(callee.text) && first) {
      const init = second && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(second) ? second : undefined;
      method = (init && methodOf(stringValue(prop(init, "method")))) ?? "GET";
      urlExpr = first;
      client = callee.text;
    } else if (ts.isIdentifier(callee) && callee.text === "axios" && first && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(first)) {
      urlExpr = prop(first, "url");
      method = methodOf(stringValue(prop(first, "method"))) ?? "GET";
      client = "axios";
    } else if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) && callee.name.text === "request" && first && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(first)) {
      const receiver = lastName(callee.expression);
      if (isClientReceiver(receiver, node)) {
        urlExpr = prop(first, "url");
        method = methodOf(stringValue(prop(first, "method"))) ?? "GET";
        client = receiver;
      }
    } else if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) && METHODS[callee.name.text] && first) {
      const receiver = lastName(callee.expression);
      if (isClientReceiver(receiver, node) && pathPattern(first) && looksLikeUrl(literalText(first))) {
        method = METHODS[callee.name.text];
        urlExpr = first;
        client = receiver;
      }
    }
    if (!method || !urlExpr) return;
    const pattern = pathPattern(urlExpr);
    out.push({
      method,
      path: pattern?.path ?? urlExpr.getText(),
      raw: urlExpr.getText(),
      resolved: pattern?.resolved ?? false,
      client,
      at: { file: file.rel, line: lineOf(node), symbol: enclosingSymbol(node) },
      // Interim Task 1 value: a real literal/template/unknown distinction and `base` extraction land in
      // Task 2, which rewrites this function and `pathPattern`'s return type together.
      kind: pattern ? "literal" : "unknown",
    });
  });
  return out;
}
```

Every other function in `adapters/http-calls.ts` (`methodOf`, `isClientReceiver`, `literalText`,
`looksLikeUrl`, the module-level `METHODS`/`FETCH_FNS`/`CLIENT_RE` constants) is unchanged from the current
file — only the object literal pushed to `out` gained the `kind` field shown above.

- [ ] **Step 3e: Implementation — `packages/cli/src/adapters/run.ts` (full new file)**

Only `finalizeEndpoints` changes (version 2, new `httpClients`/`mounts` arrays, new sort keys for
`httpClients`/`mounts`/`queues`); `loadContext`, `runAdapters` and `resolveHandler` are unchanged. Full file:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, posix } from "node:path";
import type { EndpointsFile, HttpClient, Mount, QueueOp, Route, TableDef } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";
import { JS_EXT, parseSource } from "./ast.js";
import { detectDb } from "./db.js";
import { detectHttpCalls } from "./http-calls.js";
import { detectPages } from "./pages.js";
import { detectQueues } from "./queues.js";
import { detectRoutes } from "./routes.js";
import { type AdapterContext, type EndpointsPart, emptyPart, type MountDraft, type ParsedFile, type RepoContext, type RouteDraft } from "./types.js";

function readDeps(abs: string): Set<string> {
  const deps = new Set<string>();
  try {
    const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(abs, "utf8")) as Record<string, Record<string, string> | undefined>;
    for (const key of ["dependencies", "devDependencies", "peerDependencies"]) {
      for (const name of Object.keys(pkg[key] ?? {})) deps.add(name);
    }
  } catch {
    // A broken package.json only means fewer adapters apply.
  }
  return deps;
}

/** Each file gets the deps of its nearest package.json at or above it (unioned with the repo
 * root's, for hoisted monorepo deps) and that package's folder; no package.json above -> empty set. */
export function loadContext(repoAbs: string, files: string[]): RepoContext {
  const byDir = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  for (const rel of files) {
    if (rel !== "package.json" && !rel.endsWith("/package.json")) continue;
    byDir.set(posix.dirname(rel) === "." ? "" : posix.dirname(rel), readDeps(join(repoAbs, rel)));
  }
  const rootDeps = byDir.get("") ?? new Set<string>();
  const contexts = new Map<string, AdapterContext>();
  const forDir = (dir: string): AdapterContext => {
    const cached = contexts.get(dir);
    if (cached) return cached;
    let ctx: AdapterContext;
    if (byDir.has(dir)) ctx = { deps: new Set([...rootDeps, ...byDir.get(dir)!]), packageRoot: dir };
    else if (dir === "") ctx = { deps: new Set(), packageRoot: "" };
    else ctx = forDir(posix.dirname(dir) === "." ? "" : posix.dirname(dir));
    contexts.set(dir, ctx);
    return ctx;
  };
  const key = [...byDir.entries()]
    .sort(([a], [b]) => compare(a, b))
    .map(([dir, deps]) => `${dir || "."}:${[...deps].sort().join("|")}`)
    .join(";");
  return {
    key,
    contextFor: (rel) => forDir(posix.dirname(rel) === "." ? "" : posix.dirname(rel)),
  };
}

export function runAdapters(rel: string, content: string, ctx: AdapterContext): EndpointsPart {
  const part = emptyPart();
  const isJs = JS_EXT.test(rel) && !rel.endsWith(".d.ts");
  if (!isJs && !rel.endsWith(".prisma") && !rel.endsWith(".sql")) return part;
  const file: ParsedFile = { rel, content, sf: isJs ? parseSource(rel, content) : undefined };
  part.pages.push(...detectPages(file, ctx));
  part.httpCalls.push(...detectHttpCalls(file));
  part.routes.push(...detectRoutes(file, ctx));
  const db = detectDb(file, ctx);
  part.dbAccess.push(...db.dbAccess);
  part.tables.push(...db.tables);
  part.models.push(...db.models);
  part.queues.push(...detectQueues(file, ctx));
  return part;
}

/** Turn a draft route's handlerImport into handlerFile, and drop the internal field. Never mutates
 * the draft (drafts are cached and must keep their specifier for later runs). */
function resolveHandler(draft: RouteDraft, resolve?: ImportResolver): Route {
  const { handlerImport, ...route } = draft;
  if (handlerImport && !route.handlerFile && resolve) {
    const file = resolve(route.at.file, handlerImport);
    if (file) route.handlerFile = file;
  }
  return route;
}

/** Turn a draft mount's targetImport into target.file, and drop the internal field. Mirrors
 * resolveHandler. Task 1 only wires the plumbing; nothing produces a MountDraft with targetImport
 * set until Task 3 (detectMounts), so this is exercised there. */
function resolveMount(draft: MountDraft, resolve?: ImportResolver): Mount {
  const { targetImport, ...mount } = draft;
  if (targetImport && !mount.target.file && resolve) {
    const file = resolve(mount.at.file, targetImport);
    if (file) mount.target = { ...mount.target, file };
  }
  return mount;
}

const byLoc = <T extends { at: { file: string; line: number } }>(a: T, b: T) =>
  compare(a.at.file, b.at.file) || a.at.line - b.at.line;

const byHttpClient = (a: HttpClient, b: HttpClient) =>
  compare(a.file, b.file) || a.line - b.line || compare(a.name, b.name);

const byMount = (a: Mount, b: Mount) =>
  compare(a.at.file, b.at.file) || a.at.line - b.at.line || compare(a.prefix, b.prefix);

const byQueue = (a: QueueOp, b: QueueOp) =>
  byLoc(a, b) || compare(a.op, b.op) || compare(a.exchange ?? "", b.exchange ?? "") || compare(a.routingKey ?? "", b.routingKey ?? "");

/** Resolves a module specifier written in `fromRel` to a repo file (null when it is not in the repo). */
export type ImportResolver = (fromRel: string, specifier: string) => string | null;

export function finalizeEndpoints(repo: string, parts: EndpointsPart[], resolveImport?: ImportResolver): EndpointsFile {
  const all = emptyPart();
  for (const p of parts) {
    for (const key of Object.keys(all) as Array<keyof EndpointsPart>) {
      (all[key] as unknown[]).push(...(p[key] as unknown[]));
    }
  }
  // First-wins collision rule: only set a key if not already present (matches tables dedup behavior)
  const exact = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const m of all.models) if (!exact.has(m.model)) exact.set(m.model, m.table);
  const lower = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const m of all.models) {
    const lower_key = m.model.toLowerCase();
    if (!lower.has(lower_key)) lower.set(lower_key, m.table);
  }
  const dbAccess = all.dbAccess.map((a) => {
    if (a.table || !a.model) return a;
    const table = exact.get(a.model) ?? lower.get(a.model.toLowerCase());
    return table ? { ...a, table } : a;
  });
  const tables = new Map<string, TableDef>();
  for (const t of all.tables) if (!tables.has(t.name)) tables.set(t.name, t);
  for (const m of all.models) {
    if (!tables.has(m.table)) tables.set(m.table, { name: m.table, file: m.file, line: 1, source: "model" });
  }
  return {
    version: 2,
    repo,
    pages: all.pages.sort((a, b) => compare(a.route, b.route) || compare(a.file, b.file)),
    httpCalls: all.httpCalls.sort(byLoc),
    httpClients: all.httpClients.sort(byHttpClient),
    routes: all.routes.map((r) => resolveHandler(r, resolveImport)).sort(byLoc),
    mounts: all.mounts.map((m) => resolveMount(m, resolveImport)).sort(byMount),
    dbAccess: dbAccess.sort(byLoc),
    tables: [...tables.values()].sort((a, b) => compare(a.name, b.name)),
    models: all.models.sort((a, b) => compare(a.model, b.model)),
    queues: all.queues.sort(byQueue),
  };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { detectHttpCalls, detectHttpClients } from "../src/adapters/http-calls.js";
import { parsed } from "./adapter-helpers.js";

const calls = (code: string) =>
  detectHttpCalls(parsed("src/lib.ts", code)).map(
    (c) => `${c.method} [${c.path}] ${c.resolved ? "ok" : "?"} ${c.kind}${c.base ? ` base=${c.base}` : ""} @${c.at.symbol}`,
  );

describe("detectHttpCalls", () => {
  it("finds fetch with and without a method", () => {
    expect(calls(`
      export async function getCustomer(id: string) { return fetch(\`\${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL}/api/customers/\${id}\`); }
      export async function saveCustomer(b: unknown) { return fetch("/api/customers", { method: "post", body: JSON.stringify(b) }); }
    `)).toEqual([
      "GET [/api/customers/:id] ok template base=process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL @getCustomer",
      "POST [/api/customers] ok literal @saveCustomer",
    ]);
  });

  it("finds axios, client instances and this.http", () => {
    expect(calls(`
      import axios from "axios";
      export const api = axios.create({ baseURL: "/x" });
      export function useCreateCustomer() { return async (input: unknown) => api.post("/api/customers", input); }
      export const remove = (id: string) => axios.delete(\`/api/customers/\${id}\`);
      export const raw = () => axios({ url: "/api/ping", method: "PUT" });
      class CustomersClient { constructor(private http: any) {} list() { return this.http.get("/api/customers"); } }
    `)).toEqual([
      "POST [/api/customers] ok literal @useCreateCustomer",
      "DELETE [/api/customers/:id] ok template @remove",
      "PUT [/api/ping] ok literal @raw",
      "GET [/api/customers] ok literal @CustomersClient.list",
    ]);
  });

  it("keeps unresolved URLs honestly (empty path, kind unknown, no base)", () => {
    expect(calls(`export function go(url: string) { return fetch(url); }`)).toEqual(["GET [] ? unknown @go"]);
  });

  it("does not mistake route registrations for HTTP calls", () => {
    expect(calls(`app.get("/health", handler); router.post("/x", h);`)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("does not mistake an in-file router/request object for a client call", () => {
    expect(
      calls(`
      import express from "express"; const api = express.Router(); api.get("/customers", list);
      export function h(request: any) { return request.get("Content-Type"); }
    `),
    ).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("finds client.request(config) like axios(config)", () => {
    expect(calls(`export const ping = () => axios.request({ url: "/api/ping", method: "PATCH" });`)).toEqual([
      "PATCH [/api/ping] ok literal @ping",
    ]);
  });

  it("still finds a real client variable named api", () => {
    expect(
      calls(`export const api = axios.create(); export const load = () => api.get("/api/customers");`),
    ).toEqual(["GET [/api/customers] ok literal @load"]);
  });

  it("does not drop a template URL whose base is an empty head (leading ${BASE})", () => {
    expect(
      calls(`
      export const api = axios.create();
      export const one = (id: string) => api.get(\`\${API}/customers/\${id}\`);
    `),
    ).toEqual(["GET [/customers/:id] ok template base=API @one"]);
  });

  it("resolves a router receiver name to its nearest in-scope declaration, not file-wide", () => {
    expect(
      calls(`
      function setupRouter() { const api = express.Router(); api.get("/health", h); return api; }
      export function useClient() { const api = axios.create(); return api.get("/api/customers"); }
    `),
    ).toEqual(["GET [/api/customers] ok literal @useClient"]);
  });

  it("sees a destructured binding and does not skip past it to an outer router", () => {
    expect(
      calls(`
      import express from "express";
      const api = express.Router();
      export function load() { const { api } = someClientFactory(); return api.get("/customers"); }
    `),
    ).toEqual(["GET [/customers] ok literal @load"]);
  });

  it("sees a renamed destructured binding (const { client: api } = make())", () => {
    expect(
      calls(`
      import express from "express";
      const api = express.Router();
      export function load2() { const { client: api } = make(); return api.get("/orders"); }
    `),
    ).toEqual(["GET [/orders] ok literal @load2"]);
  });
});

describe("detectHttpClients", () => {
  const clients = (code: string) =>
    detectHttpClients(parsed("src/lib.ts", code)).map(
      (c) => `${c.name} ${c.factory}${c.baseLiteral ? ` baseLiteral=${c.baseLiteral}` : ""}${c.baseExpr ? ` baseExpr=${c.baseExpr}` : ""}`,
    );

  it("finds axios.create with a literal baseURL", () => {
    expect(clients(`export const api = axios.create({ baseURL: "/api" });`)).toEqual(["api axios baseLiteral=/api"]);
  });

  it("finds axios.create with a non-literal baseURL", () => {
    expect(clients(`export const billingApi = axios.create({ baseURL: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BILLING_URL });`))
      .toEqual(["billingApi axios baseExpr=process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BILLING_URL"]);
  });

  it("finds ky.create and ky.extend with a prefixUrl", () => {
    expect(clients(`
      export const a = ky.create({ prefixUrl: "/api" });
      export const b = ky.extend({ prefixUrl: "/api/v2" });
    `)).toEqual(["a ky baseLiteral=/api", "b ky baseLiteral=/api/v2"]);
  });

  it("treats any receiver's .create({ baseURL }) bound to a variable as a client (deliberately permissive)", () => {
    expect(clients(`export const client = got.create({ baseURL: "/api" });`)).toEqual(["client got baseLiteral=/api"]);
  });

  it("records a client with no options as having neither baseLiteral nor baseExpr", () => {
    const [found] = detectHttpClients(parsed("src/lib.ts", `export const api = axios.create();`));
    expect(found).toMatchObject({ name: "api", factory: "axios" });
    expect(found).not.toHaveProperty("baseLiteral");
    expect(found).not.toHaveProperty("baseExpr");
  });

  it("ignores a create() call that isn't bound to a variable", () => {
    expect(clients(`doSomething(axios.create({ baseURL: "/x" }));`)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("does not treat ky.extend's plain siblings (not .create/.extend) as clients", () => {
    expect(clients(`export const x = ky.get("/api/customers");`)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("records the declaration line", () => {
    const [found] = detectHttpClients(parsed("src/lib.ts", `\n\nexport const api = axios.create({ baseURL: "/api" });`));
    expect(found!.line).toBe(3);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3a: Implementation — `packages/cli/src/adapters/ast.ts` (full new `pathPattern` function)**

Only `pathPattern` changes; `collect`, `paramName`, `Part`, `BASE_RE`, `normalizePath` and every other export
are unchanged. Full new function (replaces the current `pathPattern`):

```ts
/** Turn a URL expression into a route pattern. Undefined when it is only a variable (nothing literal).
 * A leading non-literal expression is treated as a stripped `base` when its own text matches BASE_RE
 * (looks like a url/host/env var) or when it is immediately followed by literal text starting with "/"
 * (a dynamic prefix is almost never how a real path parameter starts). `kind` is "literal" when every
 * remaining part (after stripping the base) is literal text, "template" when any part remains dynamic. */
export function pathPattern(
  expr: ts.Expression,
): { path: string; resolved: boolean; kind: "literal" | "template"; base?: string } | undefined {
  const parts: Part[] = [];
  if (!collect(expr, parts)) return undefined;
  if (!parts.some((p) => "text" in p)) return undefined;

  let base: string | undefined;
  let rest = parts;
  const [first, second] = parts;
  if (first && !("text" in first)) {
    const text = first.expr.getText();
    const followedBySlash = !!second && "text" in second && second.text.startsWith("/");
    if (BASE_RE.test(text) || followedBySlash) {
      base = text;
      rest = parts.slice(1);
    }
  }

  let out = "";
  let resolved = true;
  let hasExpr = false;
  for (const p of rest) {
    if ("text" in p) {
      out += p.text;
      continue;
    }
    hasExpr = true;
    const name = paramName(p.expr);
    if (name === undefined) {
      resolved = false;
      out += ":param";
    } else out += `:${name}`;
  }
  return {
    path: normalizePath(out),
    resolved,
    kind: hasExpr ? "template" : "literal",
    ...(base !== undefined ? { base } : {}),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3b: Implementation — `packages/cli/src/adapters/http-calls.ts` (full new file)**

```ts
import ts from "typescript";
import type { HttpCall, HttpClient, HttpMethod } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { enclosingSymbol, isRouterName, lastName, lineOf, normalizePath, pathPattern, prop, stringValue, walk } from "./ast.js";
import type { ParsedFile } from "./types.js";

const METHODS: Record<string, HttpMethod> = { get: "GET", post: "POST", put: "PUT", patch: "PATCH", delete: "DELETE" };
const FETCH_FNS = new Set(["fetch", "$fetch", "ofetch", "useFetch"]);
const CLIENT_RE = /^(axios|ky|got|superagent)$|(api|client|http|request|instance|fetcher)$/i;

function methodOf(value: string | undefined): HttpMethod | undefined {
  return value ? METHODS[value.toLowerCase()] : undefined;
}

function isClientReceiver(receiver: string, node: ts.Node): boolean {
  return !!receiver && receiver !== "this" && CLIENT_RE.test(receiver) && !isRouterName(node, receiver);
}

/** Concatenated literal text of a URL expression: string/template literal pieces and both sides of `+`, ignoring variables. */
function literalText(expr: ts.Expression): string {
  if (ts.isParenthesizedExpression(expr)) return literalText(expr.expression);
  if (ts.isStringLiteral(expr) || ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(expr)) return expr.text;
  if (ts.isTemplateExpression(expr)) {
    return expr.head.text + expr.templateSpans.map((s) => s.literal.text).join("");
  }
  if (ts.isBinaryExpression(expr) && expr.operatorToken.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.PlusToken) {
    return literalText(expr.left) + literalText(expr.right);
  }
  return "";
}

/** A URL "looks like a URL" when its literal text has a path separator or an http(s) scheme. */
function looksLikeUrl(text: string): boolean {
  return /^\s*(https?:\/\/|\/)/.test(text) || text.includes("/");
}

export function detectHttpCalls(file: ParsedFile): HttpCall[] {
  if (!file.sf) return [];
  const out: HttpCall[] = [];
  walk(file.sf, (node) => {
    if (!ts.isCallExpression(node)) return;
    const callee = node.expression;
    const [first, second] = node.arguments;
    let method: HttpMethod | undefined;
    let urlExpr: ts.Expression | undefined;
    let client = "";

    if (ts.isIdentifier(callee) && FETCH_FNS.has(callee.text) && first) {
      const init = second && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(second) ? second : undefined;
      method = (init && methodOf(stringValue(prop(init, "method")))) ?? "GET";
      urlExpr = first;
      client = callee.text;
    } else if (ts.isIdentifier(callee) && callee.text === "axios" && first && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(first)) {
      urlExpr = prop(first, "url");
      method = methodOf(stringValue(prop(first, "method"))) ?? "GET";
      client = "axios";
    } else if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) && callee.name.text === "request" && first && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(first)) {
      const receiver = lastName(callee.expression);
      if (isClientReceiver(receiver, node)) {
        urlExpr = prop(first, "url");
        method = methodOf(stringValue(prop(first, "method"))) ?? "GET";
        client = receiver;
      }
    } else if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(callee) && METHODS[callee.name.text] && first) {
      const receiver = lastName(callee.expression);
      if (isClientReceiver(receiver, node) && pathPattern(first) && looksLikeUrl(literalText(first))) {
        method = METHODS[callee.name.text];
        urlExpr = first;
        client = receiver;
      }
    }
    if (!method || !urlExpr) return;
    const pattern = pathPattern(urlExpr);
    out.push({
      method,
      path: pattern?.path ?? "",
      raw: urlExpr.getText(),
      resolved: pattern?.resolved ?? false,
      client,
      at: { file: file.rel, line: lineOf(node), symbol: enclosingSymbol(node) },
      kind: pattern?.kind ?? "unknown",
      ...(pattern?.base !== undefined ? { base: pattern.base } : {}),
    });
  });
  return out;
}

/** "axios"/"create" -> "axios"; "ky"/"create"|"extend" -> "ky"; any other receiver's "create" -> its own name. */
function clientFactory(receiver: string, method: string): string | undefined {
  if (receiver === "axios" && method === "create") return "axios";
  if (receiver === "ky" && (method === "create" || method === "extend")) return "ky";
  if (method === "create") return receiver;
  return undefined;
}

export function detectHttpClients(file: ParsedFile): HttpClient[] {
  if (!file.sf) return [];
  const out: HttpClient[] = [];
  walk(file.sf, (node) => {
    if (!ts.isCallExpression(node) || !ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node.expression)) return;
    const receiver = node.expression.expression;
    if (!ts.isIdentifier(receiver)) return;
    const factory = clientFactory(receiver.text, node.expression.name.text);
    if (!factory) return;
    const decl = node.parent;
    if (!ts.isVariableDeclaration(decl) || !ts.isIdentifier(decl.name)) return;
    const [opts] = node.arguments;
    const obj = opts && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(opts) ? opts : undefined;
    const baseNode = obj && (prop(obj, "baseURL") ?? prop(obj, "prefixUrl"));
    const literal = baseNode && stringValue(baseNode);
    out.push({
      name: decl.name.text,
      file: file.rel,
      line: lineOf(node),
      factory,
      ...(baseNode && literal === undefined ? { baseExpr: baseNode.getText() } : {}),
      ...(literal !== undefined ? { baseLiteral: normalizePath(literal) } : {}),
    });
  });
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3c: Implementation — `packages/cli/src/adapters/run.ts` (two-line change in `runAdapters`)**

Change the import line and add one push call; everything else in `run.ts` (as left by Task 1) is unchanged:

```ts
import { detectHttpCalls, detectHttpClients } from "./http-calls.js";
```

```ts
export function runAdapters(rel: string, content: string, ctx: AdapterContext): EndpointsPart {
  const part = emptyPart();
  const isJs = JS_EXT.test(rel) && !rel.endsWith(".d.ts");
  if (!isJs && !rel.endsWith(".prisma") && !rel.endsWith(".sql")) return part;
  const file: ParsedFile = { rel, content, sf: isJs ? parseSource(rel, content) : undefined };
  part.pages.push(...detectPages(file, ctx));
  part.httpCalls.push(...detectHttpCalls(file));
  part.httpClients.push(...detectHttpClients(file));
  part.routes.push(...detectRoutes(file, ctx));
  const db = detectDb(file, ctx);
  part.dbAccess.push(...db.dbAccess);
  part.tables.push(...db.tables);
  part.models.push(...db.models);
  part.queues.push(...detectQueues(file, ctx));
  return part;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3a: Implementation — `packages/cli/src/adapters/routes.ts`**

Change the `./types.js` import line to also bring in `MountDraft`:

```ts
import { type AdapterContext, packageRelative, type MountDraft, type ParsedFile, type RouteDraft } from "./types.js";
```

Replace `routerCall` with this full new version (adds `router` to both returned shapes; every other
function in the file — `adonisStringHandler`, `frameworkOf`, `handlerOf`, `groupPrefix`, `nestRoutes`,
`nextApiRoutes`, `locateName`, `hasBackendImport`, etc. — is unchanged):

```ts
function routerCall(node: ts.CallExpression, isRouter: IsRouter, file: ParsedFile, framework: Framework): RouteDraft | undefined {
  if (!ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node.expression)) return undefined;
  const name = node.expression.name.text;
  const recv = node.expression.expression;
  const at = { file: file.rel, line: lineOf(node), symbol: enclosingSymbol(node) };

  if (name === "route" && ts.isIdentifier(recv) && isRouter(node, recv.text) && node.arguments[0] && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(node.arguments[0])) {
    const o = node.arguments[0];
    const m = stringValue(prop(o, "method"));
    const url = prop(o, "url") ?? prop(o, "path");
    const p = url && pathPattern(url);
    if (!m || !p) return undefined;
    const method = ROUTE_METHODS[m.toLowerCase()] ?? "ANY";
    return {
      method, path: p.path, ...handlerOf(prop(o, "handler"), `${method} ${p.path}`, file.sf!),
      framework: "fastify", router: recv.text, at,
    };
  }

  const method = ROUTE_METHODS[name];
  if (!method) return undefined;
  let path: string | undefined;
  let router: string | undefined;
  if (ts.isIdentifier(recv) && isRouter(node, recv.text)) {
    if (node.arguments.length < 2) return undefined;
    path = pathPattern(node.arguments[0]!)?.path;
    router = recv.text;
  } else if (
    ts.isCallExpression(recv) &&
    ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(recv.expression) &&
    recv.expression.name.text === "route" &&
    ts.isIdentifier(recv.expression.expression) &&
    isRouter(node, recv.expression.expression.text)
  ) {
    const s = stringValue(recv.arguments[0]);
    path = s === undefined ? undefined : normalizePath(s);
    router = recv.expression.expression.text;
  } else return undefined;
  if (path === undefined) return undefined;
  const full = normalizePath(`${groupPrefix(node)}/${path}`);
  return {
    method,
    path: full,
    ...handlerOf(node.arguments[node.arguments.length - 1], `${method} ${full}`, file.sf!),
    framework,
    router,
    at,
  };
}
```

Add `detectMounts` as a new exported function, placed after `detectRoutes` at the end of the file (`detectRoutes`
itself is unchanged — it already builds `isRouter`/`generic` the same way `detectMounts` needs, just not shared
across the two functions since each builds its own closure per call):

```ts
export function detectMounts(file: ParsedFile, ctx: AdapterContext): MountDraft[] {
  if (!BACKEND_DEPS.some((d) => ctx.deps.has(d))) return [];
  if (!file.sf) return [];
  const generic = new Set<string>(hasBackendImport(file.sf) ? ROUTER_NAMES : []);
  const isRouter: IsRouter = (node, name) => generic.has(name) || isRouterName(node, name);
  const out: MountDraft[] = [];
  walk(file.sf, (node) => {
    if (!ts.isCallExpression(node) || !ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node.expression)) return;
    const method = node.expression.name.text;
    const recv = node.expression.expression;
    const at = { file: file.rel, line: lineOf(node), symbol: enclosingSymbol(node) };

    if (method === "use" && ts.isIdentifier(recv) && isRouter(node, recv.text) && node.arguments.length >= 2) {
      const prefix = stringValue(node.arguments[0]);
      if (prefix === undefined) return;
      const targetArg = node.arguments[node.arguments.length - 1]!;
      if (!ts.isIdentifier(targetArg)) return;
      const loc = locateName(file.sf!, targetArg.text);
      out.push({
        prefix: normalizePath(prefix), kind: "express-use", on: recv.text, target: { name: targetArg.text },
        ...(loc.handlerImport ? { targetImport: loc.handlerImport } : {}),
        at,
      });
      return;
    }

    if (method === "register" && ts.isIdentifier(recv) && isRouter(node, recv.text) && node.arguments.length >= 2) {
      const [pluginArg, optsArg] = node.arguments;
      if (!pluginArg || !ts.isIdentifier(pluginArg)) return;
      const opts = optsArg && ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(optsArg) ? optsArg : undefined;
      const prefix = opts && stringValue(prop(opts, "prefix"));
      if (prefix === undefined) return;
      const loc = locateName(file.sf!, pluginArg.text);
      out.push({
        prefix: normalizePath(prefix), kind: "fastify-register", on: recv.text, target: { name: pluginArg.text },
        ...(loc.handlerImport ? { targetImport: loc.handlerImport } : {}),
        at,
      });
      return;
    }

    if (method === "setGlobalPrefix" && ctx.deps.has("@nestjs/core")) {
      const prefix = stringValue(node.arguments[0]);
      if (prefix === undefined) return;
      out.push({ prefix: normalizePath(prefix), kind: "nest-global", on: "", target: {}, at });
    }
  });
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3b: Implementation — `packages/cli/src/adapters/run.ts` (two-line change in `runAdapters`)**

Change the `./routes.js` import line and add one push call; everything else in `run.ts` (as left by Task 2)
is unchanged:

```ts
import { detectMounts, detectRoutes } from "./routes.js";
```

```ts
export function runAdapters(rel: string, content: string, ctx: AdapterContext): EndpointsPart {
  const part = emptyPart();
  const isJs = JS_EXT.test(rel) && !rel.endsWith(".d.ts");
  if (!isJs && !rel.endsWith(".prisma") && !rel.endsWith(".sql")) return part;
  const file: ParsedFile = { rel, content, sf: isJs ? parseSource(rel, content) : undefined };
  part.pages.push(...detectPages(file, ctx));
  part.httpCalls.push(...detectHttpCalls(file));
  part.httpClients.push(...detectHttpClients(file));
  part.routes.push(...detectRoutes(file, ctx));
  part.mounts.push(...detectMounts(file, ctx));
  const db = detectDb(file, ctx);
  part.dbAccess.push(...db.dbAccess);
  part.tables.push(...db.tables);
  part.models.push(...db.models);
  part.queues.push(...detectQueues(file, ctx));
  return part;
}
```

`finalizeEndpoints` needs no change here: Task 1 already resolves `MountDraft.targetImport` into `target.file`
(`resolveMount`), sorts `mounts` by `at.file, at.line, prefix` (`byMount`), and copies every `EndpointsPart` key
generically — `mounts` just stops being permanently empty.

### Task 4: Queue adapter — exchange, routing key, bindQueue

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/adapters/queues.ts`
- Test: `packages/cli/test/queues.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: R1 (`store/schemas.ts`) `QueueOp` with its Plan 2a shape —
  `{ queue: string; op: "publish" | "consume" | "bind"; at: Loc; exchange?: string; routingKey?: string }`
  (Task 1 lands this; this task only produces the richer values). Also `ParsedFile`, `AdapterContext`
  (`adapters/types.ts`); `enclosingSymbol`, `lastName`, `lineOf`, `stringValue`, `walk` (`adapters/ast.ts`).
- Produces (R4): `detectQueues(file: ParsedFile, ctx: AdapterContext): QueueOp[]` — signature unchanged
  from Plan 1, richer output:
  - `sendToQueue(q)` → `{ queue: q, op: "publish" }`
  - `publish(ex, key)` → `{ queue: ex && key ? "ex:key" : ex || key, op: "publish", exchange: ex, routingKey: key }`
  - `consume(q)` → `{ queue: q, op: "consume" }`
  - `bindQueue(q, ex, key)` → `{ queue: q, op: "bind", exchange: ex, routingKey: key }` (all three must be
    string literals; `key` may be `""`)
  - `assertExchange(...)` and anything else called on a RabbitMQ-shaped receiver: no `QueueOp` (ignored) —
    this already falls out of the existing `switch` having no matching `case` and no `default`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Full new version of `packages/cli/src/adapters/queues.ts`:

```ts
import ts from "typescript";
import type { QueueOp } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { enclosingSymbol, lastName, lineOf, stringValue, walk } from "./ast.js";
import type { AdapterContext, ParsedFile } from "./types.js";

const RECEIVER_RE = /(channel|^ch$|amqp|rabbit|broker|queue|mq|publisher|consumer)/i;

export function detectQueues(file: ParsedFile, ctx: AdapterContext): QueueOp[] {
  if (!file.sf || ![...ctx.deps].some((d) => /amqp|rabbit/i.test(d))) return [];
  const out: QueueOp[] = [];
  walk(file.sf, (node) => {
    if (!ts.isCallExpression(node) || !ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node.expression)) return;
    if (!RECEIVER_RE.test(lastName(node.expression.expression))) return;
    const at = { file: file.rel, line: lineOf(node), symbol: enclosingSymbol(node) };
    const [a0, a1, a2] = node.arguments;
    switch (node.expression.name.text) {
      case "sendToQueue": {
        const q = stringValue(a0);
        if (q) out.push({ queue: q, op: "publish", at });
        break;
      }
      case "publish": {
        const exchange = stringValue(a0);
        const key = stringValue(a1);
        if (exchange === undefined || key === undefined) break;
        // Fanout exchanges publish with an empty routing key; fall back to the exchange name.
        const queue = exchange && key ? `${exchange}:${key}` : exchange || key;
        if (queue) out.push({ queue, op: "publish", exchange, routingKey: key, at });
        break;
      }
      case "consume": {
        const q = stringValue(a0);
        if (q) out.push({ queue: q, op: "consume", at });
        break;
      }
      case "bindQueue": {
        const queue = stringValue(a0);
        const exchange = stringValue(a1);
        const routingKey = stringValue(a2);
        if (queue === undefined || exchange === undefined || routingKey === undefined) break;
        out.push({ queue, op: "bind", exchange, routingKey, at });
        break;
      }
      // assertExchange and anything else: not a recognised op — ignored (no case, no default).
    }
  });
  return out;
}
```

```ts
describe("CodeloomError", () => {
  it("defaults to exit code 1", () => {
    const err = new CodeloomError("boom");
    expect(err.exitCode).toBe(1);
    expect(err.message).toBe("boom");
    expect(err.name).toBe("CodeloomError");
  });

  it("accepts an explicit exit code", () => {
    expect(new CodeloomError("boom", { exitCode: 2 }).exitCode).toBe(2);

describe("dataError", () => {

    const err = dataError("bad data");
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(CodeloomError);
    expect(err.exitCode).toBe(2);

describe("formatError", () => {
  it("prints a CodeloomError's message as is", () => {
    expect(formatError(new CodeloomError("nice message"), false)).toBe("nice message");
  });

  it("prints a CodeloomError's message as is even with debug on", () => {
    expect(formatError(new CodeloomError("nice message"), true)).toBe("nice message");
  });

  it("wraps an unexpected error without a stack by default", () => {
    const text = formatError(new Error("oops"), false);
    expect(text).toBe("codeloom hit an unexpected error: oops\nPlease report it with the command you ran.");
  });

  it("includes the stack when debugging", () => {
    const err = new Error("oops");
    const text = formatError(err, true);
    expect(text.startsWith("codeloom hit an unexpected error: oops\nPlease report it with the command you ran.\n\n")).toBe(true);
    expect(text).toContain(err.stack);
  });

  it("stringifies a non-Error throw", () => {
    expect(formatError("plain string", false)).toBe(
      "codeloom hit an unexpected error: plain string\nPlease report it with the command you ran.",
    );
  });
});
```

```ts
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { CodeloomError } from "../src/errors.js";
import { WORKSPACE_FILE } from "../src/store/paths.js";
import { loadWorkspaceData, readEndpoints, readGraph } from "../src/store/read.js";
import { makeRepo } from "./helpers.js";

const GRAPH = {
  version: "1.0.0",
  project: { name: "x", languages: [], frameworks: [], description: "", analyzedAt: "", gitCommitHash: "" },
  nodes: [{ id: "file:a.ts", type: "file", name: "a.ts", summary: "", tags: [], complexity: "simple" }],
  edges: [],
  layers: [],
  tour: [],
};

const endpointsV2 = (repo: string) => ({
  version: 2, repo,
  pages: [], httpCalls: [], httpClients: [], routes: [], mounts: [],
  dbAccess: [], tables: [], models: [], queues: [],
});

function writeCodeloomFiles(repoAbs: string, files: Record<string, unknown>): void {
  const dir = join(repoAbs, ".codeloom");
  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  for (const [name, data] of Object.entries(files)) writeFileSync(join(dir, name), JSON.stringify(data));
}

function asCodeloomError(fn: () => unknown): CodeloomError {
  try {
    fn();
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof CodeloomError) return err;
    throw err;
  }
  throw new Error("expected a CodeloomError to be thrown");
}

describe("readGraph", () => {
  it("returns null when graph.json is missing", () => {
    expect(readGraph(makeRepo({}))).toBeNull();
  });

  it("reads a valid graph.json", () => {
    const repo = makeRepo({});
    writeCodeloomFiles(repo, { "graph.json": GRAPH });
    expect(readGraph(repo)?.project.name).toBe("x");
  });
});

describe("readEndpoints", () => {
  it("returns null when endpoints.json is missing", () => {
    expect(readEndpoints(makeRepo({}))).toBeNull();
  });

  it("reads a valid v2 endpoints.json", () => {
    const repo = makeRepo({});
    writeCodeloomFiles(repo, { "endpoints.json": endpointsV2("api") });
    expect(readEndpoints(repo)?.repo).toBe("api");
  });

  it("rejects a v1 endpoints.json with a re-index message, at exit code 2", () => {
    const repo = makeRepo({});
    writeCodeloomFiles(repo, {
      "endpoints.json": {
        version: 1, repo: "api", pages: [], httpCalls: [], routes: [], dbAccess: [], tables: [], models: [], queues: [],
      },
    });
    const err = asCodeloomError(() => readEndpoints(repo));
    expect(err.exitCode).toBe(2);
    expect(err.message).toMatch(/endpoints version 1\)\. Run "codeloom index" to rebuild it\.$/);
  });
});

function makeWorkspace(repos: Array<{ id: string; path: string }>): string {
  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "codeloom-ws-"));
  writeFileSync(join(root, WORKSPACE_FILE), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, name: "ws", repos, links: [] }));
  return root;
}

describe("loadWorkspaceData", () => {
  it("loads every repo's graph + endpoints, sorted by repo id", () => {
    const root = makeWorkspace([
      { id: "web", path: "web" },
      { id: "api", path: "api" },
    ]);
    for (const id of ["web", "api"]) {
      mkdirSync(join(root, id), { recursive: true });
      writeCodeloomFiles(join(root, id), { "graph.json": GRAPH, "endpoints.json": endpointsV2(id) });
    }
    const data = loadWorkspaceData(root);
    expect(data.repos.map((r) => r.ref.id)).toEqual(["api", "web"]);
    expect(data.repos[0]!.endpoints.repo).toBe("api");
    expect(data.links).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("gives an exit-2 error naming the un-indexed repo", () => {
    const root = makeWorkspace([{ id: "web", path: "web" }]);
    mkdirSync(join(root, "web"), { recursive: true });
    const err = asCodeloomError(() => loadWorkspaceData(root));
    expect(err.exitCode).toBe(2);
    expect(err.message).toMatch(/"web".*Run "codeloom index" first\./);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the exit-code-carrying error**

Full new version of `packages/cli/src/errors.ts`:
```ts
/** An error whose message is meant for the user; the CLI prints it without a stack trace.
 * exitCode is what the CLI process exits with (default 1); a data/usage problem exits 2. */
export class CodeloomError extends Error {
  readonly exitCode: number;

  constructor(message: string, opts?: { exitCode?: number }) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "CodeloomError";
    this.exitCode = opts?.exitCode ?? 1;
  }
}

/** A data or usage problem (missing/old/invalid .codeloom files, a bad flag value): exit code 2. */
export function dataError(message: string): CodeloomError {
  return new CodeloomError(message, { exitCode: 2 });
}

/** What the CLI prints for an error: a CodeloomError's message as is; anything else as a short
 * "unexpected error" note (with the stack only when debugging). */
export function formatError(err: unknown, debug: boolean): string {
  if (err instanceof CodeloomError) return err.message;
  const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
  const text = `codeloom hit an unexpected error: ${message}\nPlease report it with the command you ran.`;
  return debug && err instanceof Error && err.stack ? `${text}\n\n${err.stack}` : text;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Make `bin.ts` honour `exitCode`**

Full new version of `packages/cli/src/bin.ts`:
```ts
#!/usr/bin/env node
import { buildProgram } from "./cli.js";
import { CodeloomError, formatError } from "./errors.js";

try {
  await buildProgram().parseAsync(process.argv);
} catch (err) {
  console.error(formatError(err, process.env.CODELOOM_DEBUG === "1"));
  process.exit(err instanceof CodeloomError ? err.exitCode : 1);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4b: Widen `loadWorkspace` to return `links` and `traces`**

In `packages/cli/src/workspace/repos.ts`, change `loadWorkspace` (add `type TracesConfig, type WorkspaceFile` to
its `../store/schemas.js` import):
```ts
export function loadWorkspace(start: string): {
  root: string; name: string; repos: RepoRef[]; links: WorkspaceFile["links"]; traces?: TracesConfig;
} {
  const wsRoot = findWorkspaceRoot(start);
  if (wsRoot) {
    const ws = readJson(join(wsRoot, WORKSPACE_FILE), WorkspaceFileSchema);
    if (ws) return { root: wsRoot, name: ws.name, repos: ws.repos, links: ws.links, traces: ws.traces };
  }
  const top = gitToplevel(start);
  if (!top) {
    throw new CodeloomError(
      `No codeloom workspace or git repo found at ${resolve(start)}. Run "codeloom init" in a repo or a folder of repos first.`,
    );
  }
  return { root: top, name: basename(top), repos: [{ id: basename(top), path: "." }], links: [] };
}
```
Existing callers only read `root`/`name`/`repos`, so they keep working.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the store readers**

Create `packages/cli/src/store/read.ts`:
```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { type KnowledgeGraph, KnowledgeGraphSchema } from "@codeloom/core";
import { dataError } from "../errors.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";
import { loadWorkspace } from "../workspace/repos.js";
import { readJson } from "./json.js";
import { CODELOOM_DIR, FILES } from "./paths.js";
import { type EndpointsFile, EndpointsFileSchema, type RepoRef, type TracesConfig, type WorkspaceFile } from "./schemas.js";

/** Missing file -> null. Bad shape -> CodeloomError (via readJson). */
export function readGraph(repoAbs: string): KnowledgeGraph | null {
  return readJson(join(repoAbs, CODELOOM_DIR, FILES.graph), KnowledgeGraphSchema);
}

/** Missing file -> null. A v1 file (written by a pre-2a codeloom) -> a clear re-index error (exit
 * code 2), checked before the zod parse so this message wins over the generic "unexpected shape" one. */
export function readEndpoints(repoAbs: string): EndpointsFile | null {
  const path = join(repoAbs, CODELOOM_DIR, FILES.endpoints);
  if (!existsSync(path)) return null;
  let raw: unknown;
  try {
    raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return readJson(path, EndpointsFileSchema); // reproduces readJson's own "not valid JSON" error
  }
  if (typeof raw === "object" && raw !== null && (raw as { version?: unknown }).version === 1) {
    throw dataError(`${path} was written by an older codeloom (endpoints version 1). Run "codeloom index" to rebuild it.`);
  }
  return readJson(path, EndpointsFileSchema);
}

export interface RepoData {
  ref: RepoRef;
  abs: string;
  graph: KnowledgeGraph;
  endpoints: EndpointsFile;
}

export interface WorkspaceData {
  root: string;
  name: string;
  repos: RepoData[];
  links: WorkspaceFile["links"];
  traces?: TracesConfig;
}

/** Every repo's graph + endpoints, sorted by repo id. Any repo missing either file -> dataError
 * (exit code 2) telling the user to run "codeloom index" first. */
export function loadWorkspaceData(start: string): WorkspaceData {
  const ws = loadWorkspace(start);
  const repos: RepoData[] = ws.repos.map((ref) => {
    const abs = join(ws.root, ref.path);
    const graph = readGraph(abs);
    const endpoints = readEndpoints(abs);
    if (!graph || !endpoints) {
      throw dataError(`Repo "${ref.id}" has no .codeloom data yet. Run "codeloom index" first.`);
    }
    return { ref, abs, graph, endpoints };
  });
  repos.sort((a, b) => compare(a.ref.id, b.ref.id));
  return { root: ws.root, name: ws.name, repos, links: ws.links, traces: ws.traces };
}
```

**Files:**
