- Test: `packages/cli/test/index-repo.test.ts` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `parseSource`, `walk`, `lineOf`, `enclosingSymbol`, `stringValue`, `JS_EXT` (`adapters/ast.ts`);
  `ParsedFile` (`adapters/types.ts`); R1 `Fingerprints`, `FingerprintsSchema` (`store/schemas.ts`); R1
  `EndpointsPart` and the existing `runAdapters`, `finalizeEndpoints` (`adapters/run.ts`, unchanged);
  `Alias`, `loadAliases`, `resolveImport` (`indexer/resolve-imports.ts`, unchanged).
- Produces (R6):
  ```ts
  export interface CodeSymbol {
    name: string;
    kind: "function" | "class" | "method" | "object-method";
    lines: [number, number];
    exported: boolean;
    exportedAs?: string;
  }
  export type ImportBinding =
    | { local: string; kind: "named"; imported: string; from: string }
    | { local: string; kind: "default"; from: string }
    | { local: string; kind: "namespace"; from: string };
  export type ReExport =
    | { kind: "all"; from: string }
    | { kind: "named"; exported: string; imported: string; from: string };
  export interface ClassField { className: string; field: string; typeName: string }
  export type Callee =
    | { kind: "ident"; name: string }
    | { kind: "member"; object: string; prop: string }
    | { kind: "new-member"; className: string; prop: string }
    | { kind: "this"; prop: string }
    | { kind: "this-field"; field: string; prop: string }
    | { kind: "other"; prop?: string };
  export interface CallSite { caller: string; line: number; callee: Callee }
  export interface FileCodeIndex {
    symbols: CodeSymbol[]; imports: ImportBinding[]; reExports: ReExport[]; classFields: ClassField[];
    localNews: Array<{ caller: string; local: string; className: string }>; calls: CallSite[];
  }
  export function buildFileCodeIndex(file: ParsedFile): FileCodeIndex;
  export function symbolAt(idx: FileCodeIndex, line: number): CodeSymbol | undefined;
  export type RepoCodeIndex = Map<string, FileCodeIndex>;
  ```
  `indexer/index-repo.ts`: `FileRecord` gains `codeIndex: FileCodeIndex`; `IndexResult` gains
  `codeIndex: RepoCodeIndex; files: Set<string>; aliases: Alias[]`. Task 7 consumes all three plus
  `symbolAt`, `CallSite`, `Callee`.

**Registry inconsistency found (fixed in this task):** R1's text says `FingerprintsSchema`'s per-file
record type "gains `codeIndex: z.any()` (Task 6)", but Task 6's own `Files:` line in the skeleton (and R6's
`Consumes`/`Produces` list) never mentions `store/schemas.ts`. Without that schema change, `readJson`'s
`z.object(...)` (default "strip unknown keys" mode, not `.strict()`) would silently drop `codeIndex` every
time `fingerprints.json` is re-read from disk — so a cache *hit* (`FileRecord` loaded back from the cache
file) would always have `codeIndex: undefined`, breaking "reused from cache" from R6's own doc comment.
Fix applied: this task also edits `store/schemas.ts`, adding `codeIndex: z.any()` to `FingerprintsSchema`'s
per-file object, and Step 7 below has a test that fails without this fix (a cache-hit file's `codeIndex`
would be `undefined` and `.symbols` would throw).

Create `packages/cli/test/code-index.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildFileCodeIndex, symbolAt } from "../src/linker/code-index.js";
import { parsed } from "./adapter-helpers.js";

describe("buildFileCodeIndex — symbols", () => {
  it("finds functions, classes, methods and object-literal methods, with export info", () => {
    const idx = buildFileCodeIndex(parsed("src/customer-service.ts", `
export function helper() {
  return 1;
}

export default class CustomerService {
  async create(payload: unknown) {
    return payload;
  }
  async find(id: number) {
    return id;
  }
}

export const invoiceService = {
  async create(data: unknown) {
    return data;
  },
  async markPaid(id: number) {
    return id;
  },
};
`));
    expect(idx.symbols.map((s) => ({ name: s.name, kind: s.kind, exported: s.exported, exportedAs: s.exportedAs })))
      .toEqual([
        { name: "helper", kind: "function", exported: true, exportedAs: undefined },
        { name: "CustomerService", kind: "class", exported: true, exportedAs: "default" },
        { name: "CustomerService.create", kind: "method", exported: false, exportedAs: undefined },
        { name: "CustomerService.find", kind: "method", exported: false, exportedAs: undefined },
        { name: "invoiceService.create", kind: "object-method", exported: true, exportedAs: undefined },
        { name: "invoiceService.markPaid", kind: "object-method", exported: true, exportedAs: undefined },
      ]);
  });

  it("marks a separately exported identifier as the default export", () => {
    const idx = buildFileCodeIndex(parsed("src/new-invoice-page.tsx", `
function NewInvoicePage() {
  return null;
}
export default NewInvoicePage;
`));
    expect(idx.symbols).toEqual([
      { name: "NewInvoicePage", kind: "function", lines: [2, 4], exported: true, exportedAs: "default" },
    ]);
  });
});

describe("symbolAt", () => {
  it("finds the innermost symbol containing a line", () => {
    const idx = buildFileCodeIndex(parsed("src/customer-service.ts", `
export default class CustomerService {
  async create(payload: unknown) {
    return payload;
  }
}
`));
    expect(symbolAt(idx, 3)?.name).toBe("CustomerService.create");
    expect(symbolAt(idx, 2)?.name).toBe("CustomerService");
    expect(symbolAt(idx, 100)).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("buildFileCodeIndex — imports and re-exports", () => {
  it("records import bindings: default, named, renamed and namespace, plus CJS require", () => {
    const idx = buildFileCodeIndex(parsed("src/invoices.ts", `
import CustomerService from "./customer-service.js";
import { CustomerService as CS, findCustomer } from "./customer-service.js";
import * as events from "./events.js";
const legacyEvents = require("./legacy-events.js");
const { publish, bind: bindQueue } = require("./amqp.js");
`));
    expect(idx.imports).toEqual([
      { local: "CustomerService", kind: "default", from: "./customer-service.js" },
      { local: "CS", kind: "named", imported: "CustomerService", from: "./customer-service.js" },
      { local: "findCustomer", kind: "named", imported: "findCustomer", from: "./customer-service.js" },
      { local: "events", kind: "namespace", from: "./events.js" },
      { local: "legacyEvents", kind: "namespace", from: "./legacy-events.js" },
      { local: "publish", kind: "named", imported: "publish", from: "./amqp.js" },
      { local: "bindQueue", kind: "named", imported: "bind", from: "./amqp.js" },
    ]);
  });

  it("records re-exports (barrels)", () => {
    const idx = buildFileCodeIndex(parsed("src/index.ts", `
export * from "./customer-service.js";
export { CustomerService as CS, findCustomer } from "./customer-service.js";
`));
    expect(idx.reExports).toEqual([
      { kind: "all", from: "./customer-service.js" },
      { kind: "named", exported: "CS", imported: "CustomerService", from: "./customer-service.js" },
      { kind: "named", exported: "findCustomer", imported: "findCustomer", from: "./customer-service.js" },
    ]);
  });
});

describe("buildFileCodeIndex — class fields", () => {
  it("records constructor parameter properties and new-initialized fields", () => {
    const idx = buildFileCodeIndex(parsed("src/customers-controller.ts", `
export default class CustomersController {
  private audit = new AuditLogger();
  constructor(private customers: CustomerService) {}
  async store() {
    return this.customers.create({});
  }
}
`));
    expect(idx.classFields).toEqual([
      { className: "CustomersController", field: "audit", typeName: "AuditLogger" },
      { className: "CustomersController", field: "customers", typeName: "CustomerService" },
    ]);
  });
});

describe("buildFileCodeIndex — call sites", () => {
  it("classifies this, this-field, new-member, member, other and ident calls; tracks local `new` bindings", () => {
    const code = `
import { CustomerService } from "./customer-service.js";
import { invoiceService } from "./invoice-service.js";

export default class CustomersController {
  constructor(private customers: CustomerService) {}

  async store(body: unknown) {
    const created = await this.customers.create(body);
    this.notify();
    return created;
  }

  async show(id: number) {
    return new CustomerService().find(id);
  }

  notify() {
    invoiceService.markPaid(1);
  }
}

export function createViaLocal() {
  const svc = new CustomerService();
  return svc.create({});
}

export function dispatch(handlers: Record<string, () => void>) {
  handlers["create"]();
}

export function bootstrap() {
  createViaLocal();
}
`;
    const idx = buildFileCodeIndex(parsed("src/customers-controller.ts", code));
    expect(idx.calls).toEqual([
      { caller: "CustomersController.store", line: 9, callee: { kind: "this-field", field: "customers", prop: "create" } },
      { caller: "CustomersController.store", line: 10, callee: { kind: "this", prop: "notify" } },
      { caller: "CustomersController.show", line: 15, callee: { kind: "new-member", className: "CustomerService", prop: "find" } },
      { caller: "CustomersController.notify", line: 19, callee: { kind: "member", object: "invoiceService", prop: "markPaid" } },
      { caller: "createViaLocal", line: 25, callee: { kind: "member", object: "svc", prop: "create" } },
      { caller: "dispatch", line: 29, callee: { kind: "other", prop: "create" } },
      { caller: "bootstrap", line: 33, callee: { kind: "ident", name: "createViaLocal" } },
    ]);
    expect(idx.localNews).toEqual([{ caller: "createViaLocal", local: "svc", className: "CustomerService" }]);
  });
});

describe("buildFileCodeIndex — non-JS/TS files", () => {
  it("returns all-empty arrays", () => {
    const idx = buildFileCodeIndex(parsed("prisma/schema.prisma", `model Invoice { id Int @id }`));
    expect(idx).toEqual({ symbols: [], imports: [], reExports: [], classFields: [], localNews: [], calls: [] });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `packages/cli/src/linker/code-index.ts`:
```ts
import ts from "typescript";
import { enclosingSymbol, lineOf, stringValue, walk } from "../adapters/ast.js";
import type { ParsedFile } from "../adapters/types.js";

export interface CodeSymbol {
  name: string;
  kind: "function" | "class" | "method" | "object-method";
  lines: [number, number];
  exported: boolean;
  exportedAs?: string;
}

export type ImportBinding =
  | { local: string; kind: "named"; imported: string; from: string }
  | { local: string; kind: "default"; from: string }
  | { local: string; kind: "namespace"; from: string };

export type ReExport =
  | { kind: "all"; from: string }
  | { kind: "named"; exported: string; imported: string; from: string };

export interface ClassField {
  className: string;
  field: string;
  typeName: string;
}

export type Callee =
  | { kind: "ident"; name: string }
  | { kind: "member"; object: string; prop: string }
  | { kind: "new-member"; className: string; prop: string }
  | { kind: "this"; prop: string }
  | { kind: "this-field"; field: string; prop: string }
  | { kind: "other"; prop?: string };

export interface CallSite {
  caller: string;
  line: number;
  callee: Callee;
}

export interface FileCodeIndex {
  symbols: CodeSymbol[];
  imports: ImportBinding[];
  reExports: ReExport[];
  classFields: ClassField[];
  localNews: Array<{ caller: string; local: string; className: string }>;
  calls: CallSite[];
}

export type RepoCodeIndex = Map<string, FileCodeIndex>;

function endLineOf(node: ts.Node): number {
  const sf = node.getSourceFile();
  return sf.getLineAndCharacterOfPosition(node.getEnd()).line + 1;
}

function memberName(name: ts.PropertyName | undefined): string | undefined {
  if (!name) return undefined;
  if (ts.isIdentifier(name) || ts.isPrivateIdentifier(name) || ts.isStringLiteral(name)) return name.text;
  return undefined;
}

function hasModifier(node: ts.Node, kind: ts.SyntaxKind): boolean {
  return !!(ts.canHaveModifiers(node) && ts.getModifiers(node)?.some((m) => m.kind === kind));
}

function isExported(node: ts.Node): boolean {
  return hasModifier(node, ts.SyntaxKind.ExportKeyword);
}

function isDefaultExport(node: ts.Node): boolean {
  return hasModifier(node, ts.SyntaxKind.DefaultKeyword);
}

/** `const name = { ... }` -> "name", for an object literal directly assigned to a variable. */
function objectVarName(obj: ts.Node): string | undefined {
  if (!ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(obj)) return undefined;
  const p = obj.parent;
  return p && ts.isVariableDeclaration(p) && ts.isIdentifier(p.name) ? p.name.text : undefined;
}

function enclosingVariableStatement(node: ts.Node): ts.VariableStatement | undefined {
  let cur: ts.Node | undefined = node;
  while (cur && !ts.isVariableStatement(cur)) cur = cur.parent;
  return cur as ts.VariableStatement | undefined;
}

function typeRefName(type: ts.TypeNode | undefined): string | undefined {
  return type && ts.isTypeReferenceNode(type) && ts.isIdentifier(type.typeName) ? type.typeName.text : undefined;
}

function newExpressionClass(expr: ts.Expression | undefined): string | undefined {
  return expr && ts.isNewExpression(expr) && ts.isIdentifier(expr.expression) ? expr.expression.text : undefined;
}

function classify(expr: ts.Expression): Callee {
  if (ts.isIdentifier(expr)) return { kind: "ident", name: expr.text };
  if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(expr)) {
    const prop = expr.name.text;
    const obj = expr.expression;
    if (obj.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ThisKeyword) return { kind: "this", prop };
    if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(obj) && obj.expression.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ThisKeyword) {
      return { kind: "this-field", field: obj.name.text, prop };
    }
    const newClass = newExpressionClass(obj);
    if (newClass) return { kind: "new-member", className: newClass, prop };
    if (ts.isIdentifier(obj)) return { kind: "member", object: obj.text, prop };
    return { kind: "other", prop };
  }
  if (ts.isElementAccessExpression(expr)) {
    const prop = stringValue(expr.argumentExpression);
    return prop !== undefined ? { kind: "other", prop } : { kind: "other" };
  }
  return { kind: "other" };
}

export function buildFileCodeIndex(file: ParsedFile): FileCodeIndex {
  const idx: FileCodeIndex = { symbols: [], imports: [], reExports: [], classFields: [], localNews: [], calls: [] };
  if (!file.sf) return idx;

  walk(file.sf, (node) => {
    if (ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node) && node.name) {
      idx.symbols.push({
        name: node.name.text,
        kind: "function",
        lines: [lineOf(node), endLineOf(node)],
        exported: isExported(node),
        exportedAs: isDefaultExport(node) ? "default" : undefined,
      });
    }

    if (ts.isClassDeclaration(node) && node.name) {
      const cls = node.name.text;
      idx.symbols.push({
        name: cls,
        kind: "class",
        lines: [lineOf(node), endLineOf(node)],
        exported: isExported(node),
        exportedAs: isDefaultExport(node) ? "default" : undefined,
      });
      for (const m of node.members) {
        if (ts.isMethodDeclaration(m) || ts.isGetAccessor(m) || ts.isSetAccessor(m)) {
          const name = memberName(m.name);
          if (name) idx.symbols.push({ name: `${cls}.${name}`, kind: "method", lines: [lineOf(m), endLineOf(m)], exported: false });
        }
        if (ts.isPropertyDeclaration(m)) {
          const name = memberName(m.name);
          const typeName = typeRefName(m.type) ?? newExpressionClass(m.initializer);
          if (name && typeName) idx.classFields.push({ className: cls, field: name, typeName });
        }
        if (ts.isConstructorDeclaration(m)) {
          for (const p of m.parameters) {
            if (!ts.isIdentifier(p.name)) continue;
            const isParamProp = ts.canHaveModifiers(p) && (ts.getModifiers(p)?.length ?? 0) > 0;
            const typeName = typeRefName(p.type);
            if (isParamProp && typeName) idx.classFields.push({ className: cls, field: p.name.text, typeName });
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(node)) {
      const objName = objectVarName(node);
      if (objName) {
        const stmt = enclosingVariableStatement(node);
        const exported = !!stmt && isExported(stmt);
        for (const p of node.properties) {
          if (ts.isMethodDeclaration(p)) {
            const name = memberName(p.name);
            if (name) idx.symbols.push({ name: `${objName}.${name}`, kind: "object-method", lines: [lineOf(p), endLineOf(p)], exported });
          } else if (ts.isPropertyAssignment(p) && (ts.isArrowFunction(p.initializer) || ts.isFunctionExpression(p.initializer))) {
            const name = memberName(p.name);
            if (name) {
              idx.symbols.push({
                name: `${objName}.${name}`, kind: "object-method",
                lines: [lineOf(p.initializer), endLineOf(p.initializer)], exported,
              });
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (ts.isImportDeclaration(node) && ts.isStringLiteral(node.moduleSpecifier)) {
      const from = node.moduleSpecifier.text;
      const clause = node.importClause;
      if (clause?.name) idx.imports.push({ local: clause.name.text, kind: "default", from });
      if (clause?.namedBindings) {
        if (ts.isNamespaceImport(clause.namedBindings)) {
          idx.imports.push({ local: clause.namedBindings.name.text, kind: "namespace", from });
        } else {
          for (const el of clause.namedBindings.elements) {
            idx.imports.push({
              local: el.name.text, kind: "named",
              imported: el.propertyName ? el.propertyName.text : el.name.text, from,
            });
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (
      ts.isVariableDeclaration(node) && node.initializer && ts.isCallExpression(node.initializer) &&
      ts.isIdentifier(node.initializer.expression) && node.initializer.expression.text === "require"
    ) {
      const from = stringValue(node.initializer.arguments[0]);
      if (from) {
        if (ts.isIdentifier(node.name)) {
          idx.imports.push({ local: node.name.text, kind: "namespace", from });
        } else if (ts.isObjectBindingPattern(node.name)) {
          for (const el of node.name.elements) {
            if (ts.isOmittedExpression(el) || !ts.isIdentifier(el.name)) continue;
            const imported = el.propertyName && ts.isIdentifier(el.propertyName) ? el.propertyName.text : el.name.text;
            idx.imports.push({ local: el.name.text, kind: "named", imported, from });
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (ts.isExportDeclaration(node) && node.moduleSpecifier && ts.isStringLiteral(node.moduleSpecifier)) {
      const from = node.moduleSpecifier.text;
      if (!node.exportClause) {
        idx.reExports.push({ kind: "all", from });
      } else if (ts.isNamedExports(node.exportClause)) {
        for (const el of node.exportClause.elements) {
          idx.reExports.push({
            kind: "named", exported: el.name.text,
            imported: el.propertyName ? el.propertyName.text : el.name.text, from,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    if (ts.isExportAssignment(node) && !node.isExportEquals && ts.isIdentifier(node.expression)) {
      const sym = idx.symbols.find((s) => s.name === (node.expression as ts.Identifier).text);
      if (sym) {
        sym.exported = true;
        sym.exportedAs = "default";
      }
    }

    if (ts.isVariableDeclaration(node) && ts.isIdentifier(node.name)) {
      const className = newExpressionClass(node.initializer);
      if (className) idx.localNews.push({ caller: enclosingSymbol(node), local: node.name.text, className });
    }

    if (ts.isCallExpression(node)) {
      idx.calls.push({ caller: enclosingSymbol(node), line: lineOf(node), callee: classify(node.expression) });
    }
  });

  return idx;
}

export function symbolAt(idx: FileCodeIndex, line: number): CodeSymbol | undefined {
  let best: CodeSymbol | undefined;
  for (const s of idx.symbols) {
    if (line < s.lines[0] || line > s.lines[1]) continue;
    if (!best || s.lines[1] - s.lines[0] < best.lines[1] - best.lines[0]) best = s;
  }
  return best;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Extend `FingerprintsSchema` so the cache keeps `codeIndex` across a disk round-trip**

In `packages/cli/src/store/schemas.ts`, change the `files` field of `FingerprintsSchema` (full new version
of that declaration — everything else in the file is unchanged by this task):
```ts
export const FingerprintsSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(2),
  contextKey: z.string(),
  indexedAt: z.string(),
  gitCommitHash: z.string(),
  files: z.record(
    z.string(),
    z.object({ hash: z.string(), structure: z.any(), calls: z.any(), endpoints: z.any(), codeIndex: z.any() }),
  ),
});
```
(Without this, `readJson`'s `z.object(...)` — "strip unknown keys" by default, not `.strict()` — would
silently drop `codeIndex` on every read back from `fingerprints.json`, so a cache-hit `FileRecord` would
always have `codeIndex: undefined`. Step 7's second new test fails without this change.)

- [ ] **Step 6: Wire the code index into `indexRepo`**

Full new version of `packages/cli/src/indexer/index-repo.ts`:
```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { contentHash, type KnowledgeGraph } from "@codeloom/core";
import { JS_EXT, parseSource } from "../adapters/ast.js";
import { finalizeEndpoints, loadContext, runAdapters } from "../adapters/run.js";
import type { EndpointsPart, ParsedFile } from "../adapters/types.js";
import { CodeloomError } from "../errors.js";
import { buildFileCodeIndex, type FileCodeIndex, type RepoCodeIndex } from "../linker/code-index.js";
import { readJson, writeJsonAtomic, writeJsonIfChanged } from "../store/json.js";
import { CODELOOM_DIR, FILES } from "../store/paths.js";
import {
  defaultRepoConfig, type EndpointsFile, type Fingerprints, FingerprintsSchema, RepoConfigSchema,
} from "../store/schemas.js";
import { VERSION } from "../version.js";
import { listFiles } from "../workspace/files.js";
import { git } from "../workspace/git.js";
import { gitHead } from "../workspace/repos.js";
import { buildGraph } from "./build-graph.js";
import { type Alias, loadAliases, resolveImport } from "./resolve-imports.js";
import { analyzeStructure, EMPTY_STRUCTURE, type FileEntry, getPlugin, isSourceFile } from "./structure.js";

const MAX_BYTES = 1_000_000;

// Bump this whenever a cached record's shape changes (e.g. new fields on FileRecord,
// a different endpoints/structure format) so stale cache entries get invalidated.
export const CACHE_FORMAT = 4;

/** The cache is disposable: a corrupt, wrong-shape or old-format file is discarded and rebuilt. */
function readCache(path: string): Fingerprints | null {
  try {
    return readJson(path, FingerprintsSchema);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function checkRepo(repoAbs: string, repoId: string): void {
  if (!existsSync(repoAbs) || !statSync(repoAbs).isDirectory()) {
    throw new CodeloomError(
      `Repo "${repoId}" was not found at ${repoAbs}. Fix its path in codeloom.workspace.json, or run "codeloom init" again.`,
    );
  }
  let inWorkTree = false;
  try {
    inWorkTree = git(repoAbs, ["rev-parse", "--is-inside-work-tree"]).trim() === "true";
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof CodeloomError) throw err;
    inWorkTree = false;
  }
  if (!inWorkTree) {
    throw new CodeloomError(
      `Repo "${repoId}" at ${repoAbs} is not a git repo. Run "git init" there, or run "codeloom init" in the right folder.`,
    );
  }
}

export interface IndexStats {
  files: number;
  parsed: number;
  reused: number;
  skipped: Array<{ file: string; reason: string }>;
}

export interface IndexResult {
  graph: KnowledgeGraph;
  endpoints: EndpointsFile;
  stats: IndexStats;
  codeIndex: RepoCodeIndex;
  files: Set<string>;
  aliases: Alias[];
}

type FileRecord = Fingerprints["files"][string] & FileEntry & { endpoints: EndpointsPart; codeIndex: FileCodeIndex };

/** The same shape adapters build internally (adapters/run.ts), so the linker's own AST pass sees
 * exactly what the adapters saw. */
function toParsedFile(rel: string, content: string): ParsedFile {
  const isJs = JS_EXT.test(rel) && !rel.endsWith(".d.ts");
  return { rel, content, sf: isJs ? parseSource(rel, content) : undefined };
}

export async function indexRepo(
  repoAbs: string,
  repoId: string,
  opts?: { codeloomVersion?: string },
): Promise<IndexResult> {
  checkRepo(repoAbs, repoId);
  const dir = join(repoAbs, CODELOOM_DIR);
  const config = readJson(join(dir, FILES.config), RepoConfigSchema) ?? defaultRepoConfig();
  const files = listFiles(repoAbs, config.ignore);
  const ctx = loadContext(repoAbs, files);
  const codeloomVersion = opts?.codeloomVersion ?? VERSION;
  const contextKey = contentHash(
    [`format:${CACHE_FORMAT}`, `codeloom:${codeloomVersion}`, `deps:${ctx.key}`].join(","),
  );
  const cachePath = join(dir, FILES.fingerprints);
  const old = readCache(cachePath);
  const oldFiles = old && old.contextKey === contextKey ? old.files : {};

  const plugin = await getPlugin();
  const entries = new Map<string, FileEntry>();
  const parts: EndpointsPart[] = [];
  const next: Record<string, FileRecord> = {};
  const stats: IndexStats = { files: files.length, parsed: 0, reused: 0, skipped: [] };
  const empty = (rel: string) => entries.set(rel, { structure: EMPTY_STRUCTURE, calls: [] });

  for (const rel of files) {
    if (!isSourceFile(rel)) {
      empty(rel);
      continue;
    }
    const abs = join(repoAbs, rel);
    let record: FileRecord;
    try {
      const size = statSync(abs).size;
      if (size > MAX_BYTES) {
        stats.skipped.push({ file: rel, reason: `larger than 1 MB (${size} bytes)` });
        empty(rel);
        continue;
      }
      const content = readFileSync(abs, "utf8");
      const hash = contentHash(content);
      const hit = oldFiles[rel] as FileRecord | undefined;
      if (hit && hit.hash === hash) {
        record = hit;
        stats.reused++;
      } else {
        record = {
          hash,
          ...analyzeStructure(plugin, rel, content),
          endpoints: runAdapters(rel, content, ctx.contextFor(rel)),
          codeIndex: buildFileCodeIndex(toParsedFile(rel, content)),
        };
        stats.parsed++;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      stats.skipped.push({ file: rel, reason: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
      empty(rel);
      continue;
    }
    next[rel] = record;
    entries.set(rel, { structure: record.structure, calls: record.calls });
    parts.push(record.endpoints);
  }

  const head = gitHead(repoAbs);
  const aliases = loadAliases(repoAbs, files);
  const fileSet = new Set(files);
  const graph = buildGraph(repoId, head, entries, aliases);
  // graph.json and endpoints.json are committed: keep them a pure function of the code, so
  // re-indexing unchanged code leaves git clean. Time and commit go to the git-ignored cache.
  graph.project.analyzedAt = "";
  graph.project.gitCommitHash = "";
  const endpoints = finalizeEndpoints(repoId, parts, (from, spec) => resolveImport(from, spec, fileSet, aliases));
  writeJsonIfChanged(join(dir, FILES.graph), graph);
  writeJsonIfChanged(join(dir, FILES.endpoints), endpoints);
  writeJsonAtomic(cachePath, {
    version: 2,
    contextKey,
    indexedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    gitCommitHash: head,
    files: next,
  } satisfies Fingerprints);

  const codeIndex: RepoCodeIndex = new Map();
  for (const [rel, record] of Object.entries(next)) {
    if (JS_EXT.test(rel) && !rel.endsWith(".d.ts")) codeIndex.set(rel, record.codeIndex);
  }

  return { graph, endpoints, stats, codeIndex, files: fileSet, aliases };
}
```

```ts
  it("builds a per-repo code index for JS/TS files only", async () => {
```

### Task 7: Call resolver

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/cli/src/linker/resolve-calls.ts`
- Test: `packages/cli/test/resolve-calls.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (R6, produced by Task 6, assumed to exist exactly as registered):
  `linker/code-index.ts` exports `buildFileCodeIndex(file: ParsedFile): FileCodeIndex`, `symbolAt`, and types
  `CodeSymbol { name; kind: "function"|"class"|"method"|"object-method"; lines: [number,number]; exported: boolean; exportedAs?: string }`,
  `ImportBinding = { local; kind:"named"; imported; from } | { local; kind:"default"; from } | { local; kind:"namespace"; from }`,
  `ReExport = { kind:"all"; from } | { kind:"named"; exported; imported; from }`,
  `ClassField { className; field; typeName }`,
  `Callee = {kind:"ident";name} | {kind:"member";object;prop} | {kind:"new-member";className;prop} | {kind:"this";prop} | {kind:"this-field";field;prop} | {kind:"other";prop?}`,
  `CallSite { caller; line; callee }`,
  `FileCodeIndex { symbols; imports; reExports; classFields; localNews: Array<{caller;local;className}>; calls }`,
  `RepoCodeIndex = Map<string, FileCodeIndex>`.
  Also consumes `resolveImport(fromRel, source, files, aliases)` + `Alias` (`indexer/resolve-imports.ts`, real code today),
  `Confidence` (`store/schemas.ts`, added by Task 1), `compare` (`util.ts`).
- Produces (R7, exact names/shapes other tasks rely on):
  `SymbolRef { repo; file; symbol }`,
  `ResolvedCall { site: CallSite; target: SymbolRef; confidence: Confidence; via: "local"|"import"|"barrel"|"new"|"this"|"di"|"object"|"unique-method" }`,
  `RepoResolveInput { repo; index: RepoCodeIndex; files: Set<string>; aliases: Alias[] }`,
  `CallResolver { resolve(file, site); callsFrom(ref); callersOf(ref); resolveExport(file, exportedName); fileIndex(file) }`,
  `createCallResolver(input: RepoResolveInput): CallResolver`,
  `MAX_DEPTH = 6`,
  `WalkStep { ref: SymbolRef; depth: number; via?: ResolvedCall; parent?: SymbolRef }`,
  `walkCalls(resolver, start, maxDepth?)`, `walkCallers(resolver, start, maxDepth?)`.

**Design notes for the implementer (binding, resolves ambiguity the registry leaves open):**
- `via: "import"` vs `"barrel"`: only the plain-identifier import path (`Callee.kind === "ident"`) distinguishes
  them, by whether `resolveExport` crossed ≥1 `export *`/`export {x} from` hop to find the target. Every other
  via tag (`"new"`, `"this"`, `"di"`, `"object"`) keeps its own tag regardless of how many barrel hops the
  class/object lookup crossed internally.
- `this.m()` / `new X().m()` / `this.svc.m()` all end up resolving "a method on class/object Y, found starting
  from file F" via one shared helper (`resolveClassMethod`) that itself may cross barrels; only the outer call
  shape decides the `via` tag.
- Object-literal service resolution (`svc.m()` where `svc` is an imported named export whose target module
  exports an object literal, i.e. a `CodeSymbol` named `"<obj>.<method>"` with `kind: "object-method"`) is
  implemented even though the demo-shop tests below don't exercise it from the resolver's own unit tests —
  Task 12's fixture (`billing/src/invoice-service.ts`) depends on it, so it must be real, not a stub.
  Default-export object literals are out of scope (no reliable name to key on); such calls fall through to
  the unique-method guess.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildFileCodeIndex } from "../src/linker/code-index.js";
import type { RepoCodeIndex } from "../src/linker/code-index.js";
import { createCallResolver, MAX_DEPTH, walkCalls, walkCallers } from "../src/linker/resolve-calls.js";
import type { CallResolver, SymbolRef } from "../src/linker/resolve-calls.js";
import { parsed } from "./adapter-helpers.js";

function repoOf(files: Record<string, string>): { index: RepoCodeIndex; files: Set<string> } {
  const index: RepoCodeIndex = new Map();
  for (const [rel, code] of Object.entries(files)) index.set(rel, buildFileCodeIndex(parsed(rel, code)));
  return { index, files: new Set(Object.keys(files)) };
}

function resolverOf(files: Record<string, string>): CallResolver {
  const { index, files: fileSet } = repoOf(files);
  return createCallResolver({ repo: "api", index, files: fileSet, aliases: [] });
}

function ref(file: string, symbol: string): SymbolRef {
  return { repo: "api", file, symbol };
}

function siteAt(resolver: CallResolver, file: string, symbol: string) {
  // helper for tests that only check the first/only call site of a symbol
  const calls = resolver.callsFrom({ repo: "api", file, symbol });
  return calls[0] ?? null;
}

const CUSTOMER_SERVICE = `
  export class CustomerService {
    create(data: unknown) { return data; }
    find(id: number) { return { id }; }
  }
`;
const AUDIT_SERVICE = `
  export class AuditService {
    log(action: string) { return action; }
  }
`;

describe("createCallResolver", () => {
  it("resolves a plain call to a local top-level function", () => {
    const resolver = resolverOf({
      "a.ts": `
        function helper() { return 1; }
        export function run() { return helper(); }
      `,
    });

    expect(rc).toMatchObject({ target: { file: "a.ts", symbol: "helper" }, confidence: "exact", via: "local" });
```

```ts
describe("unique-method guess fallback and object-literal services", () => {
  it("guesses a method call on an unresolvable receiver when the method name is unique repo-wide", () => {
    const resolver = resolverOf({
      "services/weird-service.ts": `export class WeirdService { uniqueOp() { return 1; } }`,
      "controllers/unknown.ts": `
        export class UnknownController {
          run(svc: any) { return svc.uniqueOp(); }
        }
      `,
    });
    const rc = siteAt(resolver, "controllers/unknown.ts", "UnknownController.run");
    expect(rc).toMatchObject({
      target: { file: "services/weird-service.ts", symbol: "WeirdService.uniqueOp" },
      confidence: "guess",
      via: "unique-method",
    });
  });

  it("does not guess when the method name is ambiguous repo-wide", () => {
    const resolver = resolverOf({
      "services/a.ts": `export class A { run() { return 1; } }`,
      "services/b.ts": `export class B { run() { return 2; } }`,
      "controllers/c.ts": `
        export class C { call(svc: any) { return svc.run(); } }
      `,

      target: { file: "services/invoice-service.ts", symbol: "invoiceService.create" },
      confidence: "exact",
      via: "object",

});
```

```ts
describe("walkCalls: depth-6 limit and cycle guard", () => {
  it("stops expanding at MAX_DEPTH (6 hops from the start)", () => {
    const resolver = resolverOf({
      "chain/steps.ts": `
        export function step0() { return step1(); }
        export function step1() { return step2(); }
        export function step2() { return step3(); }
        export function step3() { return step4(); }
        export function step4() { return step5(); }
        export function step5() { return step6(); }
        export function step6() { return step7(); }
        export function step7() { return 0; }
      `,
    });
    const steps = walkCalls(resolver, ref("chain/steps.ts", "step0"));
    expect(MAX_DEPTH).toBe(6);
    expect(steps).toHaveLength(7); // step0..step6 (depth 0..6); step7 is never reached
    expect(steps.map((s) => s.ref.symbol)).not.toContain("step7");
    expect(steps[steps.length - 1]).toMatchObject({ ref: { symbol: "step6" }, depth: 6 });
  });

  it("does not loop forever on a call cycle", () => {
    const resolver = resolverOf({
      "chain/cycle.ts": `
        export function ping() { return pong(); }
        export function pong() { return ping(); }
      `,
    });
    const steps = walkCalls(resolver, ref("chain/cycle.ts", "ping"));
    expect(steps.map((s) => s.ref.symbol)).toEqual(["ping", "pong"]);
  });
});

describe("callersOf / walkCallers", () => {
  it("finds the resolved caller of a method (reverse edge)", () => {
    const resolver = resolverOf({
      "services/customer-service.ts": CUSTOMER_SERVICE,
      "controllers/customers.ts": `
        import { CustomerService } from "../services/customer-service.js";
        export class CustomersController {
          store() { return new CustomerService().create({}); }
        }
      `,
    });
    const target = ref("services/customer-service.ts", "CustomerService.create");
    const callers = resolver.callersOf(target);
    expect(callers).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(callers[0]!.from).toEqual(ref("controllers/customers.ts", "CustomersController.store"));

    const steps = walkCallers(resolver, target);
    expect(steps.map((s) => s.ref.symbol)).toEqual(["CustomerService.create", "CustomersController.store"]);
  });
});

describe("fileIndex", () => {
  it("returns the cached per-file index, or undefined for an unknown file", () => {
    const resolver = resolverOf({ "services/customer-service.ts": CUSTOMER_SERVICE });
    expect(resolver.fileIndex("services/customer-service.ts")?.symbols.map((s) => s.name)).toContain("CustomerService.create");
    expect(resolver.fileIndex("nope.ts")).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`packages/cli/src/linker/resolve-calls.ts`:
```ts
import type { Alias } from "../indexer/resolve-imports.js";
import { resolveImport } from "../indexer/resolve-imports.js";
import type { Confidence } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";
import type { Callee, CallSite, FileCodeIndex, RepoCodeIndex } from "./code-index.js";

export interface SymbolRef {
  repo: string;
  file: string;
  symbol: string;
}

export interface ResolvedCall {
  site: CallSite;
  target: SymbolRef;
  /** "exact" for import/new/this/DI/object; "guess" for the unique-method fallback. */
  confidence: Confidence;
  via: "local" | "import" | "barrel" | "new" | "this" | "di" | "object" | "unique-method";
}

export interface RepoResolveInput {
  repo: string;
  index: RepoCodeIndex;
  files: Set<string>;
  aliases: Alias[];
}

export interface CallResolver {
  resolve(file: string, site: CallSite): ResolvedCall | null;
  callsFrom(ref: SymbolRef): ResolvedCall[];
  callersOf(ref: SymbolRef): Array<{ from: SymbolRef; call: ResolvedCall }>;
  resolveExport(file: string, exportedName: string): SymbolRef | null;
  /** The cached per-file index this resolver was built over (used by linkUi / findHandler with symbolAt). */
  fileIndex(file: string): FileCodeIndex | undefined;
}

export const MAX_DEPTH = 6;
const BARREL_MAX_HOPS = 3;

export interface WalkStep {
  ref: SymbolRef;
  depth: number;
  via?: ResolvedCall;
  parent?: SymbolRef;
}

interface ExportHit {
  file: string;
  symbol: string;
  /** Number of export-* / export{}-from hops crossed to find this symbol (0 = declared in the start file). */
  hops: number;
}
```

```ts
function splitDotted(name: string): { base: string; rest: string } {
  const dot = name.indexOf(".");
  return dot < 0 ? { base: name, rest: "" } : { base: name.slice(0, dot), rest: name.slice(dot) };
}

/** Follow `exportedName` (possibly dotted, e.g. "CustomerService.create") through `export *` / named
 * re-exports starting at `file`, up to BARREL_MAX_HOPS files beyond the start. Direct declarations
 * (symbols[].exported) always win before any re-export is followed. */
function resolveExportIn(
  index: RepoCodeIndex,
  files: Set<string>,
  aliases: Alias[],
  file: string,
  exportedName: string,
  hops: number,
): ExportHit | null {
  const fi = index.get(file);
  if (!fi) return null;
  const direct = fi.symbols.find(
    (s) => s.exported && (s.name === exportedName || (exportedName === "default" && s.exportedAs === "default")),
  );
  if (direct) return { file, symbol: direct.name, hops };
  if (hops >= BARREL_MAX_HOPS) return null;
  const { base, rest } = splitDotted(exportedName);
  for (const re of fi.reExports) {
    if (re.kind === "all") {
      const target = resolveImport(file, re.from, files, aliases);
      if (!target) continue;
      const hit = resolveExportIn(index, files, aliases, target, exportedName, hops + 1);
      if (hit) return hit;
    } else if (re.exported === base) {
      const target = resolveImport(file, re.from, files, aliases);
      if (!target) continue;
      const hit = resolveExportIn(index, files, aliases, target, re.imported + rest, hops + 1);
      if (hit) return hit;
    }
  }
  return null;
}
```

```ts
/** Class name of a caller symbol ("Class.method" -> "Class"); undefined for top-level functions. */
function classOfCaller(caller: string): string | undefined {
  const dot = caller.indexOf(".");
  return dot < 0 ? undefined : caller.slice(0, dot);
}

/** Resolve `<localClassName>.<method>` starting at `file`: a method declared directly in `file` wins;
 * otherwise `localClassName` must be an import binding, chased (default export's real class name is
 * read off the target file's own symbols) through barrels via resolveExportIn. */
function resolveClassMethod(
  index: RepoCodeIndex,
  files: Set<string>,
  aliases: Alias[],
  file: string,
  localClassName: string,
  method: string,
): ExportHit | null {
  const fi = index.get(file);
  if (!fi) return null;
  const name = `${localClassName}.${method}`;
  const direct = fi.symbols.find((s) => s.name === name && s.kind === "method");
  if (direct) return { file, symbol: direct.name, hops: 0 };
  const imp = fi.imports.find((i) => i.local === localClassName && (i.kind === "named" || i.kind === "default"));
  if (!imp) return null;
  const target = resolveImport(file, imp.from, files, aliases);
  if (!target) return null;
  if (imp.kind === "default") {
    const tf = index.get(target);
    const cls = tf?.symbols.find((s) => s.kind === "class" && s.exportedAs === "default");
    return cls ? resolveExportIn(index, files, aliases, target, `${cls.name}.${method}`, 0) : null;
  }
  return resolveExportIn(index, files, aliases, target, `${imp.imported}.${method}`, 0);
}

/** Resolve `<objectLocal>.<prop>` as an object-literal service method: declared locally as an
 * `object-method` symbol, or imported (named/default) from a file that declares it. Default-export
 * object literals are not supported (no name to key the method lookup on). */
function resolveObjectLiteral(
  index: RepoCodeIndex,
  files: Set<string>,
  aliases: Alias[],
  file: string,
  objectLocal: string,
  prop: string,
): ExportHit | null {
  const fi = index.get(file);
  if (!fi) return null;
  const name = `${objectLocal}.${prop}`;
  const local = fi.symbols.find((s) => s.kind === "object-method" && s.name === name);
  if (local) return { file, symbol: local.name, hops: 0 };
  const imp = fi.imports.find((i) => i.local === objectLocal && i.kind === "named");
  if (!imp) return null;
  const target = resolveImport(file, imp.from, files, aliases);
  return target ? resolveExportIn(index, files, aliases, target, `${imp.imported}.${prop}`, 0) : null;
}
```

```ts
/** Repo-wide fallback: a method/object-method named exactly `method` on exactly one class/object
 * anywhere in the repo. More than one candidate (or zero) means no guess is made. */
function guessUniqueMethod(repo: string, index: RepoCodeIndex, site: CallSite, method: string): ResolvedCall | null {
  const suffix = `.${method}`;
  let hit: { file: string; symbol: string } | null = null;
  for (const [file, fi] of index) {
    for (const s of fi.symbols) {
      if ((s.kind === "method" || s.kind === "object-method") && s.name.endsWith(suffix)) {
        if (hit) return null;
        hit = { file, symbol: s.name };
      }
    }
  }
  if (!hit) return null;
  return { site, target: { repo, file: hit.file, symbol: hit.symbol }, confidence: "guess", via: "unique-method" };
}

function resolveIdent(input: RepoResolveInput, file: string, site: CallSite, callee: Extract<Callee, { kind: "ident" }>): ResolvedCall | null {
  const { repo, index, files, aliases } = input;
  const fi = index.get(file);
  if (!fi) return null;
  const local = fi.symbols.find((s) => s.name === callee.name && s.kind === "function");
  if (local) return { site, target: { repo, file, symbol: local.name }, confidence: "exact", via: "local" };
  const imp = fi.imports.find((i) => i.local === callee.name && (i.kind === "named" || i.kind === "default"));
  if (imp) {
    const target = resolveImport(file, imp.from, files, aliases);
    const exportedName = imp.kind === "default" ? "default" : imp.imported;
    const hit = target ? resolveExportIn(index, files, aliases, target, exportedName, 0) : null;
    if (hit) {
      return { site, target: { repo, file: hit.file, symbol: hit.symbol }, confidence: "exact", via: hit.hops > 0 ? "barrel" : "import" };
    }
  }
  return guessUniqueMethod(repo, index, site, callee.name);
}
```

```ts
function resolveMember(input: RepoResolveInput, file: string, site: CallSite, callee: Extract<Callee, { kind: "member" }>): ResolvedCall | null {
  const { repo, index, files, aliases } = input;
  const fi = index.get(file);
  if (!fi) return null;

  const ns = fi.imports.find((i) => i.local === callee.object && i.kind === "namespace");
  if (ns) {
    const target = resolveImport(file, ns.from, files, aliases);
    const hit = target ? resolveExportIn(index, files, aliases, target, callee.prop, 0) : null;
    if (hit) return { site, target: { repo, file: hit.file, symbol: hit.symbol }, confidence: "exact", via: "import" };
  }

  const localNew = fi.localNews.find((n) => n.caller === site.caller && n.local === callee.object);
  if (localNew) {
    const hit = resolveClassMethod(index, files, aliases, file, localNew.className, callee.prop);
    if (hit) return { site, target: { repo, file: hit.file, symbol: hit.symbol }, confidence: "exact", via: "new" };
  }

  const obj = resolveObjectLiteral(index, files, aliases, file, callee.object, callee.prop);
  if (obj) return { site, target: { repo, file: obj.file, symbol: obj.symbol }, confidence: "exact", via: "object" };

  return guessUniqueMethod(repo, index, site, callee.prop);
}

function resolveNewMember(input: RepoResolveInput, file: string, site: CallSite, callee: Extract<Callee, { kind: "new-member" }>): ResolvedCall | null {
  const { repo, index, files, aliases } = input;
  const hit = resolveClassMethod(index, files, aliases, file, callee.className, callee.prop);
  if (hit) return { site, target: { repo, file: hit.file, symbol: hit.symbol }, confidence: "exact", via: "new" };
  return guessUniqueMethod(repo, index, site, callee.prop);
}

function resolveThis(input: RepoResolveInput, file: string, site: CallSite, callee: Extract<Callee, { kind: "this" }>): ResolvedCall | null {
  const { repo, index, files, aliases } = input;
  const cls = classOfCaller(site.caller);
  if (cls) {
    const hit = resolveClassMethod(index, files, aliases, file, cls, callee.prop);
    if (hit) return { site, target: { repo, file: hit.file, symbol: hit.symbol }, confidence: "exact", via: "this" };
  }
  return guessUniqueMethod(repo, index, site, callee.prop);
}

function resolveThisField(input: RepoResolveInput, file: string, site: CallSite, callee: Extract<Callee, { kind: "this-field" }>): ResolvedCall | null {
  const { repo, index, files, aliases } = input;
  const fi = index.get(file);
  const cls = classOfCaller(site.caller);
  if (fi && cls) {
    const field = fi.classFields.find((f) => f.className === cls && f.field === callee.field);
    if (field) {
      const hit = resolveClassMethod(index, files, aliases, file, field.typeName, callee.prop);
      if (hit) return { site, target: { repo, file: hit.file, symbol: hit.symbol }, confidence: "exact", via: "di" };
    }
  }
  return guessUniqueMethod(repo, index, site, callee.prop);
}

function resolveOther(input: RepoResolveInput, site: CallSite, callee: Extract<Callee, { kind: "other" }>): ResolvedCall | null {
  return callee.prop ? guessUniqueMethod(input.repo, input.index, site, callee.prop) : null;
}

function resolveOne(input: RepoResolveInput, file: string, site: CallSite): ResolvedCall | null {
  const callee = site.callee;
  switch (callee.kind) {
    case "ident": return resolveIdent(input, file, site, callee);
    case "member": return resolveMember(input, file, site, callee);
    case "new-member": return resolveNewMember(input, file, site, callee);
    case "this": return resolveThis(input, file, site, callee);
    case "this-field": return resolveThisField(input, file, site, callee);
    case "other": return resolveOther(input, site, callee);
  }
}
```

```ts
export function createCallResolver(input: RepoResolveInput): CallResolver {
  let reverse: Map<string, Array<{ from: SymbolRef; call: ResolvedCall }>> | null = null;

  function buildReverse(): Map<string, Array<{ from: SymbolRef; call: ResolvedCall }>> {
    const map = new Map<string, Array<{ from: SymbolRef; call: ResolvedCall }>>();
    for (const [file, fi] of input.index) {
      for (const site of fi.calls) {
        const rc = resolveOne(input, file, site);
        if (!rc) continue;
        const key = `${rc.target.file}#${rc.target.symbol}`;
        const from: SymbolRef = { repo: input.repo, file, symbol: site.caller };
        const arr = map.get(key) ?? [];
        arr.push({ from, call: rc });
        map.set(key, arr);
      }
    }
    return map;
  }

  return {
    resolve: (file, site) => resolveOne(input, file, site),
    callsFrom(ref) {
      const fi = input.index.get(ref.file);
      if (!fi) return [];
      const out: ResolvedCall[] = [];
      for (const site of fi.calls) {
        if (site.caller !== ref.symbol) continue;
        const rc = resolveOne(input, ref.file, site);
        if (rc) out.push(rc);
      }
      return out.sort((a, b) => a.site.line - b.site.line);
    },
    callersOf(ref) {
      reverse ??= buildReverse();
      return reverse.get(`${ref.file}#${ref.symbol}`) ?? [];
    },
    resolveExport(file, exportedName) {
      const hit = resolveExportIn(input.index, input.files, input.aliases, file, exportedName, 0);
      return hit ? { repo: input.repo, file: hit.file, symbol: hit.symbol } : null;
    },
    fileIndex: (file) => input.index.get(file),
  };
}
```

```ts
function stepKey(ref: SymbolRef): string {
  return `${ref.file}#${ref.symbol}`;
}

function walk(
  neighbors: (ref: SymbolRef) => Array<{ target: SymbolRef; call: ResolvedCall }>,
  start: SymbolRef,
  maxDepth: number,
): WalkStep[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>([stepKey(start)]);
  const first: WalkStep = { ref: start, depth: 0 };
  const steps: WalkStep[] = [first];
  const queue: WalkStep[] = [first];
  while (queue.length > 0) {
    const cur = queue.shift()!;
    if (cur.depth >= maxDepth) continue;
    for (const { target, call } of neighbors(cur.ref)) {
      const k = stepKey(target);
      if (seen.has(k)) continue;
      seen.add(k);
      const step: WalkStep = { ref: target, depth: cur.depth + 1, via: call, parent: cur.ref };
      steps.push(step);
      queue.push(step);
    }
  }
  return steps.sort((a, b) => a.depth - b.depth || compare(a.ref.file, b.ref.file) || compare(a.ref.symbol, b.ref.symbol));
}

export function walkCalls(resolver: CallResolver, start: SymbolRef, maxDepth = MAX_DEPTH): WalkStep[] {
  return walk((ref) => resolver.callsFrom(ref).map((call) => ({ target: call.target, call })), start, maxDepth);
}

export function walkCallers(resolver: CallResolver, start: SymbolRef, maxDepth = MAX_DEPTH): WalkStep[] {
  return walk((ref) => resolver.callersOf(ref).map(({ from, call }) => ({ target: from, call })), start, maxDepth);
}
```

### Task 8: Path segment model and matcher

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/cli/src/linker/paths.ts`
- Test: `packages/cli/test/paths.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Confidence`, `HttpMethod` (`store/schemas.ts`, added by Task 1); `compare` (`util.ts`).
- Produces (R8, exact names/shapes Task 9 and later tasks rely on):
  `Segment = {kind:"literal";value} | {kind:"param";name} | {kind:"splat";name}`,
  `parsePath(path: string): Segment[]`, `joinPaths(...parts: string[]): string`,
  `PathMatch { confidence: Confidence; literals: number; reason?: string }`,
  `matchPath(callPath: string, routePath: string): PathMatch | null`,
  `methodMatches(call: HttpMethod, route: HttpMethod): boolean`,
  `RouteCandidate<T> { item: T; method: HttpMethod; path: string; mountsKnown: boolean }`,
  `pickRoute<T>(method, callPath, candidates): { item: T; match: PathMatch } | null`.

**Design notes (binding, resolves ambiguity the registry leaves open):**
- `parsePath` works on already-normalised path text (as produced by `adapters/ast.ts`'s `normalizePath` /
  `fileRoute`, which already turns `[...x]`/`[[...x]]` into `:x*`). It additionally recognises the two other
  splat spellings frameworks write literally in route path strings: bare `*` and `(.*)` (both -> splat `"*"`).
- `matchPath`'s `confidence` is the worst-ranked per-segment outcome (`exact` < `param` < `guess`); `literals`
  counts only literal-segment-vs-literal-segment matches (specificity, used to rank candidates). A splat that
  consumes zero call segments contributes neither a literal nor a downgrade — "zero or more" really means a
  route can match a call that stops exactly where the splat begins.
- `reason` is set only for the `"guess"` case (`"call segment is a variable"`); `pickRoute` sets its own reasons
  (`"tie between equally specific routes"`, `"suffix match under unknown mount"`) when it overrides the match.
- `pickRoute`'s "call-param-vs-route-literal match is kept only when no other candidate exists" rule: among
  method-matching, `mountsKnown: true` candidates, if the single most-specific match has `confidence: "guess"`
  **and** more than one candidate matched at all, the pick is rejected (`null`) — an ambiguous guess among
  competing routes is not trustworthy. A lone guessing candidate is still returned.

```ts
import { joinPaths, matchPath, methodMatches, parsePath, pickRoute } from "../src/linker/paths.js";
import type { RouteCandidate } from "../src/linker/paths.js";

describe("parsePath", () => {

    ]);

  it("treats root as no segments and trims a trailing slash", () => {

    expect(joinPaths()).toBe("/");
    expect(joinPaths("", "")).toBe("/");
    expect(joinPaths("/api")).toBe("/api");
```

```ts
describe("matchPath", () => {
  it("literal = literal -> exact, full specificity", () => {
    expect(matchPath("/api/customers", "/api/customers")).toEqual({ confidence: "exact", literals: 2 });
  });

  it("a literal call segment against a route param/splat -> param", () => {
    expect(matchPath("/customers/42", "/customers/:id")).toEqual({ confidence: "param", literals: 1 });
    expect(matchPath("/files/a/b/c", "/files/:rest*")).toEqual({ confidence: "param", literals: 1 });
  });

  it("a splat matches zero segments too ([...x] zero-or-more)", () => {
    expect(matchPath("/files", "/files/:rest*")).toEqual({ confidence: "exact", literals: 1 });
  });

  it("a dynamic call segment against a route literal -> guess, with a reason", () => {
    expect(matchPath("/customers/:id", "/customers/active")).toEqual({
      confidence: "guess",
      literals: 1, // the leading "customers" segment still matches literal-for-literal
      reason: "call segment is a variable",
    });
  });

  it("two params in the same position both dynamic -> exact (no literal credit)", () => {
    expect(matchPath("/customers/:x", "/customers/:id")).toEqual({ confidence: "exact", literals: 1 });
  });

  it("returns null when segment counts cannot line up", () => {
    expect(matchPath("/api/customers", "/api/customers/extra")).toBeNull();
    expect(matchPath("/api/customers/extra", "/api/customers")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("methodMatches", () => {
  it("ANY on either side matches anything; otherwise methods must be equal", () => {
    expect(methodMatches("GET", "ANY")).toBe(true);
    expect(methodMatches("ANY", "POST")).toBe(true);
    expect(methodMatches("ANY", "ANY")).toBe(true);
    expect(methodMatches("GET", "POST")).toBe(false);
    expect(methodMatches("GET", "GET")).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
describe("pickRoute", () => {
  function cand(path: string, method: "GET" | "POST" | "ANY" = "GET", mountsKnown = true): RouteCandidate<string> {
    return { item: `${method} ${path}`, method, path, mountsKnown };
  }

  it("picks the most specific (most literal segments) candidate", () => {
    const candidates = [cand("/customers/:id"), cand("/customers/active")];
    const picked = pickRoute("GET", "/customers/active", candidates);
    expect(picked).toEqual({ item: "GET /customers/active", match: { confidence: "exact", literals: 2 } });
  });

  it("matches ANY-method routes against any call method", () => {
    const candidates = [cand("/health", "ANY")];
    const picked = pickRoute("GET", "/health", candidates);
    expect(picked).toEqual({ item: "GET /health", match: { confidence: "exact", literals: 1 } });
  });

  it("ties on specificity -> guess, winner is first by compare of path", () => {
    const candidates = [cand("/items/:id"), cand("/items/:slug")];
    const picked = pickRoute("GET", "/items/42", candidates);
    expect(picked).toEqual({
      item: "GET /items/:id",
      match: { confidence: "guess", literals: 1, reason: "tie between equally specific routes" },
    });
  });

  it("drops a call-param-vs-route-literal guess when a less-specific candidate also matched", () => {
    // "/customers/active" is the most specific (1 literal) but only a guess; "/:section/:id" also
    // matches (0 literals, confidence "param") so the guess is not the sole candidate and is dropped.
    const candidates = [cand("/customers/active"), cand("/:section/:id")];
    expect(pickRoute("GET", "/customers/:id", candidates)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("keeps a call-param-vs-route-literal guess when it is the only matching candidate", () => {
    const candidates = [cand("/customers/active")];
    expect(pickRoute("GET", "/customers/:id", candidates)).toEqual({
      item: "GET /customers/active",
      match: { confidence: "guess", literals: 1, reason: "call segment is a variable" },
    });
  });

  it("falls back to a suffix match under an unknown mount only when unique", () => {
    const one = [cand("/invoices/:id/pay", "POST", false)];
    expect(pickRoute("POST", "/billing/invoices/42/pay", one)).toEqual({
      item: "POST /invoices/:id/pay",
      match: { confidence: "guess", literals: 2, reason: "suffix match under unknown mount" },
    });

    // "/:prefix/pay" also matches the call's last 2 segments, so the suffix match is no longer unique.
    const two = [cand("/invoices/:id/pay", "POST", false), cand("/:prefix/pay", "POST", false)];
    expect(pickRoute("POST", "/billing/invoices/42/pay", two)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("prefers a known-mount match over an unknown-mount suffix match", () => {
    const candidates = [cand("/billing/invoices/:id/pay", "POST", true), cand("/invoices/:id/pay", "POST", false)];
    const picked = pickRoute("POST", "/billing/invoices/42/pay", candidates);
    expect(picked!.item).toBe("POST /billing/invoices/:id/pay");
  });

  it("returns null when nothing matches", () => {
    expect(pickRoute("GET", "/nope", [cand("/customers")])).toBeNull();
    expect(pickRoute("POST", "/customers", [cand("/customers", "GET")])).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`packages/cli/src/linker/paths.ts`:
```ts
import type { Confidence, HttpMethod } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";

export type Segment = { kind: "literal"; value: string } | { kind: "param"; name: string } | { kind: "splat"; name: string };

export function parsePath(path: string): Segment[] {
  const trimmed = path.length > 1 ? path.replace(/\/+$/, "") : path;
  const parts = trimmed.split("/").filter((p) => p.length > 0);
  return parts.map((p): Segment => {
    const namedSplat = p.match(/^:(.+)\*$/);
    if (namedSplat) return { kind: "splat", name: namedSplat[1]! };
    if (p === "*" || p === "(.*)") return { kind: "splat", name: "*" };
    const param = p.match(/^:(.+)$/);
    if (param) return { kind: "param", name: param[1]! };
    return { kind: "literal", value: p };
  });
}

export function joinPaths(...parts: string[]): string {
  const segs = parts
    .flatMap((p) => p.split("/"))
    .map((s) => s.trim())
    .filter((s) => s.length > 0);
  return segs.length === 0 ? "/" : `/${segs.join("/")}`;
}

export interface PathMatch {
  confidence: Confidence;
  literals: number;
  reason?: string;
}
```

```ts
function rank(c: Confidence): number {
  return c === "exact" ? 0 : c === "param" ? 1 : 2;
}
function worse(a: Confidence, b: Confidence): Confidence {
  return rank(a) >= rank(b) ? a : b;
}

interface SegMatch {
  confidence: Confidence;
  literals: number;
}

/** Recursive segment matcher: `route` must consume `call` exactly (a splat may consume zero or more
 * of the remaining call segments). literal=literal -> exact + 1 literal; call literal vs route
 * param/splat -> param; call param vs route literal -> guess; two dynamic segments -> exact, no
 * literal credit. Returns null when the shapes cannot line up (lengths mismatch with no splat to
 * absorb the difference, or a literal mismatch). */
function matchSegments(call: Segment[], route: Segment[]): SegMatch | null {
  if (route.length === 0) return call.length === 0 ? { confidence: "exact", literals: 0 } : null;
  const [rHead, ...rTail] = route as [Segment, ...Segment[]];
  if (rHead.kind === "splat") {
    let best: SegMatch | null = null;
    for (let consumed = 0; consumed <= call.length; consumed++) {
      const rest = matchSegments(call.slice(consumed), rTail);
      if (!rest) continue;
      const confidence = consumed > 0 ? worse(rest.confidence, "param") : rest.confidence;
      const combined: SegMatch = { confidence, literals: rest.literals };
      if (!best || combined.literals > best.literals || (combined.literals === best.literals && rank(combined.confidence) < rank(best.confidence))) {
        best = combined;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }
  if (call.length === 0) return null;
  const [cHead, ...cTail] = call as [Segment, ...Segment[]];
  const tail = matchSegments(cTail, rTail);
  if (!tail) return null;
  if (rHead.kind === "literal") {
    if (cHead.kind === "literal") {
      return cHead.value === rHead.value ? { confidence: tail.confidence, literals: tail.literals + 1 } : null;
    }
    return { confidence: worse(tail.confidence, "guess"), literals: tail.literals };
  }
  // rHead.kind === "param"
  if (cHead.kind === "literal") return { confidence: worse(tail.confidence, "param"), literals: tail.literals };
  return { confidence: tail.confidence, literals: tail.literals };
}
```

```ts
export function matchPath(callPath: string, routePath: string): PathMatch | null {
  const m = matchSegments(parsePath(callPath), parsePath(routePath));
  if (!m) return null;
  return m.confidence === "guess" ? { ...m, reason: "call segment is a variable" } : m;
}

/** Match `routePath` against the *tail* of `callPath` only (the call's leading segments are an
 * unknown mount prefix). Not supported when the route itself contains a splat (its own length
 * against the tail would be ambiguous). */
function matchSuffix(callPath: string, routePath: string): SegMatch | null {
  const call = parsePath(callPath);
  const route = parsePath(routePath);
  if (route.length === 0 || route.length > call.length) return null;
  if (route.some((s) => s.kind === "splat")) return null;
  return matchSegments(call.slice(call.length - route.length), route);
}

export function methodMatches(call: HttpMethod, route: HttpMethod): boolean {
  return call === "ANY" || route === "ANY" || call === route;
}

export interface RouteCandidate<T> {
  item: T;
  method: HttpMethod;
  path: string;
  mountsKnown: boolean;
}

export function pickRoute<T>(
  method: HttpMethod,
  callPath: string,
  candidates: RouteCandidate<T>[],
): { item: T; match: PathMatch } | null {
  type Scored = { candidate: RouteCandidate<T>; match: PathMatch };
  const known: Scored[] = [];
  const suffixOnly: Scored[] = [];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (!methodMatches(method, c.method)) continue;
    if (c.mountsKnown) {
      const m = matchPath(callPath, c.path);
      if (m) known.push({ candidate: c, match: m });
    } else {
      const m = matchSuffix(callPath, c.path);
      if (m) suffixOnly.push({ candidate: c, match: { confidence: m.confidence, literals: m.literals } });
    }
  }
  if (known.length > 0) {
    known.sort((a, b) => b.match.literals - a.match.literals || compare(a.candidate.path, b.candidate.path));
    const best = known[0]!;
    const tied = known.filter((k) => k.match.literals === best.match.literals);
    if (tied.length > 1) {
      return { item: best.candidate.item, match: { confidence: "guess", literals: best.match.literals, reason: "tie between equally specific routes" } };
    }
    if (best.match.confidence === "guess" && known.length > 1) return null;
    return { item: best.candidate.item, match: best.match };
  }
  if (suffixOnly.length === 1) {
    const only = suffixOnly[0]!;
    return { item: only.candidate.item, match: { confidence: "guess", literals: only.match.literals, reason: "suffix match under unknown mount" } };
  }
  return null;
}
```

### Task 9: Mount resolution and effective paths

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/cli/src/linker/mounts.ts`
- Test: `packages/cli/test/mounts.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Route`, `Mount`, `EndpointsFile` (`store/schemas.ts`, R1, added by Task 1 — fields used here:
  `Route.{method,path,at:{file,line,symbol},framework,router?}`, `Mount.{prefix,kind,on,target:{name?,file?},at}`,
  `EndpointsFile.{repo,routes,mounts}`); `joinPaths` (R8, `linker/paths.ts`, Task 8); `compare` (`util.ts`).
  The integration test additionally uses `matchPath`, `methodMatches`, `pickRoute`, `RouteCandidate` (R8) to show
  `mountsKnown` feeding the suffix-guess rule end to end.
- Produces (R9, exact names/shapes Task 10/11/12 rely on):
  `EffectiveRoute { repo; route: Route; effectivePath: string; mountsKnown: boolean; mounts: Mount[] }`,
  `effectiveRoutes(endpoints: EndpointsFile): EffectiveRoute[]`,
  `routeKey(r: EffectiveRoute): string` — `` `${repo}:${METHOD} ${effectivePath}` `` (memo (g)).

**Design notes (binding, resolves ambiguity the registry leaves open):**
- A route belongs to a mount when either (a) same file, same receiver name: `mount.at.file === route.at.file &&
  mount.target.name === route.router`, or (b) the mount's target was resolved (by Task 3's `finalizeEndpoints`)
  to the exact file the route lives in: `mount.target.file === route.at.file`. Case (b) needs no name check —
  codeloom already proved the mount's import specifier points at this file.
- Multi-level composition: a matched mount's own `on` receiver (`"app"`, `"router"`, an inner router var, …) is
  looked up the same way, one level further out, cycle-guarded by `` `${file}#${router}` `` and capped at 5
  levels past the route's own mount. `on === ""` (nest-global's placeholder) or `on` being one of the
  conventional top-level receivers (`"app"`, `"server"`, `"fastify"`) ends the chain with no further prefix.
- `nest-global` mounts are not looked up per-route: every route with `framework === "nest"` in the repo gets the
  (at most one, first by declaration order in `endpoints.mounts`) `nest-global` prefix applied, unconditionally.
- `mountsKnown` is `false` only when a non-nest route declares a `router` that is *not* one of the top-level
  receiver names, the repo has at least one non-nest-global mount recorded (so mounting is clearly this repo's
  convention), and still no mount chain was found for it — i.e. a sub-router that is presumably mounted
  somewhere codeloom could not see (dynamic `require`, re-exported through an untracked indirection, etc.).
  Everything else (top-level receivers, or a repo that records no mounts at all) is `mountsKnown: true` with an
  empty `mounts: []` and `effectivePath === route.path`.
- Several mounts matching the same router (e.g. mounted at two different prefixes) produce one `EffectiveRoute`
  per resulting prefix, sorted by `effectivePath` (via `compare`).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { effectiveRoutes, routeKey } from "../src/linker/mounts.js";
import { pickRoute } from "../src/linker/paths.js";
import type { RouteCandidate } from "../src/linker/paths.js";
import type { EndpointsFile, HttpMethod, Loc, Mount, MountKind, Route } from "../src/store/schemas.js";

function loc(file: string, line: number, symbol: string): Loc {
  return { file, line, symbol };
}
function route(method: HttpMethod, path: string, file: string, framework: Route["framework"], router?: string): Route {
  return { method, path, handler: "handler", inline: false, framework, at: loc(file, 1, "handler"), ...(router ? { router } : {}) };
}
function mount(prefix: string, kind: MountKind, on: string, target: { name?: string; file?: string }, file: string): Mount {
  return { prefix, kind, on, target, at: loc(file, 1, "<module>") };
}
function endpoints(repo: string, routes: Route[], mounts: Mount[]): EndpointsFile {
  return { version: 2, repo, pages: [], httpCalls: [], httpClients: [], routes, mounts, dbAccess: [], tables: [], models: [], queues: [] };
}
```

```ts
describe("unknown-mount routes are only matched by callers via a unique path suffix", () => {
  it("feeds mountsKnown: false into pickRoute's suffix-guess rule (Task 8)", () => {
    const r = route("POST", "/invoices/:id/pay", "billing-routes.ts", "express", "billingRouter");
    const unrelated = mount("/other", "express-use", "app", { file: "other-routes.ts" }, "server.ts");
    const [eff] = effectiveRoutes(endpoints("billing", [r], [unrelated]));
    expect(eff!.mountsKnown).toBe(false);

    type Item = typeof eff;
    const one: RouteCandidate<Item>[] = [{ item: eff, method: eff!.route.method, path: eff!.effectivePath, mountsKnown: eff!.mountsKnown }];
    expect(pickRoute("POST", "/billing/invoices/42/pay", one)).toEqual({
      item: eff,
      match: { confidence: "guess", literals: 2, reason: "suffix match under unknown mount" },
    });

    // a second, independently-unmounted route with the same effective path makes the suffix ambiguous.
    const r2 = route("POST", "/invoices/:id/pay", "legacy-routes.ts", "express", "legacyRouter");
    const [eff2] = effectiveRoutes(endpoints("billing", [r2], [unrelated]));
    const two: RouteCandidate<Item>[] = [
      { item: eff, method: eff!.route.method, path: eff!.effectivePath, mountsKnown: eff!.mountsKnown },
      { item: eff2, method: eff2!.route.method, path: eff2!.effectivePath, mountsKnown: eff2!.mountsKnown },
    ];
    expect(pickRoute("POST", "/billing/invoices/42/pay", two)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`packages/cli/src/linker/mounts.ts`:
```ts
import { joinPaths } from "./paths.js";
import type { EndpointsFile, Mount, Route } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";

export interface EffectiveRoute {
  repo: string;
  route: Route;
  effectivePath: string;
  mountsKnown: boolean;
  mounts: Mount[];
}

const TOP_LEVEL_RECEIVERS = new Set(["app", "server", "fastify"]);
const MAX_MOUNT_LEVELS = 5;

function isTopLevelReceiver(name: string): boolean {
  return TOP_LEVEL_RECEIVERS.has(name);
}

/** Mounts (excluding nest-global) that place `routerName`, declared in `file`, under a prefix. */
function mountsFor(mounts: Mount[], file: string, routerName: string): Mount[] {
  return mounts.filter((m) => {
    if (m.kind === "nest-global") return false;
    if (m.target.file !== undefined) return m.target.file === file;
    return m.at.file === file && m.target.name === routerName;
  });
}

interface Chain {
  prefix: string;
  mounts: Mount[];
}

/** All distinct outward prefix chains that apply to `routerName` declared in `file`, outermost mount
 * first in each chain's `mounts`. Cycle-guarded by `${file}#${routerName}`, capped at MAX_MOUNT_LEVELS. */
function resolveChains(mounts: Mount[], file: string, routerName: string, visited: Set<string>, depth: number): Chain[] {
  if (depth > MAX_MOUNT_LEVELS) return [];
  const key = `${file}#${routerName}`;
  if (visited.has(key)) return [];
  const seen = new Set(visited);
  seen.add(key);
  const matches = mountsFor(mounts, file, routerName);
  const out: Chain[] = [];
  for (const m of matches) {
    const outer = isTopLevelReceiver(m.on) || m.on === "" ? [] : resolveChains(mounts, m.at.file, m.on, seen, depth + 1);
    const bases: Chain[] = outer.length > 0 ? outer : [{ prefix: "", mounts: [] }];
    for (const base of bases) out.push({ prefix: joinPaths(base.prefix, m.prefix), mounts: [...base.mounts, m] });
  }
  return out;
}
```

```ts
export function effectiveRoutes(endpoints: EndpointsFile): EffectiveRoute[] {
  const nonNestMounts = endpoints.mounts.filter((m) => m.kind !== "nest-global");
  const nestGlobal = endpoints.mounts.find((m) => m.kind === "nest-global");
  const out: EffectiveRoute[] = [];

  for (const route of endpoints.routes) {
    if (route.framework === "nest") {
      out.push({
        repo: endpoints.repo,
        route,
        effectivePath: nestGlobal ? joinPaths(nestGlobal.prefix, route.path) : route.path,
        mountsKnown: true,
        mounts: nestGlobal ? [nestGlobal] : [],
      });
      continue;
    }
    const chains = route.router === undefined ? [] : resolveChains(nonNestMounts, route.at.file, route.router, new Set(), 0);
    if (chains.length === 0) {
      const known = nonNestMounts.length === 0 || route.router === undefined || isTopLevelReceiver(route.router);
      out.push({ repo: endpoints.repo, route, effectivePath: route.path, mountsKnown: known, mounts: [] });
      continue;
    }
    const sorted = [...chains].sort((a, b) => compare(a.prefix, b.prefix));
    for (const chain of sorted) {
      out.push({ repo: endpoints.repo, route, effectivePath: joinPaths(chain.prefix, route.path), mountsKnown: true, mounts: chain.mounts });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export function routeKey(r: EffectiveRoute): string {
  return `${r.repo}:${r.route.method} ${r.effectivePath}`;
}
```

**Note:** the page-symbol lookup here and the inline-handler lookup in Task 11 need a file's `FileCodeIndex`
for `symbolAt`; they get it from `CallResolver.fileIndex(file)`, defined and tested in Task 7.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: R5 `WorkspaceData`, `RepoData` (`store/read.ts`); R6 `symbolAt`, `FileCodeIndex`, `CodeSymbol`
  (`linker/code-index.ts`); R7 `CallResolver` (incl. `fileIndex`), `createCallResolver`, `walkCallers`, `WalkStep`, `SymbolRef`
  (`linker/resolve-calls.ts`); R8 `pickRoute`, `RouteCandidate`, `joinPaths` (`linker/paths.ts`); R9
  `effectiveRoutes`, `EffectiveRoute` (`linker/mounts.ts`); R1 `HttpCall`, `HttpClient`, `Confidence`.
- Produces (exact, from the registry):
  ```ts
  export interface LinkInput { ws: WorkspaceData; resolvers: Map<string, CallResolver> }
  export interface PageReach { page: UiPage; path: SymbolRef[] }
  export interface UiLink {
    repo: string; call: HttpCall; pages: PageReach[];
    target:
      | { kind: "route"; route: EffectiveRoute; confidence: Confidence; reason?: string }
      | { kind: "external"; base: string }
      | { kind: "unresolved"; reason: string };
  }
  export function linkUi(input: LinkInput): UiLink[];
  export function chooseBackend(call: HttpCall, clients: HttpClient[], backends: RepoData[]): { repos: RepoData[]; hinted: boolean };
  export function callPathWithClient(call: HttpCall, clients: HttpClient[]): string;
  ```
  Also exports (private to the module but needed by Task 11, which appends to this same file):
  `stepKey(ref: SymbolRef): string`, `chainTowardStart(steps: WalkStep[], targetKey: string, startKey: string): WalkStep[]`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { KnowledgeGraph } from "@codeloom/core";
import { buildFileCodeIndex } from "../src/linker/code-index.js";
import type { RepoCodeIndex } from "../src/linker/code-index.js";
import { createCallResolver } from "../src/linker/resolve-calls.js";
import type { CallResolver } from "../src/linker/resolve-calls.js";
import { callPathWithClient, chooseBackend, linkUi } from "../src/linker/link.js";
import type { RepoData, WorkspaceData } from "../src/store/read.js";
import type { EndpointsFile, HttpCall, HttpClient } from "../src/store/schemas.js";
import { parsed } from "./adapter-helpers.js";

function graphStub(): KnowledgeGraph {
  return {
    version: "1",
    project: { name: "x", languages: [], frameworks: [], description: "", analyzedAt: "", gitCommitHash: "" },
    nodes: [], edges: [], layers: [], tour: [],
  };
}

function endpoints(repo: string, partial: Partial<EndpointsFile>): EndpointsFile {
  return {
    version: 2, repo, pages: [], httpCalls: [], httpClients: [], routes: [], mounts: [],
    dbAccess: [], tables: [], models: [], queues: [], ...partial,
  };
}

function repo(id: string, ep: Partial<EndpointsFile>, opts?: { role?: "ui" | "api" | "worker" | "lib"; apiBase?: string[] }): RepoData {
  return { ref: { id, path: id, ...opts }, abs: `/ws/${id}`, graph: graphStub(), endpoints: endpoints(id, ep) };
}

function index(files: Record<string, string>): RepoCodeIndex {
  const idx: RepoCodeIndex = new Map();
  for (const [rel, code] of Object.entries(files)) idx.set(rel, buildFileCodeIndex(parsed(rel, code)));
  return idx;
}

function resolverFor(files: Record<string, string>, repoId: string): CallResolver {
  const idx = index(files);
  return createCallResolver({ repo: repoId, index: idx, files: new Set(idx.keys()), aliases: [] });
}

function ws(repos: RepoData[]): WorkspaceData {
  return { root: "/ws", name: "ws", repos, links: [] };
}

describe("callPathWithClient", () => {
  it("returns the call's own literal path when the client has no literal baseURL", () => {
    const call: HttpCall = { method: "POST", path: "/billing/invoices", raw: '"/billing/invoices"', resolved: true, client: "billingApi", at: { file: "lib/billing.ts", line: 3, symbol: "createInvoice" }, kind: "literal" };
    const clients: HttpClient[] = [{ name: "billingApi", file: "lib/billing-api.ts", line: 1, factory: "axios", baseExpr: "process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BILLING_URL" }];
    expect(callPathWithClient(call, clients)).toBe("/billing/invoices");
  });

  it("prepends a known client baseLiteral", () => {
    const call: HttpCall = { method: "GET", path: "/widgets", raw: '"/widgets"', resolved: true, client: "api", at: { file: "lib/x.ts", line: 1, symbol: "f" }, kind: "literal" };
    const clients: HttpClient[] = [{ name: "api", file: "lib/api.ts", line: 1, factory: "axios", baseLiteral: "/v2" }];
    expect(callPathWithClient(call, clients)).toBe("/v2/widgets");
  });

  it('returns "" for an unknown-kind call', () => {
    const call: HttpCall = { method: "GET", path: "", raw: "url", resolved: false, client: "api", at: { file: "a.ts", line: 1, symbol: "f" }, kind: "unknown" };
    expect(callPathWithClient(call, [])).toBe("");
  });
});

describe("chooseBackend", () => {
  it("returns the only candidate untouched when there is just one", () => {
    const backends = [repo("api", {})];
    const call: HttpCall = { method: "POST", path: "/x", raw: "x", resolved: true, client: "api", at: { file: "a.ts", line: 1, symbol: "f" }, kind: "literal" };
    expect(chooseBackend(call, [], backends)).toEqual({ repos: backends, hinted: false });

    expect(chooseBackend(call, [], [api, billing])).toEqual({ repos: [billing], hinted: true });

});

describe("linkUi", () => {
  const webFiles = {
    "lib/api.ts": `import axios from "axios";\nexport const api = axios.create({ baseURL: process.env.X });\n`,
    "lib/use-create-customer.ts": [
      'import { api } from "./api.js";',
      "export function useCreateCustomer() {",
      "  return async (input: unknown) => {",
      '    const res = await api.post("/api/customers", input);',

      "export default function NewCustomerPage() {",

  };
```
