```ts
      method: "POST", path: "/api/customers", raw: '"/api/customers"', resolved: true, client: "api",
      at: { file: "lib/use-create-customer.ts", line: 4, symbol: "useCreateCustomer" }, kind: "literal",
    };
    const web = repo("web", {
      pages: [{ route: "/customers/new", file: "app/customers/new/page.tsx", line: 2, framework: "next-app" }],
      httpCalls: [call],
      httpClients: [{ name: "api", file: "lib/api.ts", line: 2, factory: "axios", baseExpr: "process.env.X" }],
    });
    const api = repo("api", { routes: apiRoutes });
    const resolvers = new Map([
      ["web", resolverFor(webFiles, "web")],
      ["api", resolverFor({}, "api")],
    ]);
    return { call, web, api, resolvers };
  }

  it("walks page -> hook -> call, and matches the call to the route", () => {
    const route: EndpointsFile["routes"][number] = {

      framework: "adonis", at: { file: "app/controllers/customers_controller.ts", line: 5, symbol: "CustomersController.store" },
    };

    const links = linkUi({ ws: ws([web, api]), resolvers });
    expect(links).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(links[0]).toMatchObject({
      repo: "web",
      call,
      pages: [{
        page: { route: "/customers/new" },
        path: [
          { repo: "web", file: "app/customers/new/page.tsx", symbol: "NewCustomerPage" },
          { repo: "web", file: "lib/use-create-customer.ts", symbol: "useCreateCustomer" },
        ],
      }],
      target: { kind: "route", confidence: "exact" },
    });
    expect(links[0]!.target.kind === "route" && links[0]!.target.route.effectivePath).toBe("/api/customers");
  });

  it("reports an unresolved call when no route matches", () => {
    const { web, api, resolvers } = webAndApi([]);
    const links = linkUi({ ws: ws([web, api]), resolvers });
    expect(links[0]!.target).toEqual({ kind: "unresolved", reason: "no matching route" });
  });

  it("reports unresolved with a clear reason for an unknown-kind URL", () => {
    const call: HttpCall = {
      method: "GET", path: "", raw: "someVar", resolved: false, client: "fetch",
      at: { file: "lib/x.ts", line: 1, symbol: "f" }, kind: "unknown",
    };
    const web = repo("web", { httpCalls: [call] });
    const links = linkUi({ ws: ws([web]), resolvers: new Map([["web", resolverFor({}, "web")]]) });
    expect(links[0]!.target).toEqual({ kind: "unresolved", reason: "url has no literal text" });
  });

  it("treats a hardcoded absolute foreign URL as external", () => {
    const call: HttpCall = {
      method: "POST", path: "/v1/charges", raw: '"https://api.stripe.com/v1/charges"', resolved: true,
      client: "axios", at: { file: "lib/pay.ts", line: 1, symbol: "pay" }, kind: "literal",
    };
    const web = repo("web", { httpCalls: [call] });
    const links = linkUi({ ws: ws([web]), resolvers: new Map([["web", resolverFor({}, "web")]]) });
    expect(links[0]!.target).toEqual({ kind: "external", base: "api.stripe.com" });
  });

  it("downgrades confidence to guess when several backends match and none is hinted", () => {
    const dupRoute: EndpointsFile["routes"][number] = {
      method: "POST", path: "/dup", handler: "f", inline: false, framework: "express",

      method: "POST", path: "/dup", raw: '"/dup"', resolved: true, client: "fetch",
      at: { file: "lib/x.ts", line: 1, symbol: "f" }, kind: "literal",
    };
    const a = repo("a", { routes: [dupRoute] });
    const b = repo("b", { routes: [dupRoute] });
    const web = repo("web", { httpCalls: [call] });
    const resolvers = new Map([["web", resolverFor({}, "web")], ["a", resolverFor({}, "a")], ["b", resolverFor({}, "b")]]);
    const links = linkUi({ ws: ws([web, a, b]), resolvers });
    expect(links[0]!.target).toMatchObject({ kind: "route", confidence: "guess" });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { symbolAt } from "./code-index.js";
import type { FileCodeIndex } from "./code-index.js";
import type { CallResolver, SymbolRef, WalkStep } from "./resolve-calls.js";
import { walkCallers } from "./resolve-calls.js";
import { joinPaths, pickRoute } from "./paths.js";
import type { RouteCandidate } from "./paths.js";
import { effectiveRoutes } from "./mounts.js";
import type { EffectiveRoute } from "./mounts.js";
import type { RepoData, WorkspaceData } from "../store/read.js";
import type { Confidence, HttpCall, HttpClient, UiPage } from "../store/schemas.js";

export interface LinkInput {
  ws: WorkspaceData;
  /** repo id → resolver (built by runLinker from IndexResult codeIndex/files/aliases). */
  resolvers: Map<string, CallResolver>;
}

export interface PageReach {
  page: UiPage;
  path: SymbolRef[];
}

export interface UiLink {
  repo: string;
  call: HttpCall;
  pages: PageReach[];
  target:
    | { kind: "route"; route: EffectiveRoute; confidence: Confidence; reason?: string }
    | { kind: "external"; base: string }
    | { kind: "unresolved"; reason: string };
}

/** Plain `repo:file#symbol` key for comparing `SymbolRef`s. */
export function stepKey(ref: SymbolRef): string {
  return `${ref.repo}:${ref.file}#${ref.symbol}`;
}

/**
 * Chase `WalkStep.parent` pointers from `steps`'s entry for `targetKey` back towards `startKey`,
 * collecting steps target-first. Shared by the UI reverse walk (Task 10, no further reversal needed:
 * target = page, start = call site, and "target-first" is exactly "page, ..., call") and the backend
 * forward walk (Task 11, which reverses the result to get "handler, ..., store-access" order).
 */
export function chainTowardStart(steps: WalkStep[], targetKey: string, startKey: string): WalkStep[] {
  const byKey = new Map(steps.map((s) => [stepKey(s.ref), s] as const));
  const chain: WalkStep[] = [];
  let cur = byKey.get(targetKey);
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  while (cur) {
    chain.push(cur);
    const key = stepKey(cur.ref);
    if (key === startKey || seen.has(key)) break;
    seen.add(key);
    cur = cur.parent ? byKey.get(stepKey(cur.parent)) : undefined;
  }
  return chain;
}
```

```ts
/** "CodeSymbol whose lines include page.line, else the file's default export." */
function pageSymbolRef(input: LinkInput, repoId: string, page: UiPage): SymbolRef | undefined {
  const idx: FileCodeIndex | undefined = input.resolvers.get(repoId)?.fileIndex(page.file);
  if (!idx) return undefined;
  const bySite = symbolAt(idx, page.line);
  if (bySite) return { repo: repoId, file: page.file, symbol: bySite.name };
  const def = idx.symbols.find((s) => s.exportedAs === "default");
  return def ? { repo: repoId, file: page.file, symbol: def.name } : undefined;
}

/** Every UiPage that reaches `call` by a reverse walk over resolved calls, ≤6 hops. */
function reachingPages(input: LinkInput, repoId: string, call: HttpCall): PageReach[] {
  const resolver = input.resolvers.get(repoId);
  const repo = input.ws.repos.find((r) => r.ref.id === repoId);
  if (!resolver || !repo) return [];
  const start: SymbolRef = { repo: repoId, file: call.at.file, symbol: call.at.symbol };
  const startKey = stepKey(start);
  // walkCallers always includes `start` itself at depth 0, so byKey.get(startKey) is never undefined.
  const steps = walkCallers(resolver, start, 6);
  const byKey = new Map(steps.map((s) => [stepKey(s.ref), s] as const));
  const reaches: PageReach[] = [];
  for (const page of repo.endpoints.pages) {
    const pageRef = pageSymbolRef(input, repoId, page);
    if (!pageRef || !byKey.has(stepKey(pageRef))) continue;
    const path = chainTowardStart(steps, stepKey(pageRef), startKey);
    reaches.push({ page, path: path.map((s) => s.ref) });
  }
  reaches.sort((a, b) => (a.page.file === b.page.file ? a.page.line - b.page.line : a.page.file < b.page.file ? -1 : 1));
  return reaches;
}

const ABSOLUTE_URL_RE = /^[`'"]?https?:\/\//i;

/** A hardcoded absolute foreign URL (no stripped `base` expression — that only happens for a pure
 * literal, which is exactly the "someone pasted a real host" case) is treated as external, never a
 * workspace backend. */
function isExternalAbsolute(call: HttpCall): boolean {
  return call.base === undefined && ABSOLUTE_URL_RE.test(call.raw);
}

function externalHost(call: HttpCall): string {
  const m = call.raw.match(/https?:\/\/([^/'"`]+)/i);
  return m ? m[1]! : call.raw;
}
```

```ts
const ENV_VAR_RE = /process\.env\.(\w+)/;

/** Text of whatever base expression a call carries, or its client's, to match against `apiBase` hints. */
function baseHintText(call: HttpCall, clients: HttpClient[]): string {
  if (call.base) return call.base;
  const client = clients.find((c) => c.name === call.client);
  return client?.baseExpr ?? "";
}

/** Backend choice for a call (memo (c)): all repos with routes; unique match wins; several → prefer
 * repos whose apiBase includes the env var / host in call.base or the client's baseExpr, then role
 * "api"; else guess (every candidate is returned, unhinted). */
export function chooseBackend(
  call: HttpCall, clients: HttpClient[], backends: RepoData[],
): { repos: RepoData[]; hinted: boolean } {
  if (backends.length <= 1) return { repos: backends, hinted: false };
  const hintText = baseHintText(call, clients);
  const envVar = hintText.match(ENV_VAR_RE)?.[1];
  const byApiBase = backends.filter((b) => {
    const bases = b.ref.apiBase ?? [];
    return bases.some((h) => h === envVar || (hintText && hintText.includes(h)));
  });
  if (byApiBase.length === 1) return { repos: byApiBase, hinted: true };
  const byRole = backends.filter((b) => b.ref.role === "api");
  if (byRole.length === 1) return { repos: byRole, hinted: true };
  return { repos: backends, hinted: false };
}

/** Call path after a known axios/ky instance's literal baseURL is prepended. */
export function callPathWithClient(call: HttpCall, clients: HttpClient[]): string {
  if (call.kind === "unknown") return "";
  const client = clients.find((c) => c.name === call.client);
  return client?.baseLiteral ? joinPaths(client.baseLiteral, call.path) : call.path;
}
```

```ts
function resolveTarget(
  call: HttpCall, clients: HttpClient[], allBackends: RepoData[], effectiveByRepo: Map<string, EffectiveRoute[]>,
): UiLink["target"] {
  if (call.kind === "unknown") return { kind: "unresolved", reason: "url has no literal text" };
  if (isExternalAbsolute(call)) return { kind: "external", base: externalHost(call) };
  if (allBackends.length === 0) return { kind: "unresolved", reason: "no matching route" };
  const path = callPathWithClient(call, clients);
  // chooseBackend only narrows the candidate pool (e.g. to avoid matching the wrong repo's same-named
  // route); it is NOT itself a confidence signal. pickRoute already produces "guess" on a genuine tie
  // (two backends whose best candidate is equally specific), which is the only real ambiguity — a
  // repo set that merely wasn't *hinted* is not ambiguous when the paths themselves don't collide
  // (demo-shop's "api" and "billing" never share a route, so every call there resolves "exact").
  const { repos: backends } = chooseBackend(call, clients, allBackends);
  const candidates: RouteCandidate<EffectiveRoute>[] = backends.flatMap((b) =>
    (effectiveByRepo.get(b.ref.id) ?? []).map((er) => ({
      item: er, method: er.route.method, path: er.effectivePath, mountsKnown: er.mountsKnown,
    })),
  );
  const picked = pickRoute(call.method, path, candidates);
  if (!picked) return { kind: "unresolved", reason: "no matching route" };
  return { kind: "route", route: picked.item, confidence: picked.match.confidence, reason: picked.match.reason };
}

/** For each HttpCall: the UI pages that reach it, and the route (or external/unresolved) it targets. */
export function linkUi(input: LinkInput): UiLink[] {
  const allBackends = input.ws.repos.filter((r) => r.endpoints.routes.length > 0);
  const effectiveByRepo = new Map(input.ws.repos.map((r) => [r.ref.id, effectiveRoutes(r.endpoints)]));
  const links: UiLink[] = [];
  for (const repo of input.ws.repos) {
    for (const call of repo.endpoints.httpCalls) {
      links.push({
        repo: repo.ref.id,
        call,
        pages: reachingPages(input, repo.ref.id, call),
        target: resolveTarget(call, repo.endpoints.httpClients, allBackends, effectiveByRepo),
      });
    }
  }
  links.sort((a, b) => (a.repo === b.repo ? (a.call.at.file === b.call.at.file ? a.call.at.line - b.call.at.line : a.call.at.file < b.call.at.file ? -1 : 1) : a.repo < b.repo ? -1 : 1));
  return links;
}
```

### Task 11: Link backend — route → handler → store/queue; consumers; manual links

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/linker/link.ts` (append the backend half: `findHandler`, `linkBackend`, `pairQueues`,
  `linkConsumers`, `parseLinkRef`, `resolveManualLinks`, `linkAll`; reuses `stepKey`/`chainTowardStart` from
  Task 10's half of the same file)
- Test: `packages/cli/test/link-backend.test.ts`

**Registry fix (binding for this task):** R11's `linkConsumers` must reach a queue consumer's actual message
handler and walk its calls (the fixture's `channel.consume("emails", handleEmail)` → `handleEmail` does the
`prisma.emailLog.create`). But `QueueOp` (R4, already written) only records `{ queue, op, at, exchange?,
routingKey? }` for the `consume(...)` call site itself — `at.symbol` is the *enclosing function* of the
`consume()` call (e.g. `startNotifier`), not the callback passed as its second argument, and nothing in
`CodeIndex` (R6) records a function passed *by reference* (as opposed to *called*) as an argument — `CallSite`
only exists for things that are actually invoked. So `walkCalls` from `at.symbol` can never reach `handleEmail`:
it is never textually called inside `startNotifier`, only passed as a value. Fix applied, scoped entirely to
this task's own file: `linkConsumers` reads the consume call's own source line directly (the repo's absolute
path is already on `RepoData.abs`), finds the `CallExpression` whose callee ends in `.consume` at that line, and
reads its second argument when it is a plain identifier. It then resolves that identifier to a `SymbolRef` by
building one ad hoc `CallSite` (`{ caller: at.symbol, line: at.line, callee: { kind: "ident", name } }`) and
handing it to `resolver.resolve(file, site)` — reusing R7's existing identifier-resolution rules (local /
import / barrel) rather than adding a new capability to the resolver. When the callback is an inline arrow
(the existing `invoice.created` consumer), no identifier is found and `linkConsumers` falls back to `at.symbol`
exactly as the registry describes, which is correct there (the inline callback only acks the message).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: R10 `LinkInput`, `stepKey`, `chainTowardStart` (`linker/link.ts`, this same file); R7 `walkCalls`,
  `WalkStep`, `SymbolRef`, `CallResolver.fileIndex` (R7), `CallSite`; R9 `EffectiveRoute`,
  `effectiveRoutes`; R6 `symbolAt`, `FileCodeIndex`; R1 `DbAccess`, `QueueOp`, `Confidence`, `Loc`, `HttpMethod`;
  R5 `WorkspaceData`, `RepoData`; `compare` (`util.ts`); `parseSource`, `walk`, `lineOf` (`adapters/ast.ts`).
- Produces (exact, from the registry):
  ```ts
  export type StoreHit =
    | { kind: "db"; access: DbAccess; viaPath: WalkStep[] }
    | { kind: "queue"; op: QueueOp; viaPath: WalkStep[] };
  export interface QueuePair { publish: QueueOp; consume: QueueOp; repoFrom: string; repoTo: string; confidence: Confidence; reason?: string }
  export interface BackendLink {
    repo: string; route: EffectiveRoute; handler: SymbolRef | null; handlerConfidence: Confidence;
    hits: StoreHit[]; unresolved: Array<{ at: Loc; reason: string }>;
  }
  export interface ConsumerLink { repo: string; consume: QueueOp; handler: SymbolRef; hits: StoreHit[]; unresolved: Array<{ at: Loc; reason: string }> }
  export interface ManualLink { from: string; to: string; resolved: boolean; reason?: string }
  export function findHandler(input: LinkInput, r: EffectiveRoute): { ref: SymbolRef; confidence: Confidence } | null;
  export function linkBackend(input: LinkInput): BackendLink[];
  export function pairQueues(ws: WorkspaceData): QueuePair[];
  export function linkConsumers(input: LinkInput): ConsumerLink[];
  export type LinkRef =
    | { kind: "symbol"; repo: string; file: string; symbol: string }
    | { kind: "route"; repo: string; method: HttpMethod; path: string }
    | { kind: "queue"; repo: string; queue: string };
  export function parseLinkRef(s: string): LinkRef | null;
  export function resolveManualLinks(input: LinkInput): ManualLink[];
  export interface LinkResult { ui: UiLink[]; backend: BackendLink[]; consumers: ConsumerLink[]; queuePairs: QueuePair[]; manual: ManualLink[] }
  export function linkAll(input: LinkInput): LinkResult;
  ```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { KnowledgeGraph } from "@codeloom/core";
import { buildFileCodeIndex } from "../src/linker/code-index.js";
import type { RepoCodeIndex } from "../src/linker/code-index.js";
import { createCallResolver } from "../src/linker/resolve-calls.js";
import type { CallResolver } from "../src/linker/resolve-calls.js";
import {
  findHandler, linkAll, linkBackend, linkConsumers, pairQueues, parseLinkRef, resolveManualLinks,
} from "../src/linker/link.js";
import { effectiveRoutes } from "../src/linker/mounts.js";
import type { RepoData, WorkspaceData } from "../src/store/read.js";
import type { EndpointsFile, Route } from "../src/store/schemas.js";
import { parsed } from "./adapter-helpers.js";

function graphStub(): KnowledgeGraph {
  return {
    version: "1",
    project: { name: "x", languages: [], frameworks: [], description: "", analyzedAt: "", gitCommitHash: "" },
    nodes: [], edges: [], layers: [], tour: [],
  };
}

function endpoints(repo: string, partial: Partial<EndpointsFile>): EndpointsFile {
  return {
    version: 2, repo, pages: [], httpCalls: [], httpClients: [], routes: [], mounts: [],
    dbAccess: [], tables: [], models: [], queues: [], ...partial,
  };
}

function index(files: Record<string, string>): RepoCodeIndex {
  const idx: RepoCodeIndex = new Map();
  for (const [rel, code] of Object.entries(files)) idx.set(rel, buildFileCodeIndex(parsed(rel, code)));
  return idx;
}

function repoWith(id: string, files: Record<string, string>, ep: Partial<EndpointsFile>, abs?: string): { data: RepoData; resolver: CallResolver } {
  const idx = index(files);
  return {
    data: { ref: { id, path: id }, abs: abs ?? `/ws/${id}`, graph: graphStub(), endpoints: endpoints(id, ep) },
    resolver: createCallResolver({ repo: id, index: idx, files: new Set(idx.keys()), aliases: [] }),
  };
}

function ws(repos: RepoData[], links: WorkspaceData["links"] = []): WorkspaceData {
  return { root: "/ws", name: "ws", repos, links };
}

  it("resolves a non-inline handler straight from route.handler", () => {

      "app/controllers/customers_controller.ts": "export default class CustomersController { async store() { return 1; } }",
    }, {});

      method: "POST", path: "/api/customers", handler: "CustomersController.store", inline: false,
      framework: "adonis", at: { file: "start/routes.ts", line: 1, symbol: "<module>" },
      handlerFile: "app/controllers/customers_controller.ts",
    };
    const er = effectiveRoutes(endpoints("api", { routes: [route] }))[0]!;
    const found = findHandler({ ws: ws([data]), resolvers: new Map([["api", resolver]]) }, er);
    expect(found).toEqual({ ref: { repo: "api", file: "app/controllers/customers_controller.ts", symbol: "CustomersController.store" }, confidence: "exact" });
  });

    }, {});

      method: "ANY", path: "/api/widgets", handler: "default", inline: false, framework: "next-api",

    };

    expect(found).toEqual({ ref: { repo: "api", file: "pages/api/widgets.ts", symbol: "handler" }, confidence: "exact" });

});

describe("pairQueues", () => {

    const billing = repoWith("billing", {}, { queues: [{ queue: "invoice.created", op: "publish", at: { file: "src/invoices.ts", line: 1, symbol: "createInvoice" } }] }).data;
    const notifier = repoWith("worker", {}, { queues: [{ queue: "invoice.created", op: "consume", at: { file: "src/events.ts", line: 1, symbol: "startConsumer" } }] }).data;

    const b = repoWith("b", {}, { queues: [{ queue: "k", op: "consume", at: { file: "b.ts", line: 1, symbol: "g" } }] }).data;
    const pairs = pairQueues(ws([a, b]));
    expect(pairs).toMatchObject([{ confidence: "guess" }]);

});

describe("linkConsumers", () => {
  it("resolves a named consume callback and walks its calls to a db hit", () => {
    const code = [
      'import amqp from "amqplib";',
      "export async function startNotifier() {",
      "  const conn = await amqp.connect();",
      "  const channel = await conn.createChannel();",
      '  await channel.bindQueue("emails", "billing", "invoice.*");',

      "}",
      "",
      "async function handleEmail(msg) {",
      "  return prisma.emailLog.create({ data: {} });",
      "}",
      "",
    ].join("\n");
    const { data, resolver } = repoWith("billing", { "src/notify-consumer.ts": code }, {
      queues: [{ queue: "emails", op: "consume", at: { file: "src/notify-consumer.ts", line: 6, symbol: "startNotifier" } }],
      dbAccess: [{ table: "email_logs", op: "insert", via: "prisma", at: { file: "src/notify-consumer.ts", line: 10, symbol: "handleEmail" } }],
    });
    const links = linkConsumers({ ws: ws([data]), resolvers: new Map([["billing", resolver]]) });
    expect(links).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(links[0]).toMatchObject({
      repo: "billing", handler: { repo: "billing", file: "src/notify-consumer.ts", symbol: "handleEmail" },
      hits: [{ kind: "db", access: { table: "email_logs" } }],
    });
  });

  it("falls back to the consume call's own enclosing symbol for an inline callback", () => {
    const code = [
      'import amqp from "amqplib";',
      "export async function startConsumer() {",
      "  const conn = await amqp.connect();",
      "  const channel = await conn.createChannel();",
      '  await channel.consume("invoice.created", (msg) => {',
      "    if (msg) channel.ack(msg);",
      "  });",
      "}",
      "",
    ].join("\n");
    const { data, resolver } = repoWith("billing", { "src/events.ts": code }, {
      queues: [{ queue: "invoice.created", op: "consume", at: { file: "src/events.ts", line: 5, symbol: "startConsumer" } }],
    });
    const links = linkConsumers({ ws: ws([data]), resolvers: new Map([["billing", resolver]]) });
    expect(links[0]).toMatchObject({ handler: { repo: "billing", file: "src/events.ts", symbol: "startConsumer" }, hits: [] });
  });
});

describe("parseLinkRef", () => {
  it("parses a symbol ref", () => {
    expect(parseLinkRef("api:app/services/customer_service.ts#CustomerService.create")).toEqual({
      kind: "symbol", repo: "api", file: "app/services/customer_service.ts", symbol: "CustomerService.create",
    });
  });
  it("parses a route ref", () => {
    expect(parseLinkRef("route:billing:POST /billing/invoices")).toEqual({ kind: "route", repo: "billing", method: "POST", path: "/billing/invoices" });
  });
  it("parses a queue ref", () => {
    expect(parseLinkRef("queue:billing:invoice.created")).toEqual({ kind: "queue", repo: "billing", queue: "invoice.created" });
  });
  it("returns null for an unparseable ref", () => {
    expect(parseLinkRef("not-a-ref")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("resolveManualLinks", () => {
  it("resolves a link between two existing symbols", () => {
    const { data, resolver } = repoWith("api", { "a.ts": "export function f() { return 1; }", "b.ts": "export function g() { return 2; }" }, {});
    const links = resolveManualLinks({
      ws: ws([data], [{ from: "api:a.ts#f", to: "api:b.ts#g" }]),
      resolvers: new Map([["api", resolver]]),
    });
    expect(links).toEqual([{ from: "api:a.ts#f", to: "api:b.ts#g", resolved: true }]);
  });

  it("marks a link unresolved when a referenced symbol does not exist", () => {
    const { data, resolver } = repoWith("api", { "a.ts": "export function f() { return 1; }" }, {});
    const links = resolveManualLinks({
      ws: ws([data], [{ from: "api:a.ts#f", to: "api:a.ts#missing" }]),
      resolvers: new Map([["api", resolver]]),
    });
    expect(links).toEqual([{ from: "api:a.ts#f", to: "api:a.ts#missing", resolved: false, reason: '"api:a.ts#missing" was not found' }]);
  });

  it("marks a link unresolved when it cannot be parsed", () => {
    const links = resolveManualLinks({ ws: ws([], [{ from: "garbage", to: "also garbage" }]), resolvers: new Map() });
    expect(links).toEqual([{ from: "garbage", to: "also garbage", resolved: false, reason: "could not parse link reference" }]);
  });
});

describe("linkAll", () => {
  it("combines every stage into one result", () => {
    const result = linkAll({ ws: ws([]), resolvers: new Map() });
    expect(result).toEqual({ ui: [], backend: [], consumers: [], queuePairs: [], manual: [] });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import ts from "typescript";
import { lineOf, parseSource, walk } from "../adapters/ast.js";
import type { CallSite } from "./code-index.js";
import { walkCalls } from "./resolve-calls.js";
import type { Loc, DbAccess, HttpMethod, QueueOp } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";

export type StoreHit =
  | { kind: "db"; access: DbAccess; viaPath: WalkStep[] }
  | { kind: "queue"; op: QueueOp; viaPath: WalkStep[] };

export interface QueuePair {
  publish: QueueOp; consume: QueueOp; repoFrom: string; repoTo: string; confidence: Confidence; reason?: string;
}

export interface BackendLink {
  repo: string;
  route: EffectiveRoute;
  handler: SymbolRef | null;
  handlerConfidence: Confidence;
  hits: StoreHit[];
  unresolved: Array<{ at: Loc; reason: string }>;
}

export interface ConsumerLink {
  repo: string; consume: QueueOp; handler: SymbolRef; hits: StoreHit[]; unresolved: Array<{ at: Loc; reason: string }>;
}

export interface ManualLink { from: string; to: string; resolved: boolean; reason?: string }
```

```ts
const DEFAULT_PREFIX_RE = /^default\.?/;

/** Strip a leading "default." (or bare "default") and resolve what remains against the file's actual
 * default-exported symbol name, e.g. route.handler "default" on a next-api file whose default export is
 * named "handler" → symbol "handler"; "default.create" on a default-exported object → "theObject.create". */
function handlerSymbolName(idx: FileCodeIndex | undefined, handler: string): string {
  if (!DEFAULT_PREFIX_RE.test(handler)) return handler;
  const rest = handler.replace(DEFAULT_PREFIX_RE, "");
  const def = idx?.symbols.find((s) => s.exportedAs === "default");
  if (!def) return handler;
  return rest ? `${def.name}.${rest}` : def.name;
}

/** route.handlerFile + route.handler (strip "default."); inline → symbolAt(handlerFile, handlerLines[0])
 * or a synthetic "<route.handler>" symbol spanning handlerLines. */
export function findHandler(input: LinkInput, r: EffectiveRoute): { ref: SymbolRef; confidence: Confidence } | null {
  const route = r.route;
  if (!route.handlerFile) return null;
  const idx = input.resolvers.get(r.repo)?.fileIndex(route.handlerFile);
  if (!route.inline) {
    return { ref: { repo: r.repo, file: route.handlerFile, symbol: handlerSymbolName(idx, route.handler) }, confidence: "exact" };
  }
  const line = route.handlerLines?.[0];
  const bySite = idx && line !== undefined ? symbolAt(idx, line) : undefined;
  const symbol = bySite ? bySite.name : route.handler;
  return { ref: { repo: r.repo, file: route.handlerFile, symbol }, confidence: "exact" };
}
```

```ts
function describeCallee(c: CallSite["callee"]): string {
  switch (c.kind) {
    case "ident": return `could not resolve "${c.name}(...)"`;
    case "member": return `could not resolve "${c.object}.${c.prop}(...)"`;
    case "new-member": return `could not resolve "new ${c.className}().${c.prop}(...)"`;
    case "this": return `could not resolve "this.${c.prop}(...)"`;
    case "this-field": return `could not resolve "this.${c.field}.${c.prop}(...)"`;
    default: return "could not resolve call";
  }
}

/** Every call site inside a visited symbol that the resolver could not resolve — except a site that is
 * itself a recognised DbAccess/QueueOp location (`knownAt`): those are terminal adapter-level facts, not
 * code symbols to resolve further, and must not also show up as a broken call (`codeloom check`'s
 * `unmatched-call` is about genuinely lost calls, not "the ORM call we already turned into a db hit"). */
function collectUnresolved(resolver: CallResolver, steps: WalkStep[], knownAt: Set<string>): Array<{ at: Loc; reason: string }> {
  const out: Array<{ at: Loc; reason: string }> = [];
  for (const step of steps) {
    const idx = resolver.fileIndex(step.ref.file);
    if (!idx) continue;
    for (const site of idx.calls) {
      if (site.caller !== step.ref.symbol) continue;
      if (knownAt.has(`${step.ref.file}:${site.line}`)) continue;
      if (resolver.resolve(step.ref.file, site)) continue;
      out.push({ at: { file: step.ref.file, line: site.line, symbol: step.ref.symbol }, reason: describeCallee(site.callee) });
    }
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => compare(`${a.at.file}:${String(a.at.line).padStart(8, "0")}`, `${b.at.file}:${String(b.at.line).padStart(8, "0")}`));
}

function knownAtKeys(access: DbAccess[], queues: QueueOp[]): Set<string> {
  return new Set([...access, ...queues].map((x) => `${x.at.file}:${x.at.line}`));
}

/** Store hits reachable from `start`, sorted by how far into the walk they were found. */
function storeHits(resolver: CallResolver, repoId: string, start: SymbolRef, access: DbAccess[], queues: QueueOp[]): { steps: WalkStep[]; hits: StoreHit[] } {
  const steps = walkCalls(resolver, start, 6);
  const visited = new Map(steps.map((s) => [stepKey(s.ref), s]));
  const startKey = stepKey(start);
  const hits: StoreHit[] = [];
  for (const a of access) {
    const key = `${repoId}:${a.at.file}#${a.at.symbol}`;
    if (!visited.has(key)) continue;
    const viaPath = chainTowardStart(steps, key, startKey).reverse();
    hits.push({ kind: "db", access: a, viaPath });
  }
  for (const q of queues) {
    if (q.op !== "publish") continue;
    const key = `${repoId}:${q.at.file}#${q.at.symbol}`;
    if (!visited.has(key)) continue;
    const viaPath = chainTowardStart(steps, key, startKey).reverse();
    hits.push({ kind: "queue", op: q, viaPath });
  }
  hits.sort((a, b) => a.viaPath.length - b.viaPath.length || compare(hitLabel(a), hitLabel(b)));
  return { steps, hits };
}

function hitLabel(h: StoreHit): string {
  return h.kind === "db" ? `${h.access.at.file}:${h.access.at.line}` : `${h.op.at.file}:${h.op.at.line}`;
}
```

```ts
/** Route → handler → walkCalls → every DbAccess/publish QueueOp whose at.symbol was visited. */
export function linkBackend(input: LinkInput): BackendLink[] {
  const out: BackendLink[] = [];
  for (const repo of input.ws.repos) {
    const resolver = input.resolvers.get(repo.ref.id);
    if (!resolver) continue;
    for (const er of effectiveRoutes(repo.endpoints)) {
      const found = findHandler(input, er);
      if (!found) {
        out.push({ repo: repo.ref.id, route: er, handler: null, handlerConfidence: "guess", hits: [], unresolved: [] });
        continue;
      }
      const { steps, hits } = storeHits(resolver, repo.ref.id, found.ref, repo.endpoints.dbAccess, repo.endpoints.queues);
      const knownAt = knownAtKeys(repo.endpoints.dbAccess, repo.endpoints.queues);
      out.push({
        repo: repo.ref.id, route: er, handler: found.ref, handlerConfidence: found.confidence,
        hits, unresolved: collectUnresolved(resolver, steps, knownAt),
      });
    }
  }
  out.sort((a, b) => compare(`${a.repo}:${a.route.route.method} ${a.route.effectivePath}`, `${b.repo}:${b.route.route.method} ${b.route.effectivePath}`));
  return out;
}
```

```ts
function routingKeyMatches(bindKey: string, pubKey: string): boolean {
  if (bindKey === pubKey) return true;
  const pattern = bindKey
    .split(".")
    .map((seg) => (seg === "#" ? ".*" : seg === "*" ? "[^.]+" : seg.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")))
    .join("\\.");
  return new RegExp(`^${pattern}$`).test(pubKey);
}

function sortQueuePairs(pairs: QueuePair[]): QueuePair[] {
  return pairs.sort((a, b) => compare(`${a.repoFrom}:${a.publish.at.file}:${a.publish.at.line}|${a.repoTo}:${a.consume.at.file}:${a.consume.at.line}`, `${b.repoFrom}:${b.publish.at.file}:${b.publish.at.line}|${b.repoTo}:${b.consume.at.file}:${b.consume.at.line}`));
}

/** Memo (f): sendToQueue<->consume same queue -> exact; exchange publish -> bind(exchange,key) ->
 * consume(bound queue) -> exact, or param when the binding key has a topic wildcard; fallback
 * queue === key or === exchange -> guess. */
export function pairQueues(ws: WorkspaceData): QueuePair[] {
  const pairs: QueuePair[] = [];
  const binds = ws.repos.flatMap((r) => r.endpoints.queues.filter((q) => q.op === "bind").map((q) => ({ repo: r.ref.id, q })));
  for (const from of ws.repos) {
    for (const publish of from.endpoints.queues.filter((q) => q.op === "publish")) {
      if (publish.exchange === undefined) {
        for (const to of ws.repos) {
          for (const consume of to.endpoints.queues.filter((q) => q.op === "consume" && q.queue === publish.queue)) {
            pairs.push({ publish, consume, repoFrom: from.ref.id, repoTo: to.ref.id, confidence: "exact" });
          }
        }
        continue;
      }
      const matches = binds.filter((b) => b.q.exchange === publish.exchange && routingKeyMatches(b.q.routingKey ?? "", publish.routingKey ?? ""));
      if (matches.length > 0) {
        for (const b of matches) {
          for (const to of ws.repos) {
            for (const consume of to.endpoints.queues.filter((q) => q.op === "consume" && q.queue === b.q.queue)) {
              const confidence: Confidence = /[*#]/.test(b.q.routingKey ?? "") ? "param" : "exact";
              pairs.push({ publish, consume, repoFrom: from.ref.id, repoTo: to.ref.id, confidence });
            }
          }
        }
        continue;
      }
      for (const to of ws.repos) {
        for (const consume of to.endpoints.queues.filter((q) => q.op === "consume" && (q.queue === publish.routingKey || q.queue === publish.exchange))) {
          pairs.push({ publish, consume, repoFrom: from.ref.id, repoTo: to.ref.id, confidence: "guess", reason: "no binding found; matched by exchange/routing-key heuristic" });
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return sortQueuePairs(pairs);
}
```

```ts
/** `channel.consume("q", handleEmail)` at `at.line` -> "handleEmail"; undefined for an inline callback
 * (nothing to find) or if the file cannot be read. See this task's "Registry fix". */
function namedConsumeCallback(repoAbs: string, at: Loc): string | undefined {
  let content: string;
  try {
    content = readFileSync(join(repoAbs, at.file), "utf8");
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
  const sf = parseSource(at.file, content);
  let found: string | undefined;
  walk(sf, (node) => {
    if (found || !ts.isCallExpression(node) || !ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node.expression)) return;
    if (node.expression.name.text !== "consume" || lineOf(node) !== at.line) return;
    const second = node.arguments[1];
    if (second && ts.isIdentifier(second)) found = second.text;
  });
  return found;
}

/** The consume callback's own symbol: a named reference resolved like any other identifier call, or
 * (inline callback) the consume() call site's own enclosing symbol. */
function consumeHandlerRef(input: LinkInput, repo: RepoData, q: QueueOp): SymbolRef {
  const resolver = input.resolvers.get(repo.ref.id);
  const name = resolver && namedConsumeCallback(repo.abs, q.at);
  if (name && resolver) {
    const site: CallSite = { caller: q.at.symbol, line: q.at.line, callee: { kind: "ident", name } };
    const resolved = resolver.resolve(q.at.file, site);
    if (resolved) return resolved.target;
  }
  return { repo: repo.ref.id, file: q.at.file, symbol: q.at.symbol };
}

/** Every `consume` op -> its handler -> walkCalls -> store hits, each starting its own chain. */
export function linkConsumers(input: LinkInput): ConsumerLink[] {
  const out: ConsumerLink[] = [];
  for (const repo of input.ws.repos) {
    const resolver = input.resolvers.get(repo.ref.id);
    if (!resolver) continue;
    for (const q of repo.endpoints.queues) {
      if (q.op !== "consume") continue;
      const handler = consumeHandlerRef(input, repo, q);
      const { steps, hits } = storeHits(resolver, repo.ref.id, handler, repo.endpoints.dbAccess, repo.endpoints.queues);
      const knownAt = knownAtKeys(repo.endpoints.dbAccess, repo.endpoints.queues);
      out.push({ repo: repo.ref.id, consume: q, handler, hits, unresolved: collectUnresolved(resolver, steps, knownAt) });
    }
  }
  out.sort((a, b) => compare(`${a.repo}:${a.consume.at.file}:${a.consume.at.line}`, `${b.repo}:${b.consume.at.file}:${b.consume.at.line}`));
  return out;
}
```

```ts
export type LinkRef =
  | { kind: "symbol"; repo: string; file: string; symbol: string }
  | { kind: "route"; repo: string; method: HttpMethod; path: string }
  | { kind: "queue"; repo: string; queue: string };

/** "<repo>:<file>#<symbol>" | "route:<repo>:<METHOD path>" | "queue:<repo>:<queue>". Route/queue prefixes
 * are checked first so a plain symbol ref's repo id is never mistaken for one of them. */
export function parseLinkRef(s: string): LinkRef | null {
  let m = s.match(/^route:([^:]+):([A-Z]+) (.+)$/);
  if (m) return { kind: "route", repo: m[1]!, method: m[2] as HttpMethod, path: m[3]! };
  m = s.match(/^queue:([^:]+):(.+)$/);
  if (m) return { kind: "queue", repo: m[1]!, queue: m[2]! };
  m = s.match(/^([^:]+):([^#]+)#(.+)$/);
  if (m) return { kind: "symbol", repo: m[1]!, file: m[2]!, symbol: m[3]! };
  return null;
}

function refExists(input: LinkInput, ref: LinkRef): boolean {
  const repo = input.ws.repos.find((r) => r.ref.id === ref.repo);
  if (!repo) return false;
  if (ref.kind === "symbol") return !!input.resolvers.get(ref.repo)?.fileIndex(ref.file)?.symbols.some((s) => s.name === ref.symbol);
  if (ref.kind === "route") return effectiveRoutes(repo.endpoints).some((er) => er.route.method === ref.method && er.effectivePath === ref.path);
  return repo.endpoints.queues.some((q) => q.queue === ref.queue);
}

/** Validate every workspace `links` entry against the repos/symbols/routes/queues that actually exist. */
export function resolveManualLinks(input: LinkInput): ManualLink[] {
  return input.ws.links.map((l) => {
    const from = parseLinkRef(l.from);
    const to = parseLinkRef(l.to);
    if (!from || !to) return { from: l.from, to: l.to, resolved: false, reason: "could not parse link reference" };
    if (!refExists(input, from)) return { from: l.from, to: l.to, resolved: false, reason: `"${l.from}" was not found` };
    if (!refExists(input, to)) return { from: l.from, to: l.to, resolved: false, reason: `"${l.to}" was not found` };
    return { from: l.from, to: l.to, resolved: true };
  });
}

export interface LinkResult {
  ui: UiLink[]; backend: BackendLink[]; consumers: ConsumerLink[]; queuePairs: QueuePair[]; manual: ManualLink[];
}

export function linkAll(input: LinkInput): LinkResult {
  return {
    ui: linkUi(input),
    backend: linkBackend(input),
    consumers: linkConsumers(input),
    queuePairs: pairQueues(input.ws),
    manual: resolveManualLinks(input),
  };
}
```

  Journey/JourneysFile)

- Test: `packages/cli/test/chains.test.ts` (new), `packages/cli/test/linker-demo-shop.test.ts` (new),
  `packages/cli/test/index-cmd.test.ts` (modified for the new fixture content)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: R11 `LinkResult`, `linkAll`, `BackendLink`, `ConsumerLink`, `StoreHit`, `UiLink` (`linker/link.ts`);
  R10 `LinkInput`, `PageReach`, `stepKey` (`linker/link.ts`); R7 `createCallResolver`, `WalkStep`, `SymbolRef`
  (`linker/resolve-calls.ts`); R9 `routeKey`, `EffectiveRoute` (`linker/mounts.ts`); R6 `IndexResult` extras
  (`indexer/index-repo.ts`); R5 `WorkspaceData`, `RepoData` (`store/read.ts`); `writeJsonIfChanged`, `readJson`,
  `writeJsonAtomic` (`store/json.ts`); `compare` (`util.ts`); R1 `FILES`, `Confidence`, `DbAccess`, `QueueOp`.
- Produces (exact, from the registry): the schema block and `linker/chains.ts` / `linker/run.ts` /
  `commands/index-cmd.ts` exports reproduced in Step 3 below.

**Design decision (documented, not a registry bug — no file outside this task's own scope is touched):**
`PageReach.path` (R10) is `SymbolRef[]`, with no per-hop confidence — the reverse UI walk only ever follows
*resolved* calls, and threading a confidence through would widen `PageReach`'s exact registry signature. Every
intra-UI "function" stop this task builds from a `PageReach.path` is therefore recorded with hop confidence
`"exact"`. This matches reality for every `via` tag except the rare `"unique-method"` guess fallback; a page
reached only through a guessed receiver call would show as more confident than it is. Out of scope for this
task. By contrast, `StoreHit.viaPath` (R11) is `WalkStep[]`, which *does* carry each step's own resolved
`ResolvedCall` (`step.via`), so every backend-side "function" stop uses that step's real confidence.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test for `linker/chains.ts`**

`packages/cli/test/chains.test.ts`:
```ts
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { KnowledgeGraph } from "@codeloom/core";
import {
  buildChains, chainId, defaultChainName, journeyId, nearestNodeId, sortChains, syncJourneys, writeChains,
} from "../src/linker/chains.js";
import type { BackendLink, ConsumerLink, LinkResult, UiLink } from "../src/linker/link.js";
import type { RepoData, WorkspaceData } from "../src/store/read.js";
import type { DbAccess, EndpointsFile, HttpCall, QueueOp, Route, UiPage } from "../src/store/schemas.js";
import { writeJsonAtomic } from "../src/store/json.js";

function graphWith(nodes: KnowledgeGraph["nodes"]): KnowledgeGraph {
  return {
    version: "1",
    project: { name: "x", languages: [], frameworks: [], description: "", analyzedAt: "", gitCommitHash: "" },
    nodes, edges: [], layers: [], tour: [],
  };
}

function endpoints(repo: string, partial: Partial<EndpointsFile>): EndpointsFile {
  return {
    version: 2, repo, pages: [], httpCalls: [], httpClients: [], routes: [], mounts: [],
    dbAccess: [], tables: [], models: [], queues: [], ...partial,
  };
}
```

```ts
describe("chainId / journeyId", () => {
  it("is deterministic and matches the expected shape", () => {
    const id = chainId("page:web:/customers/new", "api:POST /api/customers");
    expect(id).toMatch(/^ch_[0-9a-f]{12}$/);
    expect(chainId("page:web:/customers/new", "api:POST /api/customers")).toBe(id);
    expect(chainId("page:web:/customers/new", "api:GET /api/customers/:id")).not.toBe(id);
  });

  it("journeyId is order-independent over the chain ids it hashes", () => {
    const a = journeyId(["ch_111111111111", "ch_222222222222"]);
    const b = journeyId(["ch_222222222222", "ch_111111111111"]);
    expect(a).toMatch(/^j_[0-9a-f]{12}$/);
    expect(a).toBe(b);
  });
});

describe("nearestNodeId", () => {
  const graph = graphWith([
    { id: "function:a.ts:top", type: "function", name: "top", filePath: "a.ts", lineRange: [1, 3], summary: "", tags: [], complexity: "simple" },
    { id: "class:b.ts:Widget", type: "class", name: "Widget", filePath: "b.ts", lineRange: [1, 10], summary: "", tags: [], complexity: "simple" },
    { id: "file:c.ts", type: "file", name: "c.ts", filePath: "c.ts", summary: "", tags: [], complexity: "simple" },
  ]);

  it("prefers an exact function node", () => {
    expect(nearestNodeId(graph, "a.ts", "top")).toBe("function:a.ts:top");
  });
  it("falls back to the class node for a method symbol", () => {
    expect(nearestNodeId(graph, "b.ts", "Widget.render")).toBe("class:b.ts:Widget");
  });
  it("falls back to the file node", () => {
    expect(nearestNodeId(graph, "c.ts", "anything")).toBe("file:c.ts");
  });
  it("returns undefined when nothing matches", () => {
    expect(nearestNodeId(graph, "z.ts", "nope")).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

```ts
function scenario(): { ws: WorkspaceData; links: LinkResult } {
  const call: HttpCall = { method: "POST", path: "/api/items", raw: '"/api/items"', resolved: true, client: "api", at: { file: "lib/use-create-item.ts", line: 3, symbol: "useCreateItem" }, kind: "literal" };
  const page: UiPage = { route: "/items/new", file: "app/items/new/page.tsx", line: 2, framework: "next-app" };
  const route: Route = { method: "POST", path: "/api/items", handler: "ItemsController.store", inline: false, framework: "adonis", at: { file: "start/routes.ts", line: 1, symbol: "<module>" }, handlerFile: "app/controllers/items_controller.ts" };
  const effRoute = { repo: "api", route, effectivePath: "/api/items", mountsKnown: true, mounts: [] };
  const uiLink: UiLink = {
    repo: "web", call,
    pages: [{ page, path: [{ repo: "web", file: "app/items/new/page.tsx", symbol: "NewItemPage" }, { repo: "web", file: "lib/use-create-item.ts", symbol: "useCreateItem" }] }],
    target: { kind: "route", route: effRoute, confidence: "exact" },
  };
  const dbAccess: DbAccess = { table: "items", op: "insert", via: "lucid", at: { file: "app/services/item_service.ts", line: 2, symbol: "ItemService.create" } };
  const handlerRef = { repo: "api", file: "app/controllers/items_controller.ts", symbol: "ItemsController.store" };
  const serviceRef = { repo: "api", file: "app/services/item_service.ts", symbol: "ItemService.create" };
  const backendLink: BackendLink = {
    repo: "api", route: effRoute, handler: handlerRef, handlerConfidence: "exact",
    hits: [{
      kind: "db", access: dbAccess,
      viaPath: [
        { ref: handlerRef, depth: 0 },
        { ref: serviceRef, depth: 1, parent: handlerRef, via: { site: { caller: "ItemsController.store", line: 5, callee: { kind: "new-member", className: "ItemService", prop: "create" } }, target: serviceRef, confidence: "exact", via: "new" } },
      ],
    }],
    unresolved: [],
  };
  const route2: Route = { method: "GET", path: "/health", handler: "GET /health", inline: true, framework: "express", at: { file: "src/server.ts", line: 1, symbol: "<module>" }, handlerFile: "src/server.ts", handlerLines: [1, 1] };
  const effRoute2 = { repo: "api2", route: route2, effectivePath: "/health", mountsKnown: true, mounts: [] };
  const backendLink2: BackendLink = { repo: "api2", route: effRoute2, handler: { repo: "api2", file: "src/server.ts", symbol: "GET /health" }, handlerConfidence: "exact", hits: [], unresolved: [] };
  const consumeOp: QueueOp = { queue: "jobs", op: "consume", at: { file: "src/worker.ts", line: 2, symbol: "startWorker" } };
  const jobRef = { repo: "api2", file: "src/worker.ts", symbol: "handleJob" };
  const consumerLink: ConsumerLink = {
    repo: "api2", consume: consumeOp, handler: jobRef,
    hits: [{ kind: "db", access: { table: "jobs_log", op: "insert", via: "prisma", at: { file: "src/worker.ts", line: 5, symbol: "handleJob" } }, viaPath: [{ ref: jobRef, depth: 0 }] }],
    unresolved: [],
  };
  const web: RepoData = { ref: { id: "web", path: "web" }, abs: "/ws/web", graph: graphWith([]), endpoints: endpoints("web", { pages: [page], httpCalls: [call] }) };
  const api: RepoData = {
    ref: { id: "api", path: "api" }, abs: "/ws/api",
    graph: graphWith([{ id: "class:app/controllers/items_controller.ts:ItemsController", type: "class", name: "ItemsController", filePath: "app/controllers/items_controller.ts", lineRange: [2, 10], summary: "", tags: [], complexity: "simple" }]),
    endpoints: endpoints("api", { routes: [route], dbAccess: [dbAccess] }),
  };
  const api2: RepoData = { ref: { id: "api2", path: "api2" }, abs: "/ws/api2", graph: graphWith([]), endpoints: endpoints("api2", { routes: [route2], queues: [consumeOp] }) };
  const ws: WorkspaceData = { root: "/ws", name: "ws", repos: [web, api, api2], links: [] };
  const links: LinkResult = { ui: [uiLink], backend: [backendLink, backendLink2], consumers: [consumerLink], queuePairs: [], manual: [] };
```

```ts
describe("buildChains", () => {
  it("builds a page chain, a route-entry chain and a consumer chain", () => {
    const { ws, links } = scenario();
    const chains = buildChains(ws, links);
    expect(chains).toHaveLength(3);

    const page = chains.find((c) => c.entry === "page:web:/items/new")!;
    expect(page.routeKey).toBe("api:POST /api/items");
    expect(page.id).toBe(chainId(page.entry, page.routeKey));
    expect(page.complete).toBe(true);
    expect(page.confidence).toBe("exact");
    expect(page.name).toBe("POST /api/items · NewItemPage");
    expect(page.stops.map((s) => `${s.lane}/${s.kind}`)).toEqual(["ui/page", "ui/function", "ui/call", "api/route", "code/handler", "code/function", "db/db"]);

    const health = chains.find((c) => c.entry === "route:api2:GET /health")!;
    expect(health.routeKey).toBe("api2:GET /health");
    expect(health.complete).toBe(false); // no db/queue hits: check's "no-store"
    expect(health.name).toBe("GET /health");

    const consumer = chains.find((c) => c.entry === "consumer:api2:jobs")!;
    expect(consumer.routeKey).toBe("");
    expect(consumer.complete).toBe(true);
    expect(consumer.name).toBe("consume jobs");
    expect(consumer.stops[0]).toMatchObject({ lane: "queue", kind: "consume", label: "jobs" });
  });
});

describe("sortChains", () => {
  it("sorts by id", () => {
    const { ws, links } = scenario();
    const chains = buildChains(ws, links);
    const shuffled = [chains[2]!, chains[0]!, chains[1]!];
    expect(sortChains(shuffled).map((c) => c.id)).toEqual([...chains.map((c) => c.id)].sort());
  });
});

describe("writeChains / syncJourneys", () => {
  function root() {
    return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "codeloom-chains-"));
  }

  it("writes chains.json only when content changes", () => {
    const { ws, links } = scenario();
    const chains = sortChains(buildChains(ws, links));
    const wsRoot = root();
    expect(writeChains(wsRoot, chains)).toBe(true);
    const before = readFileSync(join(wsRoot, ".codeloom", "chains.json"), "utf8");
    expect(writeChains(wsRoot, chains)).toBe(false);
    expect(readFileSync(join(wsRoot, ".codeloom", "chains.json"), "utf8")).toBe(before);
  });

  it("creates journeys.json when missing", () => {
    const wsRoot = root();
    const result = syncJourneys(wsRoot, new Set(["ch_aaaaaaaaaaaa"]));
    expect(result).toEqual({ created: true, orphaned: 0, changed: true });
    expect(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(wsRoot, ".codeloom", "journeys.json"), "utf8"))).toEqual({ version: 1, journeys: [] });
  });

  it("orphans a journey whose chain id disappeared, and un-orphans it when the id comes back", () => {
    const wsRoot = root();
    writeJsonAtomic(join(wsRoot, ".codeloom", "journeys.json"), {
      version: 1,
      journeys: [{ id: "j_aaaaaaaaaaaa", name: "Create item", area: "items", note: "", chainIds: ["ch_aaaaaaaaaaaa"], status: "ok" }],
    });
    const gone = syncJourneys(wsRoot, new Set());
    expect(gone).toEqual({ created: false, orphaned: 1, changed: true });
    const after = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(wsRoot, ".codeloom", "journeys.json"), "utf8"));
    expect(after.journeys[0].status).toBe("orphaned");

    const back = syncJourneys(wsRoot, new Set(["ch_aaaaaaaaaaaa"]));
    expect(back).toEqual({ created: false, orphaned: 0, changed: true });

    const noChange = syncJourneys(wsRoot, new Set(["ch_aaaaaaaaaaaa"]));
    expect(noChange).toEqual({ created: false, orphaned: 0, changed: false });
  });
});
```

```ts
export const LaneSchema = z.enum(["ui", "api", "code", "db", "queue"]);
export type Lane = z.infer<typeof LaneSchema>;

export const StopKindSchema = z.enum(["page", "call", "route", "handler", "function", "db", "publish", "consume"]);
export type StopKind = z.infer<typeof StopKindSchema>;

export const HopSchema = z.object({ confidence: ConfidenceSchema, reason: z.string().optional() });
export type Hop = z.infer<typeof HopSchema>;

export const ChainStopSchema = z.object({
  lane: LaneSchema,
  kind: StopKindSchema,
  repo: z.string(),
  nodeId: z.string().optional(),
  file: z.string(),
  line: z.number().int().positive(),
  symbol: z.string(),
  op: z.string().optional(),
  label: z.string().optional(),
  hop: HopSchema,
});
export type ChainStop = z.infer<typeof ChainStopSchema>;

export const UnresolvedSchema = z.object({ at: LocSchema.extend({ repo: z.string() }), reason: z.string() });
export type Unresolved = z.infer<typeof UnresolvedSchema>;

export const ChainSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(/^ch_[0-9a-f]{12}$/),
  entry: z.string(),
  routeKey: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  stops: z.array(ChainStopSchema),
  unresolved: z.array(UnresolvedSchema),
  confidence: ConfidenceSchema,
  complete: z.boolean(),
});
export type Chain = z.infer<typeof ChainSchema>;

export const ChainsFileSchema = z.object({ version: z.literal(1), chains: z.array(ChainSchema) });
export type ChainsFile = z.infer<typeof ChainsFileSchema>;

export const JourneySchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(/^j_[0-9a-f]{12}$/),
  name: z.string(),
  area: z.string(),
  note: z.string(),
  chainIds: z.array(z.string()),
  status: z.enum(["ok", "orphaned"]),
});
export type Journey = z.infer<typeof JourneySchema>;

export const JourneysFileSchema = z.object({ version: z.literal(1), journeys: z.array(JourneySchema) });
export type JourneysFile = z.infer<typeof JourneysFileSchema>;
```

```ts
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { join } from "node:path";
import type { KnowledgeGraph } from "@codeloom/core";
import { stepKey } from "./link.js";
import type { BackendLink, ConsumerLink, LinkResult, PageReach, StoreHit, UiLink } from "./link.js";
import type { SymbolRef, WalkStep } from "./resolve-calls.js";
import type { EffectiveRoute } from "./mounts.js";
import { routeKey } from "./mounts.js";
import type { RepoData, WorkspaceData } from "../store/read.js";
import {
  type Chain, type ChainStop, type ChainsFile, type Confidence, type Hop, type Journey, type JourneysFile,
  JourneysFileSchema, type Unresolved,
} from "../store/schemas.js";
import { readJson, writeJsonAtomic, writeJsonIfChanged } from "../store/json.js";
import { CODELOOM_DIR, FILES } from "../store/paths.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";

export function chainId(entry: string, routeKey: string): string {
  return `ch_${createHash("sha256").update(`${entry}|${routeKey}`).digest("hex").slice(0, 12)}`;
}

export function journeyId(chainIds: string[]): string {
  const sorted = [...chainIds].sort(compare).join(",");
  return `j_${createHash("sha256").update(sorted).digest("hex").slice(0, 12)}`;
}

/** Nearest graph.json node: function node, else class node, else file node. */
export function nearestNodeId(graph: KnowledgeGraph, file: string, symbol: string): string | undefined {
  const ids = new Set(graph.nodes.map((n) => n.id));
  const fnId = `function:${file}:${symbol}`;
  if (ids.has(fnId)) return fnId;
  const cls = symbol.includes(".") ? symbol.slice(0, symbol.indexOf(".")) : symbol;
  const clsId = `class:${file}:${cls}`;
  if (ids.has(clsId)) return clsId;
  const fileId = `file:${file}`;
  return ids.has(fileId) ? fileId : undefined;
}

function nodeLine(graph: KnowledgeGraph, nodeId: string | undefined): number {
  const node = nodeId ? graph.nodes.find((n) => n.id === nodeId) : undefined;
  return node?.lineRange?.[0] ?? 1;
}

function graphOf(ws: WorkspaceData, repoId: string): KnowledgeGraph {
  return ws.repos.find((r) => r.ref.id === repoId)!.graph;
}

/** A stop for a bare SymbolRef (no Loc of its own): nodeId/line come from the nearest graph node,
 * unless `line` is given directly (the page stop, which has a real UiPage.line). */
function refStop(graph: KnowledgeGraph, lane: ChainStop["lane"], kind: "function" | "handler" | "page", ref: SymbolRef, hop: Hop, line?: number): ChainStop {
  const nodeId = nearestNodeId(graph, ref.file, ref.symbol);
  return { lane, kind, repo: ref.repo, nodeId, file: ref.file, line: line ?? nodeLine(graph, nodeId), symbol: ref.symbol, hop };
}
```

```ts
function uiStops(ws: WorkspaceData, link: UiLink, page: PageReach): ChainStop[] {
  const graph = graphOf(ws, link.repo);
  const stops: ChainStop[] = page.path.map((ref, i) =>
    i === 0
      ? refStop(graph, "ui", "page", ref, { confidence: "exact" }, page.page.line)
      : refStop(graph, "ui", "function", ref, { confidence: "exact" }),
  );
  stops.push({
    lane: "ui", kind: "call", repo: link.repo,
    nodeId: nearestNodeId(graph, link.call.at.file, link.call.at.symbol),
    file: link.call.at.file, line: link.call.at.line, symbol: link.call.at.symbol,
    op: link.call.method, hop: { confidence: "exact" },
  });
  return stops;
}

function routeStop(ws: WorkspaceData, er: EffectiveRoute, hop: Hop): ChainStop {
  const graph = graphOf(ws, er.repo);
  return {
    lane: "api", kind: "route", repo: er.repo,
    nodeId: nearestNodeId(graph, er.route.at.file, er.route.at.symbol),
    file: er.route.at.file, line: er.route.at.line, symbol: er.route.handler, op: er.route.method,
    label: `${er.route.method} ${er.effectivePath}`, hop,
  };
}

function dbStop(repo: string, access: StoreHit & { kind: "db" }): ChainStop {
  return {
    lane: "db", kind: "db", repo,
    file: access.access.at.file, line: access.access.at.line, symbol: access.access.at.symbol,
    op: access.access.op, label: access.access.table ?? access.access.model ?? "", hop: { confidence: "exact" },
  };
}

function publishStop(repo: string, hit: StoreHit & { kind: "queue" }): ChainStop {
  return {
    lane: "queue", kind: "publish", repo,
    file: hit.op.at.file, line: hit.op.at.line, symbol: hit.op.at.symbol,
    op: "publish", label: hit.op.queue, hop: { confidence: "exact" },
  };
}
```

```ts
function hopFromStep(step: WalkStep): Hop {
  return { confidence: step.via?.confidence ?? "exact", reason: step.via ? `via ${step.via.via}` : undefined };
}

/** Intermediate "function" stops for every hit's viaPath (deduped by symbol across all of a link's
 * hits), then each hit's own terminal db/publish stop. `emitted` starts containing the entry symbol
 * (handler, or the consume call's own symbol) so it is never re-emitted as a redundant function stop. */
function hitStops(ws: WorkspaceData, repo: string, hits: StoreHit[], emitted: Set<string>): ChainStop[] {
  const graph = graphOf(ws, repo);
  const stops: ChainStop[] = [];
  for (const hit of hits) {
    const viaPath = hit.viaPath;
    for (let i = 1; i < viaPath.length - 1; i++) {
      const step = viaPath[i]!;
      const key = stepKey(step.ref);
      if (emitted.has(key)) continue;
      emitted.add(key);
      stops.push(refStop(graph, "code", "function", step.ref, hopFromStep(step)));
    }
    const last = viaPath[viaPath.length - 1];
    if (last && viaPath.length > 1 && !emitted.has(stepKey(last.ref))) {
      emitted.add(stepKey(last.ref));
      stops.push(refStop(graph, "code", "function", last.ref, hopFromStep(last)));
    }
    stops.push(hit.kind === "db" ? dbStop(repo, hit) : publishStop(repo, hit));
  }
  return stops;
}

function backendStops(ws: WorkspaceData, link: BackendLink): ChainStop[] {
  if (!link.handler) return [];
  const graph = graphOf(ws, link.repo);
  const stops: ChainStop[] = [refStop(graph, "code", "handler", link.handler, { confidence: link.handlerConfidence })];
  stops.push(...hitStops(ws, link.repo, link.hits, new Set([stepKey(link.handler)])));
  return stops;
}

function consumerStops(ws: WorkspaceData, link: ConsumerLink): ChainStop[] {
  const graph = graphOf(ws, link.repo);
  const consumeRef: SymbolRef = { repo: link.repo, file: link.consume.at.file, symbol: link.consume.at.symbol };
  const stops: ChainStop[] = [{
    lane: "queue", kind: "consume", repo: link.repo,
    nodeId: nearestNodeId(graph, consumeRef.file, consumeRef.symbol),
    file: consumeRef.file, line: link.consume.at.line, symbol: consumeRef.symbol,
    op: "consume", label: link.consume.queue, hop: { confidence: "exact" },
  }];
  const emitted = new Set([stepKey(consumeRef)]);
  if (stepKey(link.handler) !== stepKey(consumeRef)) {
    stops.push(refStop(graph, "code", "handler", link.handler, { confidence: "exact" }));
    emitted.add(stepKey(link.handler));
  }
  stops.push(...hitStops(ws, link.repo, link.hits, emitted));
  return stops;
}
```

```ts
function toUnresolved(repo: string, u: { at: { file: string; line: number; symbol: string }; reason: string }): Unresolved {
  return { at: { repo, ...u.at }, reason: u.reason };
}

function sortUnresolved(list: Unresolved[]): Unresolved[] {
  return list.sort((a, b) => compare(`${a.at.repo}:${a.at.file}:${String(a.at.line).padStart(8, "0")}:${a.reason}`, `${b.at.repo}:${b.at.file}:${String(b.at.line).padStart(8, "0")}:${b.reason}`));
}

function isComplete(stops: ChainStop[]): boolean {
  return stops.some((s) => s.lane === "db") || stops.some((s) => s.lane === "queue" && s.kind === "publish");
}

const RANK: Record<Confidence, number> = { exact: 2, param: 1, guess: 0 };

function worstConfidence(stops: ChainStop[]): Confidence {
  let worst: Confidence = "exact";
  for (const s of stops) if (RANK[s.hop.confidence] < RANK[worst]) worst = s.hop.confidence;
  return worst;
}

/** "POST /api/customers · NewCustomerPage" (page); "POST /billing/invoices/:id/pay" (route-only, uses the
 * route stop's label); "consume invoice.created" (consumer, uses the consume stop's label). */
export function defaultChainName(c: Pick<Chain, "entry" | "stops">): string {
  if (c.entry.startsWith("consumer:")) {
    const consume = c.stops.find((s) => s.kind === "consume");
    return `consume ${consume?.label ?? ""}`;
  }
  const route = c.stops.find((s) => s.kind === "route");
  if (c.entry.startsWith("page:")) {
    const page = c.stops.find((s) => s.kind === "page");
    return `${route?.label ?? ""} · ${page?.symbol ?? ""}`;
  }
  return route?.label ?? c.entry;
}
```

```ts
/** One chain per (entry, routeKey): each page reaching a call -> route; each route with no UI caller ->
 * route-entry chain; each consumer -> consumer chain. */
export function buildChains(ws: WorkspaceData, links: LinkResult): Chain[] {
  const backendByRouteKey = new Map(links.backend.map((b) => [routeKey(b.route), b] as const));
  const targetedRouteKeys = new Set(
    links.ui.filter((u): u is UiLink & { target: { kind: "route"; route: EffectiveRoute; confidence: Confidence; reason?: string } } => u.target.kind === "route")
      .map((u) => routeKey(u.target.route)),
  );
  const chains: Chain[] = [];

  for (const uiLink of links.ui) {
    if (uiLink.target.kind !== "route") continue;
    const target = uiLink.target;
    const rKey = routeKey(target.route);
    const backend = backendByRouteKey.get(rKey);
    for (const page of uiLink.pages) {
      const entry = `page:${uiLink.repo}:${page.page.route}`;
      const stops = [
        ...uiStops(ws, uiLink, page),
        routeStop(ws, target.route, { confidence: target.confidence, reason: target.reason }),
        ...(backend ? backendStops(ws, backend) : []),
      ];
      chains.push({
        id: chainId(entry, rKey), entry, routeKey: rKey, name: defaultChainName({ entry, stops }), stops,
        unresolved: sortUnresolved((backend?.unresolved ?? []).map((u) => toUnresolved(backend!.repo, u))),
        confidence: worstConfidence(stops), complete: isComplete(stops),
      });
    }
  }

  for (const b of links.backend) {
    const rKey = routeKey(b.route);
    if (targetedRouteKeys.has(rKey)) continue;
    const entry = `route:${rKey}`;
    const stops = [routeStop(ws, b.route, { confidence: "exact" }), ...backendStops(ws, b)];
    chains.push({
      id: chainId(entry, rKey), entry, routeKey: rKey, name: defaultChainName({ entry, stops }), stops,
      unresolved: sortUnresolved(b.unresolved.map((u) => toUnresolved(b.repo, u))),
      confidence: worstConfidence(stops), complete: isComplete(stops),
    });
  }

  for (const c of links.consumers) {
    const entry = `consumer:${c.repo}:${c.consume.queue}`;
    const stops = consumerStops(ws, c);
    chains.push({
      id: chainId(entry, ""), entry, routeKey: "", name: defaultChainName({ entry, stops }), stops,
      unresolved: sortUnresolved(c.unresolved.map((u) => toUnresolved(c.repo, u))),
      confidence: worstConfidence(stops), complete: isComplete(stops),
    });
  }

  return chains;
}

/** Sort chains by id; stops keep walk order; unresolved is sorted when each chain is built. */
export function sortChains(chains: Chain[]): Chain[] {
  return chains.sort((a, b) => compare(a.id, b.id));
}

export function writeChains(wsRoot: string, chains: Chain[]): boolean {
  const file: ChainsFile = { version: 1, chains };
  return writeJsonIfChanged(join(wsRoot, CODELOOM_DIR, FILES.chains), file);
}
```

```ts
/** Create journeys.json if missing; otherwise flip each journey's status to "ok"/"orphaned" depending on
 * whether every one of its chainIds is still in `ids`, writing only if some status actually changed. Never
 * touches order or any other field. A malformed existing file throws (via readJson) and is left untouched. */
export function syncJourneys(wsRoot: string, ids: Set<string>): { created: boolean; orphaned: number; changed: boolean } {
  const path = join(wsRoot, CODELOOM_DIR, FILES.journeys);
  const existing = readJson(path, JourneysFileSchema);
  if (!existing) {
    const empty: JourneysFile = { version: 1, journeys: [] };
    writeJsonAtomic(path, empty);
    return { created: true, orphaned: 0, changed: true };
  }
  let changed = false;
  let orphaned = 0;
  const journeys: Journey[] = existing.journeys.map((j) => {
    const status = j.chainIds.every((id) => ids.has(id)) ? "ok" : "orphaned";
    if (status === "orphaned") orphaned++;
    if (status !== j.status) changed = true;
    return { ...j, status };
  });
  if (changed) writeJsonAtomic(path, { version: 1, journeys } satisfies JourneysFile);
  return { created: false, orphaned, changed };
}
```

```ts
import type { Request, Response } from "express";
import { invoiceService } from "./invoice-service";
import { publishInvoiceCreated, publishInvoicePaid } from "./events";

export async function createInvoice(req: Request, res: Response) {
  const invoice = await invoiceService.create(req.body);
  await publishInvoiceCreated(invoice.id);
  res.status(201).json(invoice);
}

export async function payInvoice(req: Request, res: Response) {
  const invoice = await invoiceService.markPaid(Number(req.params.id));
  await publishInvoicePaid(invoice.id);
  res.json(invoice);
}
```

`fixtures/demo-shop/billing/src/events.ts` (full new content, replacing the current file):
```ts
import amqp from "amqplib";

export async function publishInvoiceCreated(id: number) {
  const conn = await amqp.connect(process.env.AMQP_URL ?? "amqp://localhost");
  const channel = await conn.createChannel();
  channel.sendToQueue("invoice.created", Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ id })));
}

export async function publishInvoicePaid(id: number) {
  const conn = await amqp.connect(process.env.AMQP_URL ?? "amqp://localhost");
  const channel = await conn.createChannel();
  channel.publish("billing", "invoice.paid", Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ id })));
}

export async function startConsumer() {
  const conn = await amqp.connect(process.env.AMQP_URL ?? "amqp://localhost");
  const channel = await conn.createChannel();
  await channel.consume("invoice.created", (msg) => {
    if (msg) channel.ack(msg);
  });
}
```

```ts
import amqp from "amqplib";
import { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";

const prisma = new PrismaClient();

export async function startNotifier() {
  const conn = await amqp.connect(process.env.AMQP_URL ?? "amqp://localhost");
  const channel = await conn.createChannel();
  await channel.bindQueue("emails", "billing", "invoice.*");
  await channel.consume("emails", handleEmail);
}

async function handleEmail(msg: unknown) {
  await prisma.emailLog.create({ data: { invoiceId: 1 } });
}
```

```ts
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { runIndex } from "../src/commands/index-cmd.js";
import { materializeFixture } from "./helpers.js";

function workspace(): string {
  const root = materializeFixture("demo-shop");
  writeFileSync(
    join(root, "codeloom.workspace.json"),
    JSON.stringify({
      version: 1, name: "demo-shop",
      repos: [{ id: "api", path: "api" }, { id: "billing", path: "billing" }, { id: "web", path: "web" }],
      links: [],
    }),
  );
  return root;
}

function readChains(root: string): { version: number; chains: Array<Record<string, unknown>> } {
  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, ".codeloom", "chains.json"), "utf8"));
}

describe("the linker on demo-shop", () => {
  it("builds every expected chain: UI -> API -> code -> DB, the /billing mount, DI, the object-literal service, queue pairing, and backend-only routes", async () => {
    const root = workspace();
    await runIndex(root);
    const { chains } = readChains(root);
    const byEntry = Object.fromEntries(chains.map((c) => [c.entry as string, c]));
    expect(Object.keys(byEntry).sort()).toEqual([
      "consumer:billing:emails",
      "consumer:billing:invoice.created",
      "page:web:/customers/:id",
      "page:web:/customers/new",
      "page:web:/invoices/new",
      "route:billing:GET /health",
      "route:billing:POST /billing/invoices/:id/pay",
    ]);

    // UI -> API, DI hop (this.customers.create)
    expect(byEntry["page:web:/customers/new"]).toMatchObject({ routeKey: "api:POST /api/customers", complete: true, confidence: "exact" });
    // UI -> API, new-member hop (new CustomerService().find)
    expect(byEntry["page:web:/customers/:id"]).toMatchObject({ routeKey: "api:GET /api/customers/:id", complete: true, confidence: "exact" });
    // UI -> billing: only matches through the /billing mount + the billingApi axios instance
    expect(byEntry["page:web:/invoices/new"]).toMatchObject({ routeKey: "billing:POST /billing/invoices", complete: true, confidence: "exact" });

    expect(byEntry["route:billing:POST /billing/invoices/:id/pay"]).toMatchObject({ complete: true, confidence: "exact" });
    expect(byEntry["route:billing:GET /health"]).toMatchObject({ complete: false }); // no db/queue hit: check's "no-store"

    expect(byEntry["consumer:billing:invoice.created"]).toMatchObject({ complete: false }); // ack-only, no store
    expect(byEntry["consumer:billing:emails"]).toMatchObject({ complete: true, confidence: "exact" }); // named handler -> email_logs insert
  });

  it("matches the invoices chain through the /billing mount and the object-literal service", async () => {
    const root = workspace();
    await runIndex(root);
    const { chains } = readChains(root);
    const invoiceChain = chains.find((c) => c.entry === "page:web:/invoices/new")! as { stops: Array<Record<string, unknown>> };
    const route = invoiceChain.stops.find((s) => s.kind === "route")!;
    expect(route).toMatchObject({ label: "POST /billing/invoices", hop: { confidence: "exact" } });
    const db = invoiceChain.stops.filter((s) => s.lane === "db");
    expect(db).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ label: "invoices", op: "insert" })]);
    const handler = invoiceChain.stops.find((s) => s.kind === "handler")!;
    expect(handler).toMatchObject({ symbol: "createInvoice" });
  });

  it("creates an empty journeys.json alongside chains.json", async () => {
    const root = workspace();
    await runIndex(root);
    expect(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, ".codeloom", "journeys.json"), "utf8"))).toEqual({ version: 1, journeys: [] });
  });

  it("is byte-identical on a second run with no code change", async () => {
    const root = workspace();
    await runIndex(root);
    const chainsBefore = readFileSync(join(root, ".codeloom", "chains.json"), "utf8");
    const journeysBefore = readFileSync(join(root, ".codeloom", "journeys.json"), "utf8");
    await runIndex(root);
    expect(readFileSync(join(root, ".codeloom", "chains.json"), "utf8")).toBe(chainsBefore);
    expect(readFileSync(join(root, ".codeloom", "journeys.json"), "utf8")).toBe(journeysBefore);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formatIndexSummary, runIndex } from "../src/commands/index-cmd.js";
import { materializeFixture } from "./helpers.js";

function workspace() {
  const root = materializeFixture("demo-shop");
  writeFileSync(
    join(root, "codeloom.workspace.json"),
    JSON.stringify({ version: 1, name: "demo-shop", repos: [{ id: "api", path: "api" }, { id: "billing", path: "billing" }, { id: "web", path: "web" }], links: [] }),
  );
  return root;
}

describe("codeloom index on demo-shop", () => {
  it("finds pages, calls, routes, tables, DB access and queues in every repo", async () => {
    const result = await runIndex(workspace());
    const ep = Object.fromEntries(result.repos.map((r) => [r.id, r.endpoints]));

    expect(ep.web!.pages.map((p) => p.route)).toEqual(["/customers/:id", "/customers/new", "/invoices/new"]);
    expect(ep.web!.httpCalls).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(ep.web!.httpCalls.map((c) => `${c.method} ${c.path} @${c.at.symbol}`)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      "GET /api/customers/:id @getCustomer",
      "POST /api/customers @useCreateCustomer",
      "POST /billing/invoices @createInvoice",
    ]));

    expect(ep.api!.routes.map((r) => `${r.method} ${r.path} -> ${r.handler}`)).toEqual([
      "POST /api/customers -> CustomersController.store",
      "GET /api/customers/:id -> CustomersController.show",
    ]);
    expect(ep.api!.dbAccess.map((a) => `${a.op} ${a.table} @${a.at.symbol}`)).toEqual([
      "insert customers @CustomerService.create",
      "insert audit_logs @CustomerService.create",
      "read customers @CustomerService.find",
    ]);
    expect(ep.api!.tables.map((t) => t.name)).toEqual(["audit_logs", "customers"]);

    expect(ep.billing!.routes.map((r) => `${r.method} ${r.path} -> ${r.handler}`)).toEqual([
      "POST /invoices -> createInvoice",
      "POST /invoices/:id/pay -> payInvoice",
      "GET /health -> GET /health",
    ]);
    expect(ep.billing!.dbAccess).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(ep.billing!.dbAccess.map((a) => `${a.op} ${a.table} @${a.at.symbol}`)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      "insert invoices @invoiceService.create",
      "update invoices @invoiceService.markPaid",
      "insert email_logs @handleEmail",
    ]));
    expect(ep.billing!.queues).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(ep.billing!.queues.map((q) => `${q.op} ${q.queue}`)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      "publish invoice.created",
      "consume invoice.created",
      "publish billing:invoice.paid",
      "bind emails",
      "consume emails",
    ]));
    // Plain code-point order (not locale order): uppercase ASCII, then lowercase "e", then lowercase "i".
    expect(ep.billing!.tables.map((t) => t.name)).toEqual(["Payment", "email_logs", "invoices"]);
  });

  it("links files through package.json import aliases", async () => {
    const root = workspace();
    const result = await runIndex(root);
    const api = result.repos.find((r) => r.id === "api")!;
    expect(api.stats.skipped).toEqual([]);
    const { readFileSync } = await import("node:fs");
    const graph = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, "api/.codeloom/graph.json"), "utf8"));
    expect(graph.edges).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      expect.objectContaining({ type: "imports", source: "file:app/controllers/customers_controller.ts", target: "file:app/services/customer_service.ts" }),
    ]));
  });

  it("locates route handlers in their files", async () => {
    const result = await runIndex(workspace());
    const routes = Object.fromEntries(result.repos.map((r) => [r.id, r.endpoints.routes]));
    const store = routes.api!.find((r) => r.handler === "CustomersController.store")!;
    expect(store.handlerFile).toBe("app/controllers/customers_controller.ts");
    expect(routes.api!.find((r) => r.handler === "CustomersController.show")!.handlerFile).toBe("app/controllers/customers_controller.ts");
    const invoice = routes.billing!.find((r) => r.handler === "createInvoice")!;
    expect(invoice.handlerFile).toBe("src/invoices.ts");
    expect(invoice.handlerLines).toBeUndefined();
    const pay = routes.billing!.find((r) => r.handler === "payInvoice")!;
    expect(pay.handlerFile).toBe("src/invoices.ts");
    const health = routes.billing!.find((r) => r.path === "/health")!;
    expect(health).toMatchObject({ inline: true, handlerFile: "src/server.ts" });
    for (const r of [...routes.api!, ...routes.billing!]) expect(r).not.toHaveProperty("handlerImport");
  });

  it("prints a readable summary, including the linker's chain count", async () => {
    const result = await runIndex(workspace());
    const text = formatIndexSummary(result);
    expect(text).toContain("api");
    expect(text).toMatch(/routes/);
    expect(text).toMatch(/3 repos indexed/);
    expect(text).toMatch(/7 chains \(\d+ complete\) written to \.codeloom\/chains\.json/);
  });

  it("returns a linker summary alongside each repo's stats", async () => {
    const result = await runIndex(workspace());
    expect(result.linker.chains).toBe(7);
    expect(result.linker.wrote).toBe(true);
    expect(result.linker.orphanedJourneys).toBe(0);
  });
});
```
