- [ ] **Step 9: Implementation — `linker/run.ts` and `commands/index-cmd.ts`**

`packages/cli/src/linker/run.ts` (new):
```ts
import { createCallResolver } from "./resolve-calls.js";
import { linkAll } from "./link.js";
import { buildChains, sortChains, syncJourneys, writeChains } from "./chains.js";
import type { IndexResult } from "../indexer/index-repo.js";
import type { RepoData, WorkspaceData } from "../store/read.js";
import type { RepoRef, TracesConfig, WorkspaceFile } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";

export interface LinkerSummary {
  chains: number;
  complete: number;
  orphanedJourneys: number;
  wrote: boolean;
}

export function runLinker(
  ws: { root: string; name: string; repos: RepoRef[]; links: WorkspaceFile["links"]; traces?: TracesConfig },
  indexed: Array<{ ref: RepoRef; abs: string; result: IndexResult }>,
): LinkerSummary {
  const repos: RepoData[] = indexed
    .map((x) => ({ ref: x.ref, abs: x.abs, graph: x.result.graph, endpoints: x.result.endpoints }))
    .sort((a, b) => compare(a.ref.id, b.ref.id));
  const wsData: WorkspaceData = { root: ws.root, name: ws.name, repos, links: ws.links, traces: ws.traces };
  const resolvers = new Map(
    indexed.map((x) => [
      x.ref.id,
      createCallResolver({ repo: x.ref.id, index: x.result.codeIndex, files: x.result.files, aliases: x.result.aliases }),
    ]),
  );
  const links = linkAll({ ws: wsData, resolvers });
  const chains = sortChains(buildChains(wsData, links));
  const wrote = writeChains(ws.root, chains);
  const sync = syncJourneys(ws.root, new Set(chains.map((c) => c.id)));
  return {
    chains: chains.length,
    complete: chains.filter((c) => c.complete).length,
    orphanedJourneys: sync.orphaned,
    wrote: wrote || sync.created || sync.changed,
  };
}
```

`packages/cli/src/commands/index-cmd.ts` (full new content, replacing the current file):
```ts
import { join } from "node:path";
import { type IndexStats, indexRepo } from "../indexer/index-repo.js";
import { runLinker } from "../linker/run.js";
import type { LinkerSummary } from "../linker/run.js";
import type { EndpointsFile, RepoRef } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { loadWorkspace } from "../workspace/repos.js";

export interface IndexRunResult {
  root: string;
  repos: Array<{ id: string; stats: IndexStats; endpoints: EndpointsFile }>;
  linker: LinkerSummary;
}

export async function runIndex(start: string): Promise<IndexRunResult> {
  const ws = loadWorkspace(start);
  const repos: IndexRunResult["repos"] = [];
  const indexed: Array<{ ref: RepoRef; abs: string; result: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof indexRepo>> }> = [];
  for (const repo of ws.repos) {
    const abs = join(ws.root, repo.path);
    const res = await indexRepo(abs, repo.id);
    repos.push({ id: repo.id, stats: res.stats, endpoints: res.endpoints });
    indexed.push({ ref: repo, abs, result: res });
  }
  const linker = runLinker(ws, indexed);
  return { root: ws.root, repos, linker };
}

export function formatIndexSummary(r: IndexRunResult): string {
  const header = ["repo", "files", "parsed", "reused", "pages", "calls", "routes", "tables", "queues", "skipped"];
  const rows = r.repos.map((x) => [
    x.id,
    x.stats.files,
    x.stats.parsed,
    x.stats.reused,
    x.endpoints.pages.length,
    x.endpoints.httpCalls.length,
    x.endpoints.routes.length,
    x.endpoints.tables.length,
    x.endpoints.queues.length,
    x.stats.skipped.length,
  ].map(String));
  const widths = header.map((h, i) => Math.max(h.length, ...rows.map((row) => row[i]!.length)));
  const line = (cells: string[]) => cells.map((c, i) => (i === 0 ? c.padEnd(widths[i]!) : c.padStart(widths[i]!))).join("  ");
  const skipped = r.repos.flatMap((x) => x.stats.skipped.map((s) => `  ${x.id}/${s.file}: ${s.reason}`));
  const orphanedNote = r.linker.orphanedJourneys > 0 ? [`${r.linker.orphanedJourneys} journeys orphaned (their chains no longer exist)`] : [];
  return [
    line(header),
    ...rows.map(line),
    "",
    `${r.repos.length} repos indexed in ${r.root}`,
    `${r.linker.chains} chains (${r.linker.complete} complete) written to .codeloom/chains.json`,
    ...orphanedNote,
    ...(skipped.length ? ["Skipped files:", ...skipped] : []),
  ].join("\n");
}
```

### Task 13: `codeloom check`

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/store/schemas.ts` (append `CheckItemKindSchema`, `CheckItemSchema`, `CheckReportSchema`)
- Create: `packages/cli/src/check/report.ts`
- Create: `packages/cli/src/check/format.ts`
- Create: `packages/cli/src/commands/check.ts`
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/cli.ts` (add the `check` command)
- Test: `packages/cli/test/check.test.ts`
- Test: `packages/cli/test/cli.test.ts` (one more `it` for the `check` command wiring)

**Interfaces (Interfaces Registry R13, the Interfaces Registry appendix of this plan):**
- Consumes: R12 `ChainsFileSchema`, `Chain`, `ChainStop` (`store/schemas.ts`); R5 `loadWorkspaceData`, `WorkspaceData`,
  `RepoData`, `dataError`, `CodeloomError` (`store/read.ts`, `errors.ts`); R9 `effectiveRoutes`, `routeKey`,
  `EffectiveRoute` (`linker/mounts.ts`); `readJson` (`store/json.ts`); `FILES`, `CODELOOM_DIR` (`store/paths.ts`);
  `compare` (`util.ts`).
- Produces: `CheckItemKindSchema`, `CheckItemSchema`, `CheckReportSchema`, `CheckItem`, `CheckReport`;
  `buildCheckReport(chains, ws)`, `completePercent(r)`; `formatCheckText(r)`, `formatCheckJson(r)`;
  `CheckOptions`, `runCheck(start, opts)`; the `codeloom check` CLI command.

**Design notes binding this task** (buildCheckReport reads only `chains.json` + each repo's `endpoints.json`
via `WorkspaceData` — it never re-runs the linker):
- `unresolved-url` items come straight from each repo's raw `endpoints.httpCalls` where `call.kind === "unknown"`
  (the URL has no literal text at all — memo (i) row "URLs that could not be read").
- `unmatched-call` items are a 1:1 copy of every chain's `unresolved` entries, whatever produced them (an
  internal call Task 11 could not resolve, or — by convention — a UI call Task 12 could not match to any route,
  recorded as an unresolved entry on a partial chain with `routeKey: ""`). The item's `detail` is the hop's
  `reason` untouched; `check` does not try to tell the two cases apart, since `CheckItemKindSchema` has no
  sub-kind for it (confirmed by reading the registry: six kinds, not seven).
- `route-no-caller` is computed independently of chains, directly from `effectiveRoutes(repoData.endpoints)` and
  `routeKey`, so a bug in Task 12 that silently drops a route-entry chain is still caught: a route is
  "no-caller" when no chain whose `entry` starts with `"page:"` carries that `routeKey`.
- `handler-not-found`: a chain whose last stop is still `kind: "route"` (the walk could not place a handler).
- `no-store`: a chain that reaches a `"handler"`, `"function"` or `"consume"` stop but no `"db"` or `"publish"`
  stop anywhere in `stops`.
- `guess-link`: one item per `ChainStop` whose `hop.confidence === "guess"`.
- `summary.uncalledRoutes === ` count of `route-no-caller` items; `summary.missingDb === ` count of `no-store`;
  `summary.brokenUiApi === ` count of `unresolved-url` + `handler-not-found`; `summary.unresolvedCalls === `
  count of `unmatched-call`. `byConfidence` tallies every `stop.hop.confidence` across every chain.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the check schemas**

Append to `packages/cli/src/store/schemas.ts` (after the Journey/JourneysFile types from Task 12):

```ts
export const CheckItemKindSchema = z.enum([
  "unmatched-call",
  "handler-not-found",
  "no-store",
  "unresolved-url",
  "route-no-caller",
  "guess-link",
]);
export type CheckItemKind = z.infer<typeof CheckItemKindSchema>;

export const CheckItemSchema = z.object({
  kind: CheckItemKindSchema,
  repo: z.string(),
  file: z.string(),
  line: z.number().int().positive(),
  symbol: z.string(),
  detail: z.string(),
});
export type CheckItem = z.infer<typeof CheckItemSchema>;

export const CheckReportSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(1),
  summary: z.object({
    chains: z.number().int(),
    complete: z.number().int(),
    missingDb: z.number().int(),
    brokenUiApi: z.number().int(),
    unresolvedCalls: z.number().int(),
    uncalledRoutes: z.number().int(),
    byConfidence: z.object({ exact: z.number().int(), param: z.number().int(), guess: z.number().int() }),
  }),
  items: z.array(CheckItemSchema),
  traces: z.unknown().optional(),
});
export type CheckReport = z.infer<typeof CheckReportSchema>;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `check/report.ts`**

`packages/cli/src/check/report.ts`:

```ts
import { effectiveRoutes, routeKey } from "../linker/mounts.js";
import type { RepoData, WorkspaceData } from "../store/read.js";
import type { Chain, ChainStop, CheckItem, CheckReport } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";

function routeStopLoc(chain: Chain): { repo: string; file: string; line: number; symbol: string } {
  const stop = chain.stops.find((s) => s.kind === "route") ?? chain.stops[0];
  if (!stop) return { repo: chain.entry.split(":")[1] ?? "", file: "", line: 1, symbol: chain.entry };
  return { repo: stop.repo, file: stop.file, line: stop.line, symbol: stop.symbol };
}

function lastStop(chain: Chain): ChainStop | undefined {
  return chain.stops[chain.stops.length - 1];
}

function hasStoreStop(chain: Chain): boolean {
  return chain.stops.some((s) => s.kind === "db" || s.kind === "publish");
}

function hasHandlerStop(chain: Chain): boolean {
  return chain.stops.some((s) => s.kind === "handler" || s.kind === "function" || s.kind === "consume");
}

function unresolvedUrlItems(repos: RepoData[]): CheckItem[] {
  const items: CheckItem[] = [];
  for (const repo of repos) {
    for (const call of repo.endpoints.httpCalls) {
      if (call.kind !== "unknown") continue;
      items.push({
        kind: "unresolved-url",
        repo: repo.ref.id,
        file: call.at.file,
        line: call.at.line,
        symbol: call.at.symbol,
        detail: `cannot read URL from ${call.raw}`,
      });
    }
  }
  return items;
}

function routeNoCallerItems(repos: RepoData[], chains: Chain[]): CheckItem[] {
  const pageRouteKeys = new Set(chains.filter((c) => c.entry.startsWith("page:")).map((c) => c.routeKey));
  const items: CheckItem[] = [];
  for (const repo of repos) {
    for (const r of effectiveRoutes(repo.endpoints)) {
      if (pageRouteKeys.has(routeKey(r))) continue;
      items.push({
        kind: "route-no-caller",
        repo: r.repo,
        file: r.route.at.file,
        line: r.route.at.line,
        symbol: r.route.handler,
        detail: `${r.route.method} ${r.effectivePath} has no UI caller`,
      });
    }
  }
  return items;
}
```

Continue the same file in the next step (chain-level items + `buildCheckReport` + `completePercent`).

```ts
function chainLevelItems(chain: Chain): CheckItem[] {
  const items: CheckItem[] = [];
  for (const u of chain.unresolved) {
    items.push({ kind: "unmatched-call", repo: u.at.repo, file: u.at.file, line: u.at.line, symbol: u.at.symbol, detail: u.reason });
  }
  for (const stop of chain.stops) {
    if (stop.hop.confidence !== "guess") continue;
    items.push({
      kind: "guess-link",
      repo: stop.repo,
      file: stop.file,
      line: stop.line,
      symbol: stop.symbol,
      detail: `${stop.hop.reason ?? "guessed match"} (${chain.name})`,
    });
  }
  const last = lastStop(chain);
  if (last && last.kind === "route") {
    const loc = routeStopLoc(chain);
    items.push({ kind: "handler-not-found", repo: loc.repo, file: loc.file, line: loc.line, symbol: loc.symbol, detail: `${chain.name}: handler could not be located` });
  } else if (last && hasHandlerStop(chain) && !hasStoreStop(chain)) {
    items.push({ kind: "no-store", repo: last.repo, file: last.file, line: last.line, symbol: last.symbol, detail: `${chain.name} reaches no database table or queue` });
  }
  return items;
}

function sortItems(items: CheckItem[]): CheckItem[] {
  return [...items].sort(
    (a, b) =>
      compare(a.kind, b.kind) ||
      compare(a.repo, b.repo) ||
      compare(a.file, b.file) ||
      a.line - b.line ||
      compare(a.detail, b.detail),
  );
}

export function buildCheckReport(chains: Chain[], ws: WorkspaceData): CheckReport {
  const items = sortItems([
    ...unresolvedUrlItems(ws.repos),
    ...routeNoCallerItems(ws.repos, chains),
    ...chains.flatMap(chainLevelItems),
  ]);

  const byConfidence = { exact: 0, param: 0, guess: 0 };
  for (const chain of chains) for (const stop of chain.stops) byConfidence[stop.hop.confidence]++;

  const count = (kind: CheckItem["kind"]) => items.filter((i) => i.kind === kind).length;

  return {
    version: 1,
    summary: {
      chains: chains.length,
      complete: chains.filter((c) => c.complete).length,
      missingDb: count("no-store"),
      brokenUiApi: count("unresolved-url") + count("handler-not-found"),
      unresolvedCalls: count("unmatched-call"),
      uncalledRoutes: count("route-no-caller"),
      byConfidence,
    },
    items,
  };
}

export function completePercent(r: CheckReport): number {
  if (r.summary.chains === 0) return 100;
  return Math.round((r.summary.complete / r.summary.chains) * 100);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement `check/format.ts`**

`packages/cli/src/check/format.ts`:

```ts
import type { CheckItem, CheckReport } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { completePercent } from "./report.js";

const KIND_LABEL: Record<CheckItem["kind"], string> = {
  "unmatched-call": "Calls that could not be resolved",
  "handler-not-found": "Routes whose handler could not be found",
  "no-store": "Routes or consumers that reach no database table or queue",
  "unresolved-url": "URLs that could not be read",
  "route-no-caller": "Routes with no UI caller",
  "guess-link": "Hops matched only by a guess",
};

// Same order as CheckItemKindSchema's declared enum (registry R13), so the grouped text output and
// the raw `items` array (sorted by kind with `compare`, which is NOT this order) read differently on
// purpose: text groups read UI → API → DB top to bottom; `items`/JSON stay in plain code-point order.
const KIND_ORDER: Array<CheckItem["kind"]> = [
  "unmatched-call",
  "handler-not-found",
  "no-store",
  "unresolved-url",
  "route-no-caller",
  "guess-link",
];

function summaryLine(r: CheckReport): string {
  const { chains, complete, missingDb, brokenUiApi } = r.summary;
  if (chains === 0) return "0 journeys: nothing indexed yet.";
  const parts = [`${complete} complete UI → API → DB`];
  if (missingDb > 0) parts.push(`${missingDb} missing the DB step`);
  if (brokenUiApi > 0) parts.push(`${brokenUiApi} broken at UI → API`);
  const other = chains - complete - missingDb - brokenUiApi;
  if (other > 0) parts.push(`${other} other`);
  return `${chains} journey${chains === 1 ? "" : "s"}: ${parts.join(", ")}`;
}

export function formatCheckText(r: CheckReport): string {
  const lines = [summaryLine(r), `complete: ${completePercent(r)}%`, ""];
  for (const kind of KIND_ORDER) {
    const group = r.items.filter((i) => i.kind === kind);
    if (group.length === 0) continue;
    lines.push(`${KIND_LABEL[kind]} (${group.length}):`);
    for (const item of group) lines.push(`  ${item.repo}/${item.file}:${item.line} ${item.symbol} — ${item.detail}`);
    lines.push("");
  }
  if (r.items.length === 0) lines.push("No issues found.");
  return lines.join("\n").trimEnd();
}

export function formatCheckJson(r: CheckReport): string {
  return JSON.stringify(r, null, 2);
}
```

```ts
import { writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { runCheck } from "../src/commands/check.js";
import { CodeloomError } from "../src/errors.js";
import { materializeFixture } from "./helpers.js";
import { runIndex } from "../src/commands/index-cmd.js";

function demoShopWorkspace() {
  const root = materializeFixture("demo-shop");
  writeFileSync(
    join(root, "codeloom.workspace.json"),
    JSON.stringify({ version: 1, name: "demo-shop", repos: [{ id: "api", path: "api" }, { id: "billing", path: "billing" }, { id: "web", path: "web" }], links: [] }),
  );
  return root;
}

describe("runCheck", () => {
  it("reports exit code 0 on demo-shop with no --fail-under", async () => {
    const root = demoShopWorkspace();
    await runIndex(root);
    const { output, exitCode, report } = runCheck(root, {});
    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    expect(output).toMatch(/journeys?:/);
    expect(report.summary.chains).toBe(7);
    // Registry "Fixture changes" snapshot: no guess links; the pay route and /health have no UI caller.
    expect(report.items.filter((i) => i.kind === "guess-link")).toEqual([]);
    expect(report.items.filter((i) => i.kind === "route-no-caller").map((i) => i.detail)).toEqual([
      "POST /billing/invoices/:id/pay has no UI caller",
      "GET /health has no UI caller",
    ]);
  });

  it("prints JSON with --json", async () => {
    const root = demoShopWorkspace();
    await runIndex(root);
    const { output } = runCheck(root, { json: true });
    expect(JSON.parse(output).version).toBe(1);
  });

  it("exits 1 when complete% is below --fail-under", async () => {
    const root = demoShopWorkspace();
    await runIndex(root);
    const { exitCode } = runCheck(root, { failUnder: "100" });
    expect(exitCode).toBe(1);
  });

  it("rejects an out-of-range --fail-under with a data error (exit 2)", async () => {
    const root = demoShopWorkspace();
    await runIndex(root);
    try {
      runCheck(root, { failUnder: "101" });
      throw new Error("should have thrown");
    } catch (err) {
      expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(CodeloomError);
      expect((err as CodeloomError).exitCode).toBe(2);
    }
  });

  it("fails with exit 2 when chains.json is missing", () => {
    const root = demoShopWorkspace();
    expect(() => runCheck(root, {})).toThrow(/Run "codeloom index" first/);
    try {
      runCheck(root, {});
    } catch (err) {
      expect((err as CodeloomError).exitCode).toBe(2);
    }
  });

  it("fails with exit 2 and a re-index message on a v1 endpoints.json", async () => {
    const root = demoShopWorkspace();
    await runIndex(root);
    const epPath = join(root, "api/.codeloom/endpoints.json");
    const ep = JSON.parse(readFileSync(epPath, "utf8"));
    ep.version = 1;
    writeFileSync(epPath, JSON.stringify(ep));
    expect(() => runCheck(root, {})).toThrow(/endpoints version 1/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 12: Implement `commands/check.ts`**

`packages/cli/src/commands/check.ts`:

```ts
import { join } from "node:path";
import { formatCheckJson, formatCheckText } from "../check/format.js";
import { buildCheckReport, completePercent } from "../check/report.js";
import { dataError } from "../errors.js";
import { readJson } from "../store/json.js";
import { CODELOOM_DIR, FILES } from "../store/paths.js";
import { loadWorkspaceData } from "../store/read.js";
import { ChainsFileSchema, type CheckReport } from "../store/schemas.js";

export interface CheckOptions {
  json?: boolean;
  failUnder?: string;
}

function parseFailUnder(raw: string | undefined): number | undefined {
  if (raw === undefined) return undefined;
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(raw) || Number(raw) > 100) {
    throw dataError(`--fail-under must be a whole number between 0 and 100, got "${raw}".`);
  }
  return Number(raw);
}

export function runCheck(start: string, opts: CheckOptions): { report: CheckReport; output: string; exitCode: 0 | 1 } {
  const failUnder = parseFailUnder(opts.failUnder);
  const ws = loadWorkspaceData(start);
  const chainsPath = join(ws.root, CODELOOM_DIR, FILES.chains);
  const chainsFile = readJson(chainsPath, ChainsFileSchema);
  if (!chainsFile) throw dataError(`${chainsPath} not found. Run "codeloom index" first.`);

  const report = buildCheckReport(chainsFile.chains, ws);
  const output = opts.json ? formatCheckJson(report) : formatCheckText(report);
  const exitCode: 0 | 1 = failUnder !== undefined && completePercent(report) < failUnder ? 1 : 0;
  return { report, output, exitCode };
}
```

`loadWorkspaceData` (R5, built in Task 5) already throws a `CodeloomError` with `exitCode: 2` for a missing
graph/endpoints file and for a `version: 1` `endpoints.json` (the exact re-index message from R5); this task
does not duplicate that check. `dataError` (also R5) defaults `exitCode` to 2, so both failure paths here —
a bad `--fail-under` and a missing `chains.json` — exit 2, matching the Global Constraint
("`2` = data or usage error").

### Task 14: Answer-key format, scorer, demo-shop key

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/store/schemas.ts` (append `ExpectedHopSchema`, `ExpectedJourneysSchema`)
- Create: `packages/cli/src/evals/score.ts`
- Create: `fixtures/demo-shop/expected-journeys.json`
- Test: `packages/cli/test/score.test.ts`

**Interfaces (Interfaces Registry R14, the Interfaces Registry appendix of this plan):**
- Consumes: R12 `Chain`, `ChainStop`, `ChainsFileSchema` (`store/schemas.ts`); R1 `HttpMethodSchema`,
  `ConfidenceSchema`, `Confidence` (`store/schemas.ts`); `readJson` (`store/json.ts`); `compare` (`util.ts`).
- Produces: `ExpectedHopSchema`, `ExpectedHop`, `ExpectedJourneysSchema`, `ExpectedJourneys`; `FoundHop`,
  `hopsFromChains(chains)`, `hopKey(h)`, `HopTypeScore`, `ScoreReport`, `scoreChains(key, chains)`,
  `formatScore(r)`.

**Design notes binding this task** (how a `Chain`'s `stops` map to the three `ExpectedHop` shapes — read
alongside R12's `ChainStop`/`Chain` definitions and the demo-shop `ChainStop` fields they set):
- `routeKey` (R9 format `"<repo>:<METHOD effectivePath>"`) is stored verbatim on every non-consumer `Chain` as
  `chain.routeKey`; for a consumer chain `chain.routeKey === ""` and `chain.entry` is
  `"consumer:<repo>:<queue>"` — use `chain.routeKey || chain.entry` as the `route` string on every
  `handler-store` hop, so both forms line up with the answer-key's `route` field with no special-casing.
- `ui-api` hop: a `"call"` stop immediately followed by a `"route"` stop in the same chain. `from` = the call
  stop's `{ repo, file, symbol }`; `to.repo` = the route stop's `repo`, `to.method` = the route stop's `op`
  (a `HttpMethod`, per `ChainStop.op`'s doc "route/call: HTTP method"), `to.path` = the route stop's `label`
  with the `"${method} "` prefix stripped (`label` is `"effective METHOD /path"` per `ChainStop.label`'s doc).
- `route-handler` hop: a `"route"` stop immediately followed by a `"handler"` stop. `route` =
  `chain.routeKey`; `handler` = the handler stop's `{ file, symbol }`.
- `handler-store` hop: every `"db"`, `"publish"` or `"consume"` stop in the chain (in walk order). `route` =
  `chain.routeKey || chain.entry`; `table` = the stop's `label` when `stop.kind === "db"`; `queue` = the
  stop's `label` when `stop.kind === "publish"` or `"consume"`; `op` = the stop's `op` (`DbOp` for `"db"`,
  `"publish"`/`"consume"` literal otherwise, matching `ExpectedHop`'s `handler-store.op: string`).
- A hop's `confidence` (for `FoundHop`) is the **lower** of the two stops' `hop.confidence` that produced it
  (the stop pair's own hop confidences, via `CONFIDENCE_RANK` below) — `ui-api`/`route-handler` use the
  second stop's `hop.confidence` only when the first stop's is not lower (same rule, just worded for a pair);
  `handler-store` uses the db/publish/consume stop's own `hop.confidence`.
- [ ] **Step 1: Add the answer-key schemas**

Append to `packages/cli/src/store/schemas.ts` (after `CheckReportSchema` from Task 13):

```ts
export const ExpectedHopSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("ui-api"),
    from: z.object({ repo: z.string(), file: z.string(), symbol: z.string() }),
    to: z.object({ repo: z.string(), method: HttpMethodSchema, path: z.string() }),
  }),
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("route-handler"),
    route: z.string(),
    handler: z.object({ file: z.string(), symbol: z.string() }),
  }),
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("handler-store"),
    route: z.string(),
    table: z.string().optional(),
    queue: z.string().optional(),
    op: z.string(),
  }),
]);
export type ExpectedHop = z.infer<typeof ExpectedHopSchema>;

export const ExpectedJourneysSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(1),
  source: z.object({ url: z.string(), sha: z.string(), licence: z.string() }),
  hops: z.array(ExpectedHopSchema),
});
export type ExpectedJourneys = z.infer<typeof ExpectedJourneysSchema>;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `evals/score.ts` — `hopKey` and `hopsFromChains`**

`packages/cli/src/evals/score.ts`:

```ts
import type { Chain, ChainStop, Confidence, ExpectedHop, HttpMethod } from "../store/schemas.js";
import { compare } from "../util.js";

export interface FoundHop {
  hop: ExpectedHop;
  confidence: Confidence;
}

export function hopKey(h: ExpectedHop): string {
  switch (h.type) {
    case "ui-api":
      return `ui-api|${h.from.repo}|${h.from.file}|${h.from.symbol}|${h.to.repo}|${h.to.method}|${h.to.path}`;
    case "route-handler":
      return `route-handler|${h.route}|${h.handler.file}|${h.handler.symbol}`;
    case "handler-store":
      return `handler-store|${h.route}|${h.table ?? ""}|${h.queue ?? ""}|${h.op}`;
  }
}

const CONFIDENCE_RANK: Record<Confidence, number> = { exact: 0, param: 1, guess: 2 };

function lowerConfidence(a: Confidence, b: Confidence): Confidence {
  return CONFIDENCE_RANK[a] >= CONFIDENCE_RANK[b] ? a : b;
}

/** Route/call stops store the HTTP method twice: `op` is the bare method, `label` is `"<METHOD> <path>"`.
 * This strips the method back off `label` to recover the path alone. */
function pathFromLabel(stop: ChainStop): string {
  const method = stop.op ?? "";
  return (stop.label ?? "").slice(method.length + 1);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `hopsFromChains`**

Append to `packages/cli/src/evals/score.ts`:

```ts
export function hopsFromChains(chains: Chain[]): FoundHop[] {
  const sorted = [...chains].sort((a, b) => compare(a.id, b.id));
  const found = new Map<string, FoundHop>();
  const add = (hop: ExpectedHop, confidence: Confidence) => {
    const key = hopKey(hop);
    if (!found.has(key)) found.set(key, { hop, confidence });
  };

  for (const chain of sorted) {
    const route = chain.routeKey || chain.entry;
    for (let i = 0; i < chain.stops.length; i++) {
      const stop = chain.stops[i]!;
      const next = chain.stops[i + 1];

      if (stop.kind === "call" && next?.kind === "route") {
        add(
          {
            type: "ui-api",
            from: { repo: stop.repo, file: stop.file, symbol: stop.symbol },
            to: { repo: next.repo, method: next.op as HttpMethod, path: pathFromLabel(next) },
          },
          lowerConfidence(stop.hop.confidence, next.hop.confidence),
        );
      }
      if (stop.kind === "route" && next?.kind === "handler") {
        add(
          { type: "route-handler", route: chain.routeKey, handler: { file: next.file, symbol: next.symbol } },
          lowerConfidence(stop.hop.confidence, next.hop.confidence),
        );
      }
      if (stop.kind === "db") {
        add({ type: "handler-store", route, table: stop.label, op: stop.op! }, stop.hop.confidence);
      }
      if (stop.kind === "publish" || stop.kind === "consume") {
        add({ type: "handler-store", route, queue: stop.label, op: stop.op! }, stop.hop.confidence);
      }
    }
  }
  return [...found.values()];
}
```

```ts
    const routeChain = fakeChain({ id: "ch_000000000002", entry: "route:api:POST /api/x", routeKey: "api:POST /api/x", stops: [routeStopPost, handlerStop, dbStop()] });
    const score = scoreChains(key, [uiChain, routeChain]);
    for (const type of ["ui-api", "route-handler", "handler-store"] as const) {
      expect(score[type].precision).toBe(1);
      expect(score[type].recall).toBe(1);
      expect(score[type].guesses).toBe(0);
      expect(score[type].missing).toEqual([]);
      expect(score[type].extra).toEqual([]);
    }
  });

  it("counts an extra found hop against precision and lists its key", () => {
    const uiChain = fakeChain({ id: "ch_000000000001", entry: "page:web:/x", routeKey: "api:GET /api/x", stops: [callStop, routeStop] });
    const extraDbChain = fakeChain({ id: "ch_000000000003", entry: "route:api:DELETE /api/x", routeKey: "api:DELETE /api/x", stops: [dbStop({ repo: "api", label: "y", file: "app/services/y_service.ts", symbol: "Y.destroy" })] });
    const score = scoreChains(key, [uiChain, extraDbChain]);
    expect(score["handler-store"].expected).toBe(1);
    expect(score["handler-store"].found).toBe(1);
    expect(score["handler-store"].truePositives).toBe(0);
    expect(score["handler-store"].precision).toBe(0);
    expect(score["handler-store"].extra).toEqual([hopKey({ type: "handler-store", route: "api:DELETE /api/x", table: "y", op: "insert" })]);
  });

  it("counts a missing expected hop against recall and lists its key", () => {
    const score = scoreChains(key, []);
    expect(score["route-handler"].found).toBe(0);
    expect(score["route-handler"].recall).toBe(0);
    expect(score["route-handler"].missing).toEqual([hopKey(key.hops[1]!)]);
  });

  it("reports a guess hop separately: not counted as found, not in missing/extra", () => {
    const guessRoute: ChainStop = { ...routeStop, hop: { confidence: "guess", reason: "suffix match" } };
    const uiChain = fakeChain({ id: "ch_000000000001", entry: "page:web:/x", routeKey: "api:GET /api/x", stops: [callStop, guessRoute] });
    const score = scoreChains(key, [uiChain]);
    expect(score["ui-api"].found).toBe(0);
    expect(score["ui-api"].guesses).toBe(1);
    expect(score["ui-api"].guessCorrect).toBe(1);
    expect(score["ui-api"].recall).toBe(0);
    expect(score["ui-api"].missing).toEqual([hopKey(key.hops[0]!)]);
  });

  it("treats a zero denominator as a perfect score (1), not a division error", () => {
    const score = scoreChains({ ...key, hops: [] }, []);
    expect(score["ui-api"]).toMatchObject({ expected: 0, found: 0, precision: 1, recall: 1 });
  });
});

describe("formatScore", () => {
  it("prints a table with one row per hop type", () => {
    const score = scoreChains(scoreKey, []);
    const text = formatScore(score);
    expect(text).toContain("ui-api");
    expect(text).toContain("route-handler");
    expect(text).toContain("handler-store");
  });
});
```

to the top of `score.test.ts` (merge with the existing imports from Step 2; `Chain`/`ChainStop` and `hopKey`
are now used across the whole file, not just the first `describe`).

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement `scoreChains` and `formatScore`**

Append to `packages/cli/src/evals/score.ts`:

```ts
export interface HopTypeScore {
  expected: number;
  found: number;
  truePositives: number;
  precision: number;
  recall: number;
  guesses: number;
  guessCorrect: number;
  missing: string[];
  extra: string[];
}
export type ScoreReport = Record<ExpectedHop["type"], HopTypeScore>;

const HOP_TYPES: Array<ExpectedHop["type"]> = ["ui-api", "route-handler", "handler-store"];

export function scoreChains(key: ExpectedJourneys, chains: Chain[]): ScoreReport {
  const allFound = hopsFromChains(chains);
  const report = {} as ScoreReport;

  for (const type of HOP_TYPES) {
    const expectedHops = key.hops.filter((h) => h.type === type);
    const expectedKeys = new Set(expectedHops.map(hopKey));
    const foundForType = allFound.filter((f) => f.hop.type === type);
    const counted = foundForType.filter((f) => f.confidence !== "guess");
    const guesses = foundForType.filter((f) => f.confidence === "guess");

    const countedKeys = counted.map((f) => hopKey(f.hop));
    const truePositives = countedKeys.filter((k) => expectedKeys.has(k)).length;
    const guessCorrect = guesses.filter((f) => expectedKeys.has(hopKey(f.hop))).length;

    const missing = [...expectedKeys].filter((k) => !countedKeys.includes(k)).sort(compare);
    const extra = countedKeys.filter((k) => !expectedKeys.has(k)).sort(compare);

    report[type] = {
      expected: expectedHops.length,
      found: counted.length,
      truePositives,
      precision: counted.length === 0 ? 1 : truePositives / counted.length,
      recall: expectedHops.length === 0 ? 1 : truePositives / expectedHops.length,
      guesses: guesses.length,
      guessCorrect,
      missing,
      extra,
    };
  }
  return report;
}

function pct(n: number): string {
  return `${Math.round(n * 100)}%`;
}

export function formatScore(r: ScoreReport): string {
  const header = ["hop type", "expected", "found", "precision", "recall", "guesses"];
  const rows = HOP_TYPES.map((type) => {
    const s = r[type];
    return [type, s.expected, s.found, pct(s.precision), pct(s.recall), s.guesses].map(String);
  });
  const widths = header.map((h, i) => Math.max(h.length, ...rows.map((row) => row[i]!.length)));
  const line = (cells: string[]) => cells.map((c, i) => c.padEnd(widths[i]!)).join("  ");
  return [line(header), ...rows.map(line)].join("\n");
}
```

```ts
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { runIndex } from "../src/commands/index-cmd.js";
import { ChainsFileSchema, ExpectedJourneysSchema } from "../src/store/schemas.js";
import { CODELOOM_DIR, FILES } from "../src/store/paths.js";
import { materializeFixture } from "./helpers.js";

describe("demo-shop answer key", () => {
  it("scores precision 1.0 / recall 1.0 on every hop type, with zero guesses", async () => {
    const root = materializeFixture("demo-shop");
    writeFileSync(
      join(root, "codeloom.workspace.json"),
      JSON.stringify({ version: 1, name: "demo-shop", repos: [{ id: "api", path: "api" }, { id: "billing", path: "billing" }, { id: "web", path: "web" }], links: [] }),
    );
    await runIndex(root);
    const chainsFile = ChainsFileSchema.parse(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, CODELOOM_DIR, FILES.chains), "utf8")));
    const key = ExpectedJourneysSchema.parse(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, "expected-journeys.json"), "utf8")));

    const score = scoreChains(key, chainsFile.chains);
    for (const type of ["ui-api", "route-handler", "handler-store"] as const) {
      expect(score[type], `${type}: ${JSON.stringify(score[type])}`).toMatchObject({ precision: 1, recall: 1, guesses: 0 });
    }
  });
});
```

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/cli/src/evals/score.ts` (add `EVAL_THRESHOLDS`)
- Modify: `packages/cli/test/helpers.ts` (add `materializeEval`)

  `LICENSE`, `SOURCE.md`, `expected-journeys.json`, `codeloom.workspace.json`, one sub-folder per repo

**Interfaces (Interfaces Registry R15, the Interfaces Registry appendix of this plan):**
- Consumes: R14 `ExpectedJourneysSchema`, `scoreChains`, `formatScore` (`evals/score.ts`); R12 `runIndex`,
  `ChainsFileSchema` (`commands/index-cmd.ts`, `store/schemas.ts`); `materializeFixture` (`test/helpers.ts`).
- Produces: `EVAL_THRESHOLDS`, `materializeEval(app)`.

**Thresholds:** `EVAL_THRESHOLDS` is `{ precision: 0.95, recall: 0.8 }` for every hop type — the v0.1 target in
spec §7a.2 ("precision ≥ 95%, recall ≥ 80%"). An earlier registry draft said 0.9; the registry appendix now says 0.95.

```bash
git -C "$TMPCLONE/<app-id>" rev-parse HEAD   # record this exact output as "sha" in SOURCE.md
```

```bash
git -C "$TMPCLONE/prisma-rest-nextjs" sparse-checkout set orm/typescript/rest-nextjs-api-routes LICENSE
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write each app's `expected-journeys.json` — rules**

Write `fixtures/evals/<app>/expected-journeys.json` by hand, in the `ExpectedJourneysSchema` shape (Task 14),
`source` copied from `SOURCE.md`. Follow these rules exactly — they are what makes the key trustworthy
evidence rather than a guess at what the linker will find:

1. **Derive the endpoint list first, independently of the code.** For `realworld`/`realworld-nest`, use the
   public RealWorld API spec's endpoint table (method + path for every operation: register, login, get/update
   user, list/get/create/update/delete articles, favorite/unfavorite, comments, profiles, tags). For
   `prisma-rest-nextjs`, list every file under `pages/api/**` and its HTTP method(s) from the file itself
   (Next.js API routes switch on `req.method`). This list is your ground truth for which routes exist —
   write it down before reading a single call site, so later steps can't bias which calls you go looking for.
2. **Verify every hop by reading the code**, not by assuming the framework's convention holds:
   - `ui-api`: open the UI's HTTP client wrapper (e.g. `agent.js`/`api.ts`) and every page/component that
     calls it; record the exact file and the function name that encloses the call (`LocSchema.symbol`
     semantics — the nearest function, not the file).
   - `route-handler`: open the router/controller file; record the file and symbol of the function that
     actually runs for that route (for Nest, `Controller.method`; for Express, the named handler function;
     for Next API routes, the default-exported handler function, named `"default"` if anonymous — check how
     Task 3's `detectRoutes` names anonymous default exports and match that exactly, since the key must use
     codeloom's own naming, not an invented one).
   - `handler-store`: follow the handler's calls into the service/repository layer until you hit the ORM call
     (Sequelize/TypeORM/Prisma) or (for RealWorld's classic Mongoose version, if that's what you vendor) the
     model call; record the table/collection name as it is actually declared in the model/schema file, and
     the queue name verbatim where messaging exists (none of the three listed apps publish to a queue — leave
     `handler-store` entries that type to `table`/`op` only; do not invent queue hops that are not in the
     code).
3. **`route` strings use the routeKey format** (`"<repo>:<METHOD> <effectivePath>"`, R9 `routeKey`, e.g.
   `"api:POST /api/articles"`), computed from the route's declared path plus whatever mount/router prefix the
   app actually applies (e.g. Express `app.use("/api", router)` → `effectivePath` gains the `/api` prefix even
   though the router file itself registers `router.post("/articles", ...)`). Get this wrong and every
   `route-handler`/`handler-store` hop for that route silently fails to match during scoring — double check
   against the real mount call in `app.ts`/`server.ts`/`main.ts`.
4. **Include hops codeloom is expected to miss, so recall is honest.** After writing the key from the code,
   run `codeloom index` + `codeloom check` on the materialized fixture once by hand (`node
   packages/cli/dist/bin.js index && node packages/cli/dist/bin.js check` inside the materialized temp dir —
   see `materializeEval`, Step 7) and read the `unmatched-call`/`route-no-caller`/`guess-link` items it
   reports. For each one that corresponds to a real hop in your ground-truth list from rule 1 (not a false
   alarm), keep it in `expected-journeys.json` anyway — recall is supposed to measure how much of the truth
   the linker finds, and a key that quietly drops the hard cases to make the score look better is dishonest.
   A concrete example you are likely to hit: dynamically built request paths (template literals with a
   variable segment the linker can't resolve to a literal, e.g. `` `/articles/${slug}/comments/${id}` `` built
   from a prop two components up) — these belong in the key as `ui-api` hops even if codeloom's `guess-link`
   or `unmatched-call` shows it currently misses them; that is exactly what keeps recall below 100% an honest
   number instead of a vacuous one.
5. **One `expected-journeys.json` entry per physical hop** — do not pre-deduplicate across chains yourself;
   `hopKey` (Task 14) already collapses duplicates on the scoring side. Order within the file does not matter
   (`hopKey` ignores key order inside each hop; the array order is whatever you wrote it in).

A trimmed worked example (RealWorld "create article", illustrating the exact shape — the real key has one
entry like this per endpoint in your Step-1 ground-truth list, not just this one):

```json
{
  "type": "ui-api",
  "from": { "repo": "ui", "file": "src/components/Editor/index.js", "symbol": "submitForm" },
  "to": { "repo": "api", "method": "POST", "path": "/api/articles" }
},
{
  "type": "route-handler",
  "route": "api:POST /api/articles",
  "handler": { "file": "routes/articles.js", "symbol": "default" }
},
{ "type": "handler-store", "route": "api:POST /api/articles", "table": "articles", "op": "insert" }
```
(File/symbol names above are illustrative of the *shape*; the committed key must use the real file paths and

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement `materializeEval` in `test/helpers.ts`**

Append to `packages/cli/test/helpers.ts` (it already has `git`, `commitAll`, `makeRepo`, `materializeFixture`
from Plan 1 — reuse `git`/`commitAll`, do not redefine them):

```ts
/** Copy fixtures/evals/<app> to a temp folder and git-init every repo its own codeloom.workspace.json lists
 * (unlike materializeFixture, which blindly git-inits every child folder — an eval app's root also holds
 * LICENSE/SOURCE.md/expected-journeys.json/codeloom.workspace.json, which are not repos). */
export function materializeEval(app: string): string {
  const src = fileURLToPath(new URL(`../../../fixtures/evals/${app}`, import.meta.url));
  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), `codeloom-eval-${app}-`));
  cpSync(src, root, { recursive: true });
  const ws = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, "codeloom.workspace.json"), "utf8")) as { repos: Array<{ path: string }> };
  for (const repo of ws.repos) {
    const repoAbs = join(root, repo.path);
    git(repoAbs, "init", "-q", "-b", "main");
    commitAll(repoAbs);
  }
  return root;
}
```

`git` is currently a module-private `const` in `test/helpers.ts` (not exported) — since `materializeEval`
lives in the same file, it can call it directly; no export change is needed. Add `readFileSync` to that
file's existing `node:fs` import line (it currently imports `cpSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readdirSync,
writeFileSync` — add `readFileSync` to that list).

```ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { runIndex } from "../src/commands/index-cmd.js";
import { EVAL_THRESHOLDS, formatScore, scoreChains } from "../src/evals/score.js";
import { CODELOOM_DIR, FILES } from "../src/store/paths.js";
import { ChainsFileSchema, ExpectedJourneysSchema, type ExpectedHop } from "../src/store/schemas.js";
import { materializeEval } from "./helpers.js";

const EVALS_DIR = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../fixtures/evals", import.meta.url));

function listApps(): string[] {
  return readdirSync(EVALS_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
    .filter((e) => e.isDirectory())
    .map((e) => e.name)
    .sort();
}

describe("vendored eval apps meet the precision/recall gate", () => {
  const apps = listApps();

  it("has at least one vendored eval app", () => {
    expect(apps.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  for (const app of apps) {
    it(`${app}: precision/recall meet EVAL_THRESHOLDS for every hop type`, async () => {
      const root = materializeEval(app);
      await runIndex(root);
      const chainsFile = ChainsFileSchema.parse(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, CODELOOM_DIR, FILES.chains), "utf8")));
      const key = ExpectedJourneysSchema.parse(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(EVALS_DIR, app, "expected-journeys.json"), "utf8")));

      const score = scoreChains(key, chainsFile.chains);
      console.log(`\n${app}\n${formatScore(score)}`);

      for (const type of Object.keys(EVAL_THRESHOLDS) as Array<ExpectedHop["type"]>) {
        const s = score[type];
        const t = EVAL_THRESHOLDS[type];
        expect(
          s.precision,
          `${app} ${type} precision ${s.precision} < ${t.precision}; extra=${s.extra.join(", ") || "(none)"}`,
        ).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(t.precision);
        expect(
          s.recall,
          `${app} ${type} recall ${s.recall} < ${t.recall}; missing=${s.missing.join(", ") || "(none)"}`,
        ).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(t.recall);
      }
    });
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 12: Implement `EVAL_THRESHOLDS`**

Append to `packages/cli/src/evals/score.ts`:

```ts
/** v0.1 CI gate (spec §7a.2): precision ≥ 95%, recall ≥ 80%, per hop type. Applied in `test/evals.test.ts`
 * against every vendored app under fixtures/evals/. Never lower these to make a run pass. */
export const EVAL_THRESHOLDS: Record<ExpectedHop["type"], { precision: number; recall: number }> = {
  "ui-api": { precision: 0.95, recall: 0.8 },
  "route-handler": { precision: 0.95, recall: 0.8 },
  "handler-store": { precision: 0.95, recall: 0.8 },
};
```

- [ ] **Step 13: Run the test to verify it passes**

Run: `pnpm --filter codeloom test evals`
Expected: PASS for every vendored app — the console output from Step 10's `formatScore` call shows the table
for each app (`pnpm --filter codeloom test evals -- --reporter=verbose` if you need to see it inline).

**If a real app misses a threshold:** do not edit `EVAL_THRESHOLDS`, and do not quietly remove the failing hop
from that app's `expected-journeys.json` to make it pass (that would violate Step 5 rule 4 — only remove a key
entry if, on rereading the code, it turns out not to be a real hop at all). Instead:
1. Re-check the key entry against the code one more time (file/symbol/route string typos are the most common
   cause of a real hop scoring as `missing`).
2. If the key is correct and codeloom's linker genuinely cannot find or mis-links that hop, leave the key as
   is, leave the test failing, and report **DONE_WITH_CONCERNS** at the end of this task's work: state which
   app, which hop type, the actual precision/recall numbers from the test's assertion messages, and the exact
   `missing`/`extra` hop keys. That report is for the author (or whoever reviews Task 15) to decide whether to
   fix the linker (Tasks 6-12) or accept the gap for v0.1 — it is not this task's call to make by lowering the
   bar.

- [ ] **Step 14: Write the root `fixtures/evals/README.md`**

`fixtures/evals/README.md`:

```markdown
# Eval apps

Trimmed, licence-compatible copies of real open-source apps, used only in CI (`test/evals.test.ts`) to score
codeloom's linker against hand-written answer keys — the same per-hop-type precision/recall check as
`fixtures/demo-shop/expected-journeys.json`, but on code codeloom's authors didn't write themselves. Each
`<app>/` folder holds:

- `SOURCE.md` — upstream URL(s), the exact commit SHA vendored, and licence
- `LICENSE` — the upstream project's licence file, unmodified
- `codeloom.workspace.json` — the workspace file `codeloom index` reads
- `expected-journeys.json` — the hand-verified answer key (Task 14's `ExpectedJourneysSchema`)
- one sub-folder per repo, trimmed to source + `package.json`/`tsconfig.json` + schema/migration files only

Why vendor a trimmed copy instead of cloning at test time: deterministic, offline, reviewable diffs when an
app is re-vendored at a newer commit, and no dependency on GitHub being reachable in CI (codeloom itself makes
no network calls — see Global Constraints in the Interfaces Registry appendix of this plan — and neither does its
test suite once these fixtures are committed).

Licences: every vendored app here is MIT or Apache-2.0 (verified by reading its `LICENSE` file directly, not
by trusting a badge) — see each app's `SOURCE.md` for which. `fixtures/evals/<app>/LICENSE` is kept alongside
the trimmed source per that licence's terms. Nothing under `fixtures/evals/` is codeloom's own code; the
trimming (keeping only source/config/schema files) is covered under the same licences as ordinary use of the
software, not a derivative claim.
```
