## Appendix: Interfaces Registry

> Where a task's text records a registry inconsistency and its fix, the task text governs.


The contract every task writer codes against. Names, paths and signatures are exact; a task may add private
helpers but must not rename or reshape anything here. "Consumes" lists the earlier exports a task uses.
All imports between files are ESM with `.js` suffixes. `Confidence`, `Loc`, `HttpMethod` etc. come from `store/schemas.ts`.

### R1 — Task 1: `packages/cli/src/store/schemas.ts` (endpoints v2 + workspace)

Consumes: Plan 1 schemas (unchanged ones kept as is: `HttpMethodSchema`, `LocSchema`, `UiPageSchema`,
`DbOpSchema`, `DbAccessSchema`, `TableDefSchema`, `ModelDefSchema`, `RepoConfigSchema`, `RepoRefSchema`).

```ts
export const ConfidenceSchema = z.enum(["exact", "param", "guess"]);
export type Confidence = z.infer<typeof ConfidenceSchema>;

export const HttpCallKindSchema = z.enum(["literal", "template", "unknown"]);
export type HttpCallKind = z.infer<typeof HttpCallKindSchema>;

export const HttpCallSchema = z.object({
  method: HttpMethodSchema,
  /** Normalised route pattern; "" when kind === "unknown" (nothing literal in the URL). */
  path: z.string(),
  raw: z.string(),
  resolved: z.boolean(),
  client: z.string(),
  at: LocSchema,
  /** literal: whole URL is a string; template: literal text + expressions; unknown: no literal text. */
  kind: HttpCallKindSchema,
  /** Source text of a leading base expression that was stripped, e.g. "process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL". */
  base: z.string().optional(),
});

export const HttpClientSchema = z.object({
  /** Variable the instance is bound to (`export const api = axios.create(...)` → "api"). */
  name: z.string(),
  file: z.string(),
  line: z.number().int().positive(),
  /** "axios" | "ky" | callee text of a generic `create({ baseURL })`. */
  factory: z.string(),
  /** Source text of a non-literal baseURL / prefixUrl expression. */
  baseExpr: z.string().optional(),
  /** baseURL when it is a string literal, normalised by normalizePath (host stripped). */
  baseLiteral: z.string().optional(),
});
export type HttpClient = z.infer<typeof HttpClientSchema>;

export const RouteSchema = z.object({
  method: HttpMethodSchema,
  /** As declared, including Adonis group prefixes (Plan 1 behaviour); mounts are NOT applied here. */
  path: z.string(),
  handler: z.string(),
  inline: z.boolean(),
  framework: z.enum(["adonis", "express", "fastify", "nest", "next-api"]),
  at: LocSchema,
  handlerFile: z.string().optional(),
  handlerLines: z.tuple([z.number().int().positive(), z.number().int().positive()]).optional(),
  /** Receiver identifier the route was registered on (express/fastify): "router", "app", "fastify", … */
  router: z.string().optional(),
});

export const MountKindSchema = z.enum(["express-use", "fastify-register", "nest-global"]);
export type MountKind = z.infer<typeof MountKindSchema>;

export const MountSchema = z.object({
  /** Normalised prefix ("/billing"); nest-global "api" → "/api". */
  prefix: z.string(),
  kind: MountKindSchema,
  /** Receiver the mount was called on ("app", "router", "fastify"); "" for nest-global. */
  on: z.string(),
  /** What gets the prefix. name = local identifier passed (router var / plugin fn); file = repo-relative file it
   * resolves to when imported (set by finalizeEndpoints from the draft's import specifier). Both absent for
   * nest-global (applies to every nest route in the repo). */
  target: z.object({ name: z.string().optional(), file: z.string().optional() }),
  at: LocSchema,
});
export type Mount = z.infer<typeof MountSchema>;

export const QueueOpSchema = z.object({
  /** publish via sendToQueue: queue name; publish via exchange: "exchange:key" (or exchange alone when key is "");
   * consume / bind: queue name. (Plan 1 encoding kept.) */
  queue: z.string(),
  op: z.enum(["publish", "consume", "bind"]),
  at: LocSchema,
  exchange: z.string().optional(),
  /** Present (possibly "") for exchange publishes and binds. */
  routingKey: z.string().optional(),
});
export type QueueOp = z.infer<typeof QueueOpSchema>;

export const EndpointsFileSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(2),
  repo: z.string(),
  pages: z.array(UiPageSchema),
  httpCalls: z.array(HttpCallSchema),
  httpClients: z.array(HttpClientSchema),
  routes: z.array(RouteSchema),
  mounts: z.array(MountSchema),
  dbAccess: z.array(DbAccessSchema),
  tables: z.array(TableDefSchema),
  models: z.array(ModelDefSchema),
  queues: z.array(QueueOpSchema),
});
export type EndpointsFile = z.infer<typeof EndpointsFileSchema>;

export const RepoRoleSchema = z.enum(["ui", "api", "worker", "lib"]);
export type RepoRole = z.infer<typeof RepoRoleSchema>;

export const RepoRefSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  path: z.string(),
  /** Optional hint for picking the target backend of an HTTP call. */
  role: RepoRoleSchema.optional(),
  /** Env var names (e.g. "NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL") or hosts ("api.example.com") whose calls go to this repo. */
  apiBase: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
});
export type RepoRef = z.infer<typeof RepoRefSchema>;

/** Stored now, used by Plan 2b. Validated so a typo is caught early. */
export const TracesConfigSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("kind", [
  z.object({
    kind: z.literal("tempo"),
    url: z.string(),
    headersEnv: z.string().optional(),
    services: z.record(z.string(), z.string()).optional(),
    lookback: z.string().optional(),
  }),
  z.object({ kind: z.literal("otlp-file"), path: z.string() }),
]);
export type TracesConfig = z.infer<typeof TracesConfigSchema>;

export const WorkspaceFileSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(1),
  name: z.string(),
  repos: z.array(RepoRefSchema),
  /** Manual hops. Strings use the chain-stop address form "<repo>:<file>#<symbol>" or
   * "route:<repo>:<METHOD path>" / "queue:<repo>:<queue>" (see R11 parseLinkRef). */
  links: z.array(z.object({ from: z.string(), to: z.string() })),
  traces: TracesConfigSchema.optional(),
});
export type WorkspaceFile = z.infer<typeof WorkspaceFileSchema>;
```

Workspace stays `version: 1` (additions are optional). `FingerprintsSchema` stays `version: 2`; its per-file
record type gains `codeIndex: z.any()` (Task 6). `loadWorkspace` (workspace/repos.ts) return type widens (in Task 5) to
`{ root: string; name: string; repos: RepoRef[]; links: WorkspaceFile["links"]; traces?: TracesConfig }`.

`packages/cli/src/store/paths.ts`: `FILES` gains `chains: "chains.json"`, `journeys: "journeys.json"`.

`packages/cli/src/adapters/types.ts`:

```ts
export type MountDraft = Mount & { targetImport?: string }; // raw specifier; resolved to target.file, then dropped
export interface EndpointsPart {
  pages: UiPage[]; httpCalls: HttpCall[]; httpClients: HttpClient[]; routes: RouteDraft[];
  mounts: MountDraft[]; dbAccess: DbAccess[]; tables: TableDef[]; models: ModelDef[]; queues: QueueOp[];
}
export function emptyPart(): EndpointsPart; // all nine arrays empty
```

`packages/cli/src/indexer/index-repo.ts`: `export const CACHE_FORMAT = 4;`

Sort keys added in `finalizeEndpoints` (all via `compare`): httpClients by `file, line, name`; mounts by
`at.file, at.line, prefix`; queues by existing key then `op`, `exchange ?? ""`, `routingKey ?? ""`.

### R2 — Task 2: HTTP adapter (`adapters/ast.ts`, `adapters/http-calls.ts`)

Consumes: R1 `HttpCall`, `HttpCallKind`, `HttpClient`, `EndpointsPart.httpClients`.

```ts
// adapters/ast.ts — return type widened; existing callers keep working.
export function pathPattern(expr: ts.Expression):
  { path: string; resolved: boolean; kind: "literal" | "template"; base?: string } | undefined;
// base = getText() of the leading expression dropped by the BASE_RE rule (or any leading non-literal
// expression immediately followed by literal text starting with "/").

// adapters/http-calls.ts
export function detectHttpCalls(file: ParsedFile): HttpCall[];   // now sets kind/base; unknown → path ""
export function detectHttpClients(file: ParsedFile): HttpClient[];
// Recognised: `const x = axios.create({ baseURL })`, `ky.create({ prefixUrl })`, `ky.extend({ prefixUrl })`,
// any `<callee>.create({ baseURL })` bound to a variable. factory = "axios" | "ky" | callee text.
```

`adapters/run.ts` `runAdapters` fills `httpClients` from `detectHttpClients` for JS/TS files.

### R3 — Task 3: Routes adapter (`adapters/routes.ts`)

Consumes: R1 `Route.router`, `Mount`, `MountDraft`, `EndpointsPart.mounts`, `ImportResolver` (run.ts).

```ts
export function detectRoutes(file: ParsedFile, ctx: AdapterContext): RouteDraft[]; // + router on express/fastify
export function detectMounts(file: ParsedFile, ctx: AdapterContext): MountDraft[];
// express-use: `<recv>.use("/p", <ident>)` or `.use("/p", mw..., <ident>)` (last arg) where recv is a router name
//   (isRouterName) → target.name = ident; targetImport = specifier when ident is an import (default/named/require).
// fastify-register: `<recv>.register(<ident>, { prefix: "/p" })`.
// nest-global: `<x>.setGlobalPrefix("api")` → prefix "/api", on "", target {}.
// Non-literal prefixes are skipped (no mount recorded).
```

`adapters/run.ts`:

```ts
export function finalizeEndpoints(repo: string, parts: EndpointsPart[], resolveImport?: ImportResolver): EndpointsFile;
// v2: resolves MountDraft.targetImport → target.file (dropped afterwards), sorts per R1, emits version 2.
```

### R4 — Task 4: Queue adapter (`adapters/queues.ts`)

Consumes: R1 `QueueOp`.

```ts
export function detectQueues(file: ParsedFile, ctx: AdapterContext): QueueOp[];
// sendToQueue(q)            → { queue: q, op: "publish" }
// publish(ex, key)          → { queue: ex && key ? `${ex}:${key}` : ex || key, op: "publish", exchange: ex, routingKey: key }
// consume(q)                → { queue: q, op: "consume" }
// bindQueue(q, ex, key)     → { queue: q, op: "bind", exchange: ex, routingKey: key }  (all three literal, key may be "")
```

### R5 — Task 5: Store readers + errors (`store/read.ts`, `errors.ts`, `bin.ts`)

Consumes: R1 schemas and `FILES`; `readJson` (store/json.ts); `loadWorkspace`; core `KnowledgeGraphSchema`,
`KnowledgeGraph`.

```ts
// errors.ts
export class CodeloomError extends Error {
  readonly exitCode: number;                       // default 1
  constructor(message: string, opts?: { exitCode?: number });
}
/** A data or usage problem: exit code 2. */
export function dataError(message: string): CodeloomError;
export function formatError(err: unknown, debug: boolean): string; // unchanged

// bin.ts: catch → console.error(formatError(...)); process.exit(err instanceof CodeloomError ? err.exitCode : 1)
// A successful action that set process.exitCode (check's threshold) leaves it as is.

// store/read.ts
export function readGraph(repoAbs: string): KnowledgeGraph | null;       // KnowledgeGraphSchema; missing → null
export function readEndpoints(repoAbs: string): EndpointsFile | null;    // missing → null
// raw version === 1 → dataError(`${path} was written by an older codeloom (endpoints version 1). Run "codeloom index" to rebuild it.`)  (exit 2)
// store/json.ts readJson: bad JSON / bad shape now throw dataError (exit 2), messages unchanged.
// workspace/repos.ts loadWorkspace: also returns links and traces (see R1).

export interface RepoData {
  ref: RepoRef;              // includes role / apiBase hints
  abs: string;               // absolute repo path
  graph: KnowledgeGraph;
  endpoints: EndpointsFile;
}
export interface WorkspaceData {
  root: string;              // workspace root (where chains.json / journeys.json live, under .codeloom/)
  name: string;
  repos: RepoData[];         // sorted by ref.id (compare)
  links: WorkspaceFile["links"];
  traces?: TracesConfig;
}
/** Every repo's graph + endpoints. Any missing file → dataError('... Run "codeloom index" first.'). */
export function loadWorkspaceData(start: string): WorkspaceData;
```

### R6 — Task 6: Code index (`linker/code-index.ts`, `indexer/index-repo.ts`, `store/schemas.ts` FingerprintsSchema `codeIndex`)

Consumes: `parseSource`, `walk`, `lineOf`, `enclosingSymbol` (adapters/ast.ts); `ParsedFile`.

```ts
export interface CodeSymbol {
  /** Same naming as enclosingSymbol: "fn", "Class.method", "obj.method", "Class" for the class itself. */
  name: string;
  kind: "function" | "class" | "method" | "object-method";
  lines: [number, number];
  exported: boolean;
  /** "default" when it is the default export (alone or alongside its name). */
  exportedAs?: string;
}
export type ImportBinding =
  | { local: string; kind: "named"; imported: string; from: string }   // import { a as local }, const { a: local } = require()
  | { local: string; kind: "default"; from: string }
  | { local: string; kind: "namespace"; from: string };                // import * as ns, const ns = require()
export type ReExport =
  | { kind: "all"; from: string }                                       // export * from
  | { kind: "named"; exported: string; imported: string; from: string }; // export { a as b } from
export interface ClassField {
  className: string;
  field: string;
  /** Class name the field holds: `svc = new X()` or constructor `private svc: X` / `@Inject() svc: X`. */
  typeName: string;
}
export type Callee =
  | { kind: "ident"; name: string }                              // f()
  | { kind: "member"; object: string; prop: string }             // ns.f(), svc.m(), obj.m()  (object = identifier)
  | { kind: "new-member"; className: string; prop: string }      // new X().m()
  | { kind: "this"; prop: string }                               // this.m()
  | { kind: "this-field"; field: string; prop: string }          // this.svc.m()
  | { kind: "other"; prop?: string };                            // anything else; prop = last property name if any
export interface CallSite {
  /** Enclosing symbol name per enclosingSymbol ("<module>" at top level). */
  caller: string;
  line: number;
  callee: Callee;
}
export interface FileCodeIndex {
  symbols: CodeSymbol[];
  imports: ImportBinding[];
  reExports: ReExport[];
  classFields: ClassField[];
  /** `const s = new X()` in function scope: local → class name, keyed by caller symbol. */
  localNews: Array<{ caller: string; local: string; className: string }>;
  calls: CallSite[];
}
export function buildFileCodeIndex(file: ParsedFile): FileCodeIndex;   // non-JS/TS → all arrays empty
/** Symbol whose line range contains `line` (innermost). */
export function symbolAt(idx: FileCodeIndex, line: number): CodeSymbol | undefined;

/** Per repo: repo-relative file → index (only JS/TS files present). */
export type RepoCodeIndex = Map<string, FileCodeIndex>;
```

`indexer/index-repo.ts` changes: `FileRecord` gains `codeIndex: FileCodeIndex` (built with the adapters when a
file is parsed, reused from cache otherwise). `IndexResult` gains:

```ts
export interface IndexResult {
  graph: KnowledgeGraph;
  endpoints: EndpointsFile;
  stats: IndexStats;
  codeIndex: RepoCodeIndex;
  files: Set<string>;
  aliases: Alias[];
}
```

### R7 — Task 7: Call resolver (`linker/resolve-calls.ts`)

Consumes: R6 `RepoCodeIndex`, `FileCodeIndex`, `CallSite`, `Callee`, `symbolAt`; `resolveImport`, `Alias`
(indexer/resolve-imports.ts); R1 `Confidence`.

```ts
export interface SymbolRef { repo: string; file: string; symbol: string }
export interface ResolvedCall {
  site: CallSite;
  target: SymbolRef;
  confidence: Confidence;              // "exact" for import/new/this/DI; "guess" for unique-method fallback
  via: "local" | "import" | "barrel" | "new" | "this" | "di" | "object" | "unique-method";
}
export interface RepoResolveInput {
  repo: string;
  index: RepoCodeIndex;
  files: Set<string>;
  aliases: Alias[];
}
export interface CallResolver {
  /** Resolve one call site found in `file`. null when nothing resolves. Never throws. */
  resolve(file: string, site: CallSite): ResolvedCall | null;
  /** All resolved outgoing calls of a symbol (sites whose caller === symbol). Sorted by line. */
  callsFrom(ref: SymbolRef): ResolvedCall[];
  /** Reverse edges: resolved calls whose target is `ref` (built lazily once). */
  callersOf(ref: SymbolRef): Array<{ from: SymbolRef; call: ResolvedCall }>;
  /** Follow an imported name to its defining file + symbol (barrels ≤ 3 hops). */
  resolveExport(file: string, exportedName: string): SymbolRef | null;
  /** The per-file index the resolver was built over (input.index.get(file)); used by Tasks 10/11 with symbolAt. */
  fileIndex(file: string): FileCodeIndex | undefined;
}
export function createCallResolver(input: RepoResolveInput): CallResolver;

export const MAX_DEPTH = 6;
export interface WalkStep { ref: SymbolRef; depth: number; via?: ResolvedCall; parent?: SymbolRef }
/** BFS from `start` over callsFrom, depth ≤ MAX_DEPTH, cycle guard by `${file}#${symbol}`. Includes start (depth 0).
 * Deterministic order: by depth, then file, then symbol (compare). */
export function walkCalls(resolver: CallResolver, start: SymbolRef, maxDepth?: number): WalkStep[];
/** Same, backwards over callersOf (used by the UI link). */
export function walkCallers(resolver: CallResolver, start: SymbolRef, maxDepth?: number): WalkStep[];
```

Resolution rules (memo (b)): ident → local symbol, else import binding → `resolveExport`; `ns.f` → namespace
import; `obj.m` where `obj` is an imported/local object literal → `"obj.m"`; `new X().m()` / localNews `s.m()` →
`"X.m"` in X's file; `this.m()` → `"<CallerClass>.m"`; `this.svc.m()` → ClassField type → `"X.m"`; default export
class `X` imported as `Y` → `"X.m"`. Fallback: `prop` names exactly one method `"*.prop"` across the repo → guess.
`via` is `"barrel"` (vs `"import"`) only for plain-identifier calls that crossed ≥1 re-export hop; `new`/`this`/`di`/
`object` keep their own tag however many barrel hops were crossed (Task 7).

### R8 — Task 8: Paths (`linker/paths.ts`)

Consumes: R1 `Confidence`, `HttpMethod`.

```ts
export type Segment =
  | { kind: "literal"; value: string }
  | { kind: "param"; name: string }
  | { kind: "splat"; name: string };          // ":x*", "*", "(.*)" — matches zero or more segments
export function parsePath(path: string): Segment[];   // "/" → []; trims trailing slash; lowercases nothing
export function joinPaths(...parts: string[]): string; // normalised join, "" parts skipped

export interface PathMatch {
  confidence: Confidence;
  /** Number of literal-to-literal segment matches (specificity). */
  literals: number;
  reason?: string;  // e.g. "call segment is a variable", "suffix match under unknown mount"
}
/** Memo (e): literal=literal → exact; call literal vs route param/splat → param; call param vs route literal → guess.
 * null when no match. */
export function matchPath(callPath: string, routePath: string): PathMatch | null;
export function methodMatches(call: HttpMethod, route: HttpMethod): boolean; // ANY on either side matches

export interface RouteCandidate<T> { item: T; method: HttpMethod; path: string; mountsKnown: boolean }
/** Best candidate: most literals wins; tie → confidence "guess" on the winner (first by compare of path).
 * Candidates matched only as a suffix (mountsKnown false) are allowed only when unique, as "guess".
 * A call-param-vs-route-literal ("guess") best match is dropped (null) only when 2 or more candidates actually
 * matched; a lone guessing candidate is returned (Task 8). */
export function pickRoute<T>(
  method: HttpMethod, callPath: string, candidates: RouteCandidate<T>[],
): { item: T; match: PathMatch } | null;
```

### R9 — Task 9: Mounts (`linker/mounts.ts`)

Consumes: R1 `Route`, `Mount`, `EndpointsFile`; R8 `joinPaths`.

```ts
export interface EffectiveRoute {
  repo: string;
  route: Route;
  /** All known prefixes applied: nest-global, then outer → inner express/fastify mounts, then route.path. */
  effectivePath: string;
  /** false when the route's router is passed to something codeloom could not see as a mount
   * (e.g. a router exported from a file no recorded mount targets, while the repo has mounts). */
  mountsKnown: boolean;
  /** Mounts applied, outermost first. */
  mounts: Mount[];
}
/** A route belongs to a mount when (route.at.file === mount.at.file && route.router === mount.target.name)
 * or mount.target.file === route.at.file (no name check: the import was resolved to that file; Task 9).
 * Multi-level: the mount's own `on` receiver is itself looked up as a mounted target (cycle-guarded, ≤ 5 levels).
 * nest-global applies to every framework === "nest" route in the repo. Several mounts for one router →
 * one EffectiveRoute per mount path (sorted). */
export function effectiveRoutes(endpoints: EndpointsFile): EffectiveRoute[];
/** routeKey per memo (g): `${repo}:${METHOD} ${effectivePath}`. */
export function routeKey(r: EffectiveRoute): string;
```

### R10 — Task 10: Link UI (`linker/link.ts`, UI half)

Consumes: R5 `WorkspaceData`, `RepoData`; R7 `createCallResolver`, `CallResolver`, `walkCallers`, `SymbolRef`;
R8 `pickRoute`, `joinPaths`; R9 `effectiveRoutes`, `EffectiveRoute`; R6 `symbolAt`.

```ts
export interface LinkInput {
  ws: WorkspaceData;
  /** repo id → resolver (built by runLinker from IndexResult codeIndex/files/aliases). */
  resolvers: Map<string, CallResolver>;
}
export interface PageReach { page: UiPage; path: SymbolRef[] }   // page component … call's enclosing symbol
export interface UiLink {
  repo: string;                      // repo of the call
  call: HttpCall;
  pages: PageReach[];                // [] → backend-only or helper unreachable (still linked to route)
  target:
    | { kind: "route"; route: EffectiveRoute; confidence: Confidence; reason?: string }
    | { kind: "external"; base: string }              // absolute foreign host, not a workspace backend
    | { kind: "unresolved"; reason: string };         // "no matching route", "url has no literal text", …
}
/** Page symbol = default-export / component symbol in the page file (CodeSymbol whose lines include page.line,
 * else the file's default export). Reverse walk with walkCallers from the call's enclosing symbol. */
export function linkUi(input: LinkInput): UiLink[];
/** Backend choice for a call (memo (c)): all repos with routes; unique match wins; several → prefer repos whose
 * apiBase includes the env var / host in call.base or the client's baseExpr, then role "api"; else guess. */
export function chooseBackend(
  call: HttpCall, clients: HttpClient[], backends: RepoData[],
): { repos: RepoData[]; hinted: boolean };
/** Call path after the client baseURL literal is prepended (axios instance `api.post("/x")` → baseLiteral + "/x"). */
export function callPathWithClient(call: HttpCall, clients: HttpClient[]): string;
```

### R11 — Task 11: Link backend (`linker/link.ts`, backend half)

Consumes: R10 `LinkInput`; R7 `walkCalls`, `WalkStep`, `SymbolRef`; R9 `EffectiveRoute`, `effectiveRoutes`;
R6 `symbolAt`; R1 `DbAccess`, `QueueOp`.

```ts
export type StoreHit =
  | { kind: "db"; access: DbAccess; viaPath: WalkStep[] }
  | { kind: "queue"; op: QueueOp; viaPath: WalkStep[] };
export interface QueuePair { publish: QueueOp; consume: QueueOp; repoFrom: string; repoTo: string;
  confidence: Confidence; reason?: string }
export interface BackendLink {
  repo: string;
  route: EffectiveRoute;
  /** null when the handler could not be found (→ check kind "handler-not-found"). */
  handler: SymbolRef | null;
  handlerConfidence: Confidence;
  /** Every DbAccess / publish QueueOp whose at.file+at.symbol is a visited step; sorted by walk order. */
  hits: StoreHit[];
  /** Call sites inside visited symbols that did not resolve (→ check "unmatched-call"); file/line/symbol + reason. */
  unresolved: Array<{ at: Loc; reason: string }>;
}
export interface ConsumerLink { repo: string; consume: QueueOp; handler: SymbolRef; hits: StoreHit[];
  unresolved: Array<{ at: Loc; reason: string }> }
export interface ManualLink { from: string; to: string; resolved: boolean; reason?: string }

/** handler: route.handlerFile + route.handler (strip "default."; Adonis "Controller.method"); inline →
 * symbolAt(handlerFile, handlerLines[0]) or a synthetic "<route.handler>" symbol spanning handlerLines. */
export function findHandler(input: LinkInput, r: EffectiveRoute): { ref: SymbolRef; confidence: Confidence } | null;
export function linkBackend(input: LinkInput): BackendLink[];
/** Memo (f): sendToQueue↔consume same queue → exact; exchange publish → bind(exchange, key) → consume(bound queue)
 * → exact; topic wildcards * / # in the binding key → param; fallback queue === key or === exchange → guess. */
export function pairQueues(ws: WorkspaceData): QueuePair[];
export function linkConsumers(input: LinkInput): ConsumerLink[];
/** ws.links refs: "<repo>:<file>#<symbol>" | "route:<repo>:<METHOD path>" | "queue:<repo>:<queue>". */
export type LinkRef =
  | { kind: "symbol"; repo: string; file: string; symbol: string }
  | { kind: "route"; repo: string; method: HttpMethod; path: string }
  | { kind: "queue"; repo: string; queue: string };
export function parseLinkRef(s: string): LinkRef | null;
export function resolveManualLinks(input: LinkInput): ManualLink[];

export interface LinkResult {
  ui: UiLink[]; backend: BackendLink[]; consumers: ConsumerLink[]; queuePairs: QueuePair[]; manual: ManualLink[];
}
export function linkAll(input: LinkInput): LinkResult;
```
Manual links appear as hops with `confidence: "exact"`, `reason: "manual link"` inserted where their `from`
stop occurs in a chain.

### R12 — Task 12: Chains + journeys (`store/schemas.ts`, `linker/chains.ts`, `linker/run.ts`, `commands/index-cmd.ts`)

Consumes: R11 `LinkResult`, `linkAll`; R10 `LinkInput`; R7 `createCallResolver`; R9 `routeKey`; R6 `IndexResult`
extras; R5 `WorkspaceData`; `writeJsonIfChanged`, `readJson`, `compare`; R1 `FILES`.

```ts
// store/schemas.ts
export const LaneSchema = z.enum(["ui", "api", "code", "db", "queue"]);
export const StopKindSchema = z.enum(["page", "call", "route", "handler", "function", "db", "publish", "consume"]);
export const HopSchema = z.object({ confidence: ConfidenceSchema, reason: z.string().optional() });
export const ChainStopSchema = z.object({
  lane: LaneSchema,
  kind: StopKindSchema,
  repo: z.string(),
  /** Nearest graph.json node: "function:<file>:<name>" else "class:<file>:<Class>" else "file:<file>"; absent if none. */
  nodeId: z.string().optional(),
  file: z.string(),
  line: z.number().int().positive(),
  symbol: z.string(),
  /** db: DbOp; queue: "publish" | "consume"; route/call: HTTP method. */
  op: z.string().optional(),
  /** route stops: effective "METHOD /path"; db: table (or model); queue: queue name. */
  label: z.string().optional(),
  /** How this stop was reached from the previous one (first stop: { confidence: "exact" }). */
  hop: HopSchema,
});
export type ChainStop = z.infer<typeof ChainStopSchema>;
export const UnresolvedSchema = z.object({ at: LocSchema.extend({ repo: z.string() }), reason: z.string() });
export const ChainSchema = z.object({
  /** "ch_" + sha256(`${entry}|${routeKey}`).hex.slice(0, 12); routeKey "" for consumer chains and for a
   * page chain whose UI call matched no route. */
  id: z.string().regex(/^ch_[0-9a-f]{12}$/),
  /** "page:<repo>:<page route>" | "call:<repo>:<file>#<symbol>" (a UI call that matches a route but no page
   * reaches; it replaces that route's route: chain and counts as the route's UI caller) |
   * "route:<chain route key>" | "consumer:<repo>:<queue>#<handler file>#<handler symbol>". */
  entry: z.string(),
  /** routeKey(er), or — when two routes (different files/lines) share one routeKey —
   * `<routeKey>@<route at.file>#<n>`, n = 1-based position among the colliding routes by (at.file, at.line). */
  routeKey: z.string(),
  /** Default name, e.g. "POST /api/customers · NewCustomerPage"; backend-only "POST /billing/invoices";
   * consumer "consume invoice.created". */
  name: z.string(),
  stops: z.array(ChainStopSchema),
  unresolved: z.array(UnresolvedSchema),
  /** Lowest confidence of any hop. */
  confidence: ConfidenceSchema,
  complete: z.boolean(),   // has ui→api (or is backend/consumer entry) and reaches ≥1 db/queue stop
});
export type Chain = z.infer<typeof ChainSchema>;
/** One reach fact per UI-side call (readable URL, not third-party, not backend): is it reached by ≥1 page? */
export const UiReachFactSchema = z.object({
  repo: z.string(), file: z.string(), line: z.number().int().positive(), symbol: z.string(),
  method: HttpMethodSchema, path: z.string(), reached: z.boolean(),
});
/** What became of each workspace `links` entry (workspace order): used / unused (from matched no stop) /
 * broken (+ reason). Final review batch 2 (B). */
export const ManualLinkStatusSchema = z.object({
  from: z.string(), to: z.string(), status: z.enum(["used", "unused", "broken"]), reason: z.string().optional(),
});
/** `uiReach` is optional (written by the linker; absent in older files). `manualLinks` only when the
 * workspace has links. */
export const ChainsFileSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(1), chains: z.array(ChainSchema), uiReach: z.array(UiReachFactSchema).optional(),
  manualLinks: z.array(ManualLinkStatusSchema).optional(),
});
export type ChainsFile = z.infer<typeof ChainsFileSchema>;

export const JourneySchema = z.object({
  /** "j_" + sha256(sorted initial chainIds joined by ",").hex.slice(0, 12); fixed at creation. */
  id: z.string().regex(/^j_[0-9a-f]{12}$/),
  name: z.string(),
  area: z.string(),
  note: z.string(),
  chainIds: z.array(z.string()),
  status: z.enum(["ok", "orphaned"]),
});
export type Journey = z.infer<typeof JourneySchema>;
export const JourneysFileSchema = z.object({ version: z.literal(1), journeys: z.array(JourneySchema) });
export type JourneysFile = z.infer<typeof JourneysFileSchema>;
```

```ts
// linker/chains.ts
export function chainId(entry: string, routeKey: string): string;
export function journeyId(chainIds: string[]): string;
export function defaultChainName(c: Pick<Chain, "entry" | "stops">): string;
/** nearest graph node for a stop (function node, else class node, else file node). */
export function nearestNodeId(graph: KnowledgeGraph, file: string, symbol: string): string | undefined;
/** One chain per (entry, routeKey): each page reaching a call → route (`page:`); each UI call that matches a
 * route but no page reaches → `call:<repo>:<file>#<symbol>` (that route then gets no `route:` chain); each
 * route with no page or call-entry chain → `route:` chain; each consumer → `consumer:` chain.
 * Queue pairs (final review batch 2, A): after a chain's own stops, each publish hit's paired consumers are
 * appended (consume → handler → stores; pairQueues order), one level only; hop = worse(publish hop, pair
 * confidence). The scorer keys stops after a non-first consume stop to that consumer's entry.
 * Manual links (B): a resolved link whose `from` matches a chain stop adds its `to` with hop exact /
 * "manual link": symbol → walked like a handler, inserted at the end of the from stop's segment; route →
 * route + backend half, appended; queue → its consumers, appended. A UI call with no route match whose call
 * symbol is a link `from` to a route gets that route as its target (normal page/call chain). */
export function buildChains(ws: WorkspaceData, links: LinkResult, symbolLine?: SymbolLineLookup): Chain[];
export function buildChainsAndLinks(ws: WorkspaceData, links: LinkResult, symbolLine?: SymbolLineLookup):
  { chains: Chain[]; manualLinks: ManualLinkStatus[] };
/** Sort chains by id; stops keep walk order; unresolved sorted by repo, file, line, reason. */
export function sortChains(chains: Chain[]): Chain[];
/** Write chains.json (writeJsonIfChanged), with `uiReach` only when given. Returns whether it wrote. */
export function writeChains(wsRoot: string, chains: Chain[], uiReach?: UiReachFact[], manualLinks?: ManualLinkStatus[]): boolean;
/** Create journeys.json if missing; set status "orphaned" for journeys whose chainIds are ALL gone from `ids`
 * (a non-empty list; losing only some keeps "ok"; an empty list is never orphaned), else "ok";
 * writes only when a status changed. Never touches other fields or order. Invalid file → CodeloomError, untouched. */
export function syncJourneys(wsRoot: string, ids: Set<string>): { created: boolean; orphaned: number; changed: boolean };

// linker/run.ts
export interface LinkerSummary { chains: number; complete: number; orphanedJourneys: number; wrote: boolean }
export function runLinker(
  ws: { root: string; name: string; repos: RepoRef[]; links: WorkspaceFile["links"]; traces?: TracesConfig },
  indexed: Array<{ ref: RepoRef; abs: string; result: IndexResult }>,
): LinkerSummary;

// commands/index-cmd.ts
export interface IndexRunResult {
  root: string;
  repos: Array<{ id: string; stats: IndexStats; endpoints: EndpointsFile }>;
  linker: LinkerSummary;
}
export async function runIndex(start: string): Promise<IndexRunResult>;   // indexes all repos, then runLinker
export function formatIndexSummary(r: IndexRunResult): string;
// adds line: `${chains} chains (${complete} complete) written to .codeloom/chains.json` (+ orphaned journeys note)
```

Consumes: R12 `ChainsFileSchema`, `Chain`; R5 `loadWorkspaceData`, `dataError`; R9 `effectiveRoutes`; `readJson`.

```ts
// store/schemas.ts
export const CheckItemKindSchema = z.enum([
  "ui-no-route", "unmatched-call", "handler-not-found", "no-store", "unresolved-url", "route-no-caller", "guess-link",
  "ui-unreached", "manual-link-broken", "manual-link-unused",   // manual-link-*: batch 2 (B), never in buckets
]);
export const CheckItemSchema = z.object({
  kind: CheckItemKindSchema, repo: z.string(), file: z.string(), line: z.number().int().positive(),
  symbol: z.string(), detail: z.string(),
});
export const CheckReportSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(1),
  summary: z.object({
    chains: z.number().int(), complete: z.number().int(), missingDb: z.number().int(),
    brokenUiApi: z.number().int(), unresolvedCalls: z.number().int(), uncalledRoutes: z.number().int(),
    byConfidence: z.object({ exact: z.number().int(), param: z.number().int(), guess: z.number().int() }),
  }),

});
export type CheckReport = z.infer<typeof CheckReportSchema>;

// check/report.ts

export function completePercent(r: CheckReport): number;   // 0–100, 100 when chains === 0

// check/format.ts
export function formatCheckText(r: CheckReport): string;   // §7a summary line, then items grouped by kind
export function formatCheckJson(r: CheckReport): string;   // JSON.stringify(r, null, 2)

// commands/check.ts

export function runCheck(start: string, opts: CheckOptions): { report: CheckReport; output: string; exitCode: 0 | 1 };
// failUnder not an integer 0–100 → dataError; chains.json missing → dataError('Run "codeloom index" first.')
```

### R14 — Task 14: Answer key + scorer (`store/schemas.ts`, `evals/score.ts`)

Consumes: R12 `Chain`, `ChainsFileSchema`; R1 `HttpMethodSchema`, `ConfidenceSchema`; `readJson`.

```ts
// store/schemas.ts
export const ExpectedHopSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("ui-api"),
    from: z.object({ repo: z.string(), file: z.string(), symbol: z.string() }),   // the call's enclosing symbol
    to: z.object({ repo: z.string(), method: HttpMethodSchema, path: z.string() }), // effective route path
  }),
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("route-handler"),
    route: z.string(),                                       // "<repo>:<METHOD effectivePath>" (= routeKey)
    handler: z.object({ file: z.string(), symbol: z.string() }),
  }),
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("handler-store"),
    route: z.string(),                                       // chain routeKey, or the consumer entry "consumer:<repo>:<queue>#<file>#<symbol>"
    table: z.string().optional(),
    queue: z.string().optional(),
    op: z.string(),                                          // DbOp | "publish" | "consume"
  }),
]);
export type ExpectedHop = z.infer<typeof ExpectedHopSchema>;
export const ExpectedJourneysSchema = z.object({
  version: z.literal(1),
  source: z.object({ url: z.string(), sha: z.string(), licence: z.string() }),
  hops: z.array(ExpectedHopSchema),
});
export type ExpectedJourneys = z.infer<typeof ExpectedJourneysSchema>;

// evals/score.ts
export interface FoundHop { hop: ExpectedHop; confidence: Confidence }
/** Every hop the chains assert, in answer-key form (deduplicated by hopKey). */
export function hopsFromChains(chains: Chain[]): FoundHop[];
export function hopKey(h: ExpectedHop): string;
export interface HopTypeScore {
  expected: number; found: number;   // found = exact + param only
  truePositives: number; precision: number; recall: number;   // 1 when the denominator is 0
  guesses: number; guessCorrect: number;
  missing: string[]; extra: string[];                         // hopKeys, sorted
}
export type ScoreReport = Record<ExpectedHop["type"], HopTypeScore>;
export function scoreChains(key: ExpectedJourneys, chains: Chain[]): ScoreReport;
/** Index a fixture copy (materializeFixture) then score it. Test helper lives in test/, not here. */
export function formatScore(r: ScoreReport): string;
```

```ts
export const EVAL_THRESHOLDS: Record<ExpectedHop["type"], { precision: number; recall: number }> = {
  "ui-api": { precision: 0.95, recall: 0.8 },   // spec §7a
```

Each `fixtures/evals/<app>/` holds: `LICENSE` (upstream), `SOURCE.md` (url, pinned sha, licence, what was trimmed),
`expected-journeys.json`, one sub-folder per repo (`ui/`, `api/`) with source only (no lockfiles, assets, tests).

## Fixture changes (demo-shop, Task 12; key in Task 14)

Existing hops kept (Adonis group prefix, `new X().m()`, axios instance, `fetch` template with env base,
sendToQueue ↔ consume). Added so every hop type is exercised:

| Path | Change | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `web/lib/billing-api.ts` | add | `export const billingApi = axios.create({ baseURL: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BILLING_URL })` — second HTTP client |
| `web/lib/billing.ts` | add | `export async function createInvoice(customerId: number)` → `billingApi.post("/billing/invoices", { customerId })` — UI call that only matches via billing's `/billing` mount |
| `web/app/invoices/new/page.tsx` | add | `NewInvoicePage` (client component) calling `createInvoice` from a button handler — page → helper → call reverse walk |
| `api/app/controllers/customers_controller.ts` | edit | `@inject()` + `constructor(private customers: CustomerService) {}`; `store` calls `this.customers.create(...)` (this.svc DI hop); `show` keeps `new CustomerService().find(...)` (new-member hop) |
| `billing/src/invoice-service.ts` | add | `export const invoiceService = { async create(data) { … prisma.invoice.create … }, async markPaid(id) { … prisma.invoice.update … } }` — object-literal service |
| `billing/src/invoices.ts` | edit | `createInvoice` calls `invoiceService.create(req.body)` then `publishInvoiceCreated`; new `payInvoice` calls `invoiceService.markPaid(Number(req.params.id))` then `publishInvoicePaid` |
| `billing/src/events.ts` | edit | add `publishInvoicePaid(id)` → `channel.publish("billing", "invoice.paid", …)` (topic exchange publish) |
| `billing/src/server.ts` | edit | add `router.post("/invoices/:id/pay", payInvoice)` (route under the `/billing` mount, param segment) |
| `billing/src/notify-consumer.ts` | add | `startNotifier()`: `channel.bindQueue("emails", "billing", "invoice.*")` + `channel.consume("emails", handleEmail)`; `handleEmail` → `prisma.emailLog.create` — bound topic queue, consumer chain to a store |
| `billing/prisma/schema.prisma` | edit | add `model EmailLog { id Int @id @default(autoincrement()) invoiceId Int @@map("email_logs") }` |
| `fixtures/demo-shop/expected-journeys.json` | add | answer key below |

Expected demo-shop chains after Task 12 (snapshot): page chains for `NewCustomerPage` (POST /api/customers),
`CustomerPage` (GET /api/customers/:id), `NewInvoicePage` (POST /billing/invoices); route-entry chains for
`POST /billing/invoices/:id/pay` and `GET /health` (inline, no store → check `no-store`); consumer chains
`consume invoice.created` and `consume emails`. `codeloom check` on demo-shop: 0 `guess-link`, 0 `unmatched-call`
inside walked symbols, `route-no-caller` for `/billing/invoices/:id/pay` and `/health`.

```json
{
  "version": 1,

  "hops": [
    { "type": "ui-api",
      "from": { "repo": "web", "file": "lib/use-create-customer.ts", "symbol": "useCreateCustomer" },
      "to": { "repo": "api", "method": "POST", "path": "/api/customers" } },
    { "type": "ui-api",
      "from": { "repo": "web", "file": "lib/get-customer.ts", "symbol": "getCustomer" },
      "to": { "repo": "api", "method": "GET", "path": "/api/customers/:id" } },
    { "type": "ui-api",
      "from": { "repo": "web", "file": "lib/billing.ts", "symbol": "createInvoice" },
      "to": { "repo": "billing", "method": "POST", "path": "/billing/invoices" } },

    { "type": "route-handler", "route": "api:POST /api/customers",
      "handler": { "file": "app/controllers/customers_controller.ts", "symbol": "CustomersController.store" } },
    { "type": "route-handler", "route": "api:GET /api/customers/:id",
      "handler": { "file": "app/controllers/customers_controller.ts", "symbol": "CustomersController.show" } },
    { "type": "route-handler", "route": "billing:POST /billing/invoices",
      "handler": { "file": "src/invoices.ts", "symbol": "createInvoice" } },
    { "type": "route-handler", "route": "billing:POST /billing/invoices/:id/pay",
      "handler": { "file": "src/invoices.ts", "symbol": "payInvoice" } },

    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "api:POST /api/customers", "table": "customers", "op": "insert" },
    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "api:POST /api/customers", "table": "audit_logs", "op": "insert" },
    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "api:GET /api/customers/:id", "table": "customers", "op": "read" },
    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "billing:POST /billing/invoices", "table": "invoices", "op": "insert" },
    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "billing:POST /billing/invoices", "queue": "invoice.created", "op": "publish" },
    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "billing:POST /billing/invoices/:id/pay", "table": "invoices", "op": "update" },
    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "billing:POST /billing/invoices/:id/pay", "queue": "billing:invoice.paid", "op": "publish" },
    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "consumer:billing:invoice.created#src/events.ts#startConsumer", "queue": "invoice.created", "op": "consume" },
    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "consumer:billing:emails#src/notify-consumer.ts#handleEmail", "queue": "emails", "op": "consume" },
    { "type": "handler-store", "route": "consumer:billing:emails#src/notify-consumer.ts#handleEmail", "table": "email_logs", "op": "insert" }
  ]
}
```

Notes for the Task 14 writer: `GET /health` (inline, no store) is deliberately absent from the key — it must
not produce any hop, so any found hop for it counts as an `extra` (precision). The key file is plain JSON (no
blank lines matter); `hopKey` ignores key order. Demo-shop must score precision = recall = 1.0 for all three
hop types with zero guesses.

## Done when

- `pnpm -r test` and `pnpm -r build` pass; `codeloom index` on demo-shop writes `chains.json` matching the
  snapshot and an empty `journeys.json`; a second run changes no committed file.
- `codeloom check` prints the §7a summary on demo-shop (exit 0); `--fail-under 101` → usage error exit 2;
  `--fail-under 100` with any incomplete chain → exit 1; an endpoints v1 file → exit 2 with the re-index message.
- `score.test.ts` scores demo-shop at precision = recall = 1.0 with zero guesses (Task 14); `evals.test.ts`
  passes `EVAL_THRESHOLDS` (precision ≥ 0.95, recall ≥ 0.8) on every vendored eval app (Task 15).
